# KOTOR 2: super-deep character write-ups (Oct 19, 2026)

## The Exile (player character) (continued)

- Bastila: «I have done as Revan asked and remained here, but she has been gone for too long. I will wait no longer. Whoever survives this Sith Civil War shall not be one I wish to follow. There is only Revan. Only she can shape this galaxy as it is meant to be shaped. She lives still, this I know. If she will not return, then I shall find her and discover why. I cannot remain here, not knowing. I've become certain there is something she remembered in the Unknown Regions. It is a technique she learned fighting the Mandalorians, that allowed her to convert the last of the Jedi who fought beside her - and murder those who would not. And she fears it is still out there... and silent for far too long.» [K2/Sith_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Darth Sion: «I have studied you, immersed myself in you. I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher. I know the fires that raged upon the Dxun moon while the Republic died around you. You know war. You know battle. And I know of Malachor. You know what it means to be broken. The one who travels with you will destroy you, as she did me. I can end it before it begins.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- Darth Sion: «I have studied you. I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher. I know the fires that raged upon the Dxun moon while the Republic died around you. You know war. You know battle. And I know of Malachor. You saw the heart of war, what Malachor wrought, yet you turned away from it.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- Colonel Tobin: «The beast's... progress is remarkable. But, it will take time, General. The throne room's force shields are far stronger than normal shields.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- Vaklu Officer: «You spearhead our assault. I'll guard your flank. They will be no match for you, Jedi!» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Surface]**
- Darth Sion: «If the exile has come here, then she has not come alone.» [K2/Surface] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «Of course, apprentice. The huntress is with the exile. She has a special talent for... finding that which should remain lost. It also makes her difficult for me to sense... but not to a creature bred for such things.» [K2/Surface]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Atris: «I am Atris, Jedi Master... the last historian of the Jedi... the last of the Jedi.» [K2/Telos]
- Kreia: «You betrayed yourself. Do not blame the exile. And unlike you and I, there is still a chance that one may be saved. The one that you cast out.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Where is the exile? I had thought...» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Where have you been? You have been absent so long. I feared for your safety. Were you with the exile all this time?» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «So... one exile has arrived to save another.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Kreia? Oh... that is not her name. She is gone, and is now beyond your reach, exile. Where she has gone, only I shall follow... after I have dealt with you.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Your execution has been too long delayed, exile.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «I tied my life, my decisions to the Jedi. Perhaps only in separating myself from the Jedi can I become myself again, learn who I am. Perhaps exile is what I deserve... even though it is many years too late, and you have already returned.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Very well. It is more than I deserve... perhaps they shall grant me exile.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «No... please, I beg you... bring me before the council... have me face exile... But please... do not leave me...» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden: «I am Brianna, disciple of the last of the Jedi. And the one who will stand with you against all enemies who face us.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy]**
- Kreia: «:: When the exile enters the Trayus Academy, she will be faced with a choice. ::» [K2/Trayus_Academy]
- Kreia: «:: When the exile enters Trayus Academy, he will be faced with a choice. ::» [K2/Trayus_Academy]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]**
- Darth Sion: «I am ready for you, exile. I have waited years to see the last of the Jedi fall before me. Only one may serve my Master.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Darth Sion: «I am ready for you, exile. I have waited years to see the last of the Jedi fall before me, to join the rest that lie buried within this planet's core. The end of the Jedi is at hand.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «Those are the words of Jedi, Jedi who over thousands of years, have never turned from power, from inflicting their will on the galaxy. They know power. The only weakness is not seizing it when it lies before you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «You are the one who will fall. Die, Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «[Failure] If I am the weapon to be used against you, then so be it. But you will die, Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «[Failure] Betrayal is at the core of what she is. She knows this, and she respects it. No more words, exile. Now you die.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «You intend to make me doubt her... doubt myself. This is not a battle of words, exile. It is one of blood.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «I can die a hundred times, exile, and still I will rise again, as strong as before.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «It is said that the Force has a will, it has a destiny for us all. I wield it, but it uses us all, and that is abhorrent to me. Because I hate the Force. I hate that it seems to have a will, that it would control us to achieve some measure of balance, when countless lives are lost. But in you... I see the potential to see the Force die, to turn away from its will. And that is what pleases me. You are beautiful to me, exile. A dead spot in the Force, an emptiness in which its will might be denied.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «No, there were not. In times past and in times future, there are Jedi who will stop listening to the Force, those that will try to forget it, but maintain unconscious ties. And those, as in the past just as I, who have had the force stripped from them. But no Jedi ever made the choice you did.  To sever ties so completely, so utterly, that it leaves a wound in the Force. It was a mistake to try to make you feel it again, I see that now. There is no truth in the Force. But there is truth in you, exile. And that is why I chose you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «It is your choice. I had hoped you would follow Revan's path, but you and Revan are... different, and your path is your own. You may take one of the ships that orbit Malachor and depart this place. Or you may remain here on Malachor, and wait for the others, those touched by the Force, who will come in time. Or you may return to your exile, where your presence will no longer affect the actions of others. There is no dishonor in any of these choices... I only ask that you make the choice without regret.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Many things do I see as I gaze here from the heart of Malachor. This place... channels such energies. If it matters to you, at this last moment, I shall look into the future, and tell you of what I see. It is my last gift to you, from one exile... to another.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «If she leaves this place, she will leave battle behind her... in no small part due to your influence. She will take Atris' role as historian, and teach others of the Jedi exile who gave up the Force... and became stronger for it.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

**[K2/Underground_Base]**
- Bao-Dur: «This arm of mine isn't just for show, General. Stand back.» [K2/Underground_Base]

**[K2/Western_Square]**
- Twi'lek Bounty Hunter: «Those are brave words from a droid... but perhaps you speak truthfully. Very well, Jedi. You may walk Iziz unharmed. I apologize for interrupting your journey.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Twi'lek Bounty Hunter: «Again, I ask your forgiveness for interrupting your journey, Jedi.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Colonel Tobin: «The crew of the Ebon Hawk is elusive, General. We were warned about that, but we still underestimated them. But the fools are here... in Iziz. The intel on this is certain. I'm ordering men loyal to us to take care of them now.» [K2/Western_Square]

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to The Exile (player character) (every second-person NPC line that directly answers one of the player's dialogue choices in the same section)

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- Medical Computer: «Oddly enough, the camera seems to be locked on the kolto tank you emerged from.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «Close to death, yes, closer than I'd like. You have the smell of the kolto tank about you... how do you feel?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «Yes, I had hoped as much - I slept here too long, and could not awaken. It may be I reached out unconsciously, and your mind must have been a willing one. Or perhaps you have been trained for such things?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I am Kreia, and I am your rescuer - as you are mine. Tell me - do you recall what happened?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «Your ship was attacked. You were the only survivor... a result of your Jedi training, no doubt.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I confess I know little more than you do... I do not know where here is. I do recall rescuing you... the Republic ship you were on was attacked, and you were the only survivor. A result of your Jedi training, no doubt.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «Your stance, your walk tells me you are a Jedi. Your walk is heavy, you carry something that weighs you down.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «So it would seem. Keep your past - and let us focus on the now.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «You may wish to extend your search to some clothes... if only for proper first impressions.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I am not offering to help you - I am not so young as to leap from death's door as quickly as you.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «Indeed, a Jedi trance could protect one from such poisons... in fact, the sedatives may have been intended to keep you unconscious for some time. It would prove lethal to those untrained in such techniques, however. Most curious.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «And so do you. Perhaps we could discuss it at length later on - now we have other concerns - among them, finding our new enemy.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I have found that answers come in their own time, not ours. Turn your energy to the matter at hand - if we cannot find a way out of here, the answers will prove useless anyway.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I leave you to the explorations of this place... here I will remain and attempt to center myself.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I have little choice - and neither do you. It is an advantage to us both, and I suggest we make use of it.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «I am recovering - your concern is noted, but not necessary. Keep your mind on the present - I sense trouble here.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «When I have recovered, I will do what I can to help you - you needed rescuing once, and I am prepared to rescue you again, if the need arises.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Security Desk: «That neighboring room contains a dense cluster of mining robots - storming that room with a weapon could prove dangerous unless you can take them all out at once.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «:: ...can you not sense them... reach out... cast aside your sight, cast aside what you see, and instead, reach out with your perceptions... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «:: Ah... you hear it. It is faint... but it is there. ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «:: It is the Force you feel... it has not been so long as for you to forget... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Kreia: «:: Do not turn away from it. Listen... feel it echoing within you. ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Administration Computer: «[You have found the security officer's override switch. Do you wish to activate it?]» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Damaged Mining Droid: «[Success] This droid has now been switched to defend any organic in the facility. If someone chooses to attack you, this droid will attempt to protect you.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Damaged Mining Droid: «This droid has now been switched to defend any organic in the facility. If someone chooses to attack you, this droid will attempt to protect you.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Security claimed I violated some trumped-up regulation or another - take it up with them if you want, but they stopped listening to me shortly before they stopped feeding me. Now that's criminal.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «You mean you didn't come here on purpose? I'm shocked, I really am. This slice of paradise is the Peragus mining facility, the only supplier of shipping-grade engine fuel to this corner of the galaxy. Peragus fuel plays havoc with engines, but it gets the job done... as long as you don't mind the toxic byproducts and trying to mine it without blowing yourself up.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «You know the planet with the exposed core you saw flying in? That hole was caused by the first mining station that tried to siphon fuel off the planet. Blew a whole chunk out of the planet, and set it drifting out here in a big clump of fuel-cooled asteroids. So the miners drill the asteroids now, not the planet's surface. That's why they don't allow blasters here. Can't trust a miner jumped up on juma juice not to fire one stray shot that will turn the entire colony into a thermal detonator.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «You mean, before or after that Jedi showed up? Either way, it's a real short story. You see, this Jedi shows up, and you know what that means - where there's one Jedi, the Republic will soon be crawling up your ion engine in no time. But the story gets better. See, some of the miners get it into their ferrocrete skulls that since the Jedi's unconscious, they can collect the bounty the Exchange has posted for live Jedi. Well, what passes for the law here didn't like that idea, so the two groups started fighting.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Yeah, Revan, Malak, and the Jedi that went to join them in the Mandalorian Wars. They turned against the other Jedi and had a scrap that almost laid waste to the galaxy. Heh. Where have you been?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Maybe you're right. Maybe I just hoped Revan was a woman.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Look, not like your half-naked interrogation isn't a personal fantasy of mine, but...» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «It was a misunderstanding, trumped-up charges. I could clear it up if I could just speak to somebody in charge, but seems to be no chance of that happening... unless you let me out.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «[Failure] No - should I? Maybe you should ask one of those miners who was thinking about selling you to the Exchange... maybe they wanted to drug you up for transport, ever think about that?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «You better watch yourself - I don't like being accused of something I didn't do. When I kill someone, trust me, you'll know. Got it?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «No deal. If I'm not leaving, you're not leaving.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Well, go ahead and burn your thrusters. If there's been a lockdown, you're trapped pretty nicely on this level. You're going nowhere. Oh... except I have clothes, and this security field is protecting me pretty well from any more outbreaks of poison gas.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «All right, here we are. Now this console is set on automatic hail, you may have heard it when you came in. The asteroid drift charts are constantly being updated, so it sends out a transmissions to incoming vessels so they don't get crushed into space dust. The hail warns them to keep their distance until orbital drift charts are transmitted, and then provides docking instructions to incoming ships... usually freighters. Thing is, you can bounce that same transmission back to the comm here... and suddenly, you've got access to the communications system from the inside.Pure pazaak - the console's ours. Now, all we need to do is re-activate the turbolifts, cancel the emergency lockdown... hey.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «This system's been severed from the main hub - after it was locked down from remote. You can't even reroute the system, it's been cut clean.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «We could try. But if the miners were trying to trap you up here and probably kill you, why not call them and chat? I don't think a friendly call is going to wake them up.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Atton: «Be my guest - not much else we can do. The comm's all yours.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- Administration Computer: «As you access Hangar Bay 25, there is a series of low-pitched whistles and beeps - it sounds as if there is a utility droid on the other end.» [K2/Administration_Level]

- Atton: «Well, the signs are starting to show. You don't look like you've slept in weeks. Or a year.» [K2/Atton]

- Atton: «Good match... now, what are you thinking about right now?» [K2/Atton]

- Atton: «Yeah? So what? I don't ask any dumb questions about your past, despite the fact that it keeps throwing us into life-threatening situations. Want to know why? - Atton: «Huh? Well... hey, thanks. But you've got the wrong guy. I'm good at shooting people, cracking wise, and pretending to know how to fight with my hands.» [K2/Atton] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atton: «I'm as Atton as Atton will ever be, and whoever your trusted informant is, he's right, I did show up on Nar Shaddaa during the Jedi Civil War... along with a lot of other refugees.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «No, because you're asking about it - if I wanted to tell you anything, I would have come and told you. Anything else?» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Is this an interrogation? If so, you're terrible at it, especially for an ex-Jedi... or whatever you are. Why don't you just crawl in my head and try to dig out whatever you're looking for rather than asking about it?» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «You know what? I helped you get off Peragus. If I hadn't been there, you wouldn't have even gotten off the administration level. I'm trying to help you. I don't know why I'm bothering.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Yeah, well... I - I still helped you. Sort of. Maybe you shouldn't look a free ronto in the mouth before you buy it.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «You know what? Not once have I asked you about the Mandalorian Wars. Not once. I heard about Dxun. Everyone has. I heard about Serroco, and I sure as hell know about Malachor V. What makes you think you've got the right to interrogate me on anything? You've got plenty of lives to answer for - all you Jedi do.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «How did you even live with yourself after Malachor? Is that why you went back to the Jedi Council? Hoping they'd kill you?» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Wasn't it? Maybe you thought they'd forgive you - sure, you might have thought they'd execute you.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Go ahead and try. I might be a little more than you can handle... but I expect that kind of arrogance from a Jedi.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Oh yeah? Shows how much you know. Maybe you're wrong about a lot of other things, too.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Because Jedi lie. And they manipulate. And every act of charity or kindness they do, you can drag it out squirming into the light and see it for what it is. The galaxy doesn't need Jedi arrogance or Jedi hypocrisy anymore.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «The Jedi... the Sith... you don't get it, do you? To the galaxy, they're the same thing; just men and women with too much power, squabbling over religion, while the rest of us burn.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Whatever - just leave me alone. I don't know why I'm wasting time with you anyway.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Because you can't believe in the Republic anymore after the Mandalorian Wars. After Revan, nothing was the same.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «If that's what you want to call knowing when to fight and when to kill, then yes, but you can't really break down people into Sith and Jedi and expect everything to make sense.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Yeah, maybe you'll believe this - that when fighting a Jedi, you wound the Padawan first, then let the rest take care of itself. Not only will the master move to protect the student, but the Force bond between the two will mess up the master's head better than any stab wound. Anything else? That's only a sample of my arsenal.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «If there's no whiny little Padawan, start shooting innocents - not to kill, just enough so that they're gonna die unless the Jedi does something to save them. And if any Jedi is stupid enough to use their Makashi lightsaber form against you, start shooting as fast as you can and drop them while they're exposed. Set mines. Set a lot of them. Fire gas grenades, but make sure they have magnetic lock targetters so Jedi can't force push them back.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I taught myself... techniques. It's hard for Jedi to sense what you're really thinking if you throw up walls of strong emotions and feelings. Lust, impatience, cowardice... most Jedi awareness doesn't cruise beyond the surface feelings, to see what's deeper. And I was good at that, throwing up walls, and my superiors knew it. Sometimes the Jedi on our side wouldn't even realize I was there.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Well, it doesn't really matter what you believe.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Because you've killed Jedi, too. Different circumstances, but you have a bigger body count than I ever did.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Take your time - I have.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I did what I did with all Jedi. I hurt her. I hurt her a lot. And then, right when I thought she couldn't take any more - she showed me the Force. In my head. And I felt everything she felt, and I heard just an echo of what the Force was. And how what I was doing... I think I loved her, but it wasn't that kind of love. It was the kind of love where you're willing to give up everything for someone you don't even know.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «In the end, she sacrificed herself to keep my secret, to prevent the Sith from knowing about that touch of the Force inside me. She wasted her life to save me. Me. And I felt her die, when she opened her mind. I've killed Jedi like I said, but I was never there to feel it, to be on the receiving end. And after that, I couldn't stop feeling things - before, guilt, lust, impatience, it had been orchestrated to get close - now, it all just kept tumbling out - and I couldn't keep doing what I was doing. So I left. I fled with the displaced war veterans to Nar Shaddaa and I lost myself there, until the war came to an end. I wanted no more of Jedi, or Dark Jedi, or the Force. I just wanted to be left alone. And then I met you on Peragus. And I thought, maybe, maybe she had saved me so that I could help you. And if I can't, then I have to try.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I didn't want to tell you any of this. But... I had to. Because if something happens, I can't let you think I was doing it for something other than the past.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «You're right... I didn't think of it like that... but she tricked me. Another Jedi trick, more Jedi hypocrisy, like the others. I'm tired of Jedi crawling in my head. I'm tired of not knowing who I am any more.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I will follow you, to hell and back. I will do whatever you ask, and your enemies shall be mine.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Do not discount me - I can do whatever task you ask, kill anyone you want to prove myself. You have only to name it.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «You are wrong, and I will try to show you why. Until then, I only ask that you not abandon me. To be honest, it's kind of a relief. I already know how to kill Jedi... I'd always be second-guessing myself when entering a fight.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Once, a Jedi showed me the Force - I heard it, I felt it. At the time, there was too much pain to confront it - because if I did, it meant I would be changed into something else. Now, I'm not afraid of it any more. And I think that by learning how to use it - I can help protect you. Or at least buy you some time when disaster comes screaming in. I want to learn how to use the Force. I want to learn how to use the Force to help you.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I understand. If there's any chance you will change your mind... let me know. To be honest, it's kind of a relief. I already know how to kill Jedi... I'd always be second-guessing myself when entering a fight.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «What must I do? Is there someone you need me to kill to prove myself?» [K2/Atton]

**[K2/Bao-Dur]**
- Bao-Dur: «You look like you've been standing to close to one of my shield generators.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Seriously, you don't look that way from spending extra time in the 'fresher. I've seen other Jedi - not many, but a few - who looked like that. And I feel a little... strange... around you. A little more in control - calm, like I usually only get when I'm repairing something.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «All right, if you don't want to talk about it, I won't press you.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «No, you're not leaving until we've talked about this.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «She's manipulating you, maybe all of us.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «As long as you don't mind having me around. It's a nice change from drifting.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Then you understand my restlessness. Though the war had ended, I couldn't find peace in anything.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «I'm sure you do.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «It's not that bad. I guess you'd know - you've always got someone following you around.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «I didn't want to talk about the war, but can I ask you something?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «The Force lets you look right through me, huh? But if you don't want to talk about it, that's okay.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Why did you decide to fight?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «You're right about that.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Getting philosophical on me? I'm here because you found me on Telos and I decided to come along for the ride - not that I had much choice after we got going.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Your recollection of the incident must be clouded. But as to why I'm here, it's simple.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «You might not be looking, but maybe it'll happen along the way.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «You just have to know what the circuits look like.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «That's the way I see things. Traveling with you, I know there's something else in the universe, but I can't do anything about it anyways. So I'll leave it to you to take care of.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Under your guidance, I feel like I could overcome my anger.» [K2/Bao-Dur]

**[K2/Battle_Circle]**
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «The battle circle is only a small part of our training regiment. A full Mandalorian warrior is a master of many fighting techniques, and here all of them are tested. If you were to compete, you would have to do so on our terms. For that is the way of the battle circle. Those with great honor decide the terms of engagement.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «Two rules never change - once you enter the circle, you leave the circle only if your opponent is defeated. The second is never kill anyone in the circle. The other rules are decided for each individual fight. The challenger must abide by the terms set by the warrior they challenge. Since you are new, the terms aren't yours to make. Break any rule and the battle is over, and you are dishonored.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «We are not merchants. We are Mandalorians. The only thing gained and lost here is honor. And perhaps some skill, if you have the capacity to learn.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «Be careful when you ridicule a Mandalorian. If Mandalore hadn't ordered us to... Is there anything else you want to talk about?» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «Not up to the challenge? I'm not surprised. Come back later if you change your mind.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Davrel: «Our history is one of battles and wars, but there are times when even we must deal with peace. The battle circle allows us to hone our skills by fighting other Mandalorians. It pales to what we really thirst for - honor in battle. But it keeps us prepared. Talk to the sergeant if you want to fight!» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Davrel: «A few. No fights to the death. Other parts of our training weed out the weak among us. But that isn't the function of the battle circle. There are individual rules for each fight. The warrior with greater honor determines those. Which in your case means any Mandalorian makes the rules.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «You are new here, so you will fight Davrel. He is young and has not been able to prove himself in battle. Davrel - what are your terms?» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «The battle circle is for training. It is best to choose something that will test both of your limits. In this case swords would be a good choice. It is something neither one of you are completely familiar with. But the choice is ultimately yours to make.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Kelborn: «We're taught not to. Mandalorians want to be tested to the limits of our abilities and beyond. We admire anyone who beats us on the battlefield. Instead of hate we study, practice and train until the next time, so we won't be beaten. But some young Mandalorians find this a hard lesson. Any veteran of the Mandalorian Wars respects Jedi like you. Your actions on Dxun validate this. But we aren't a simple people.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Kelborn: «As would I. I noticed that you killed more scouts than I. I am curious to see your handiwork first hand.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «Kelborn says you are worthy enough to face champions. You may fight in the battle circle again.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «I could only do that if I observed you fight an enemy in real combat. I have seen your matches so far, and I do not doubt your worthiness to face me. But the rules cannot be changed. The other champion, Kelborn, is in the jungle on a mission. Perhaps your paths will cross, and you will have your opportunity there.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «No, it is up to the sergeant. The battle circle was not designed to settle petty vendettas and rivalries. Its primary purpose has always been teaching. Learning from books or holovids is useless when battle rages and soldiers are dying around you. Real knowledge can only be gained through combat. Perhaps we can learn from one another, in time.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «You talk with the sergeant. Today is his day to preside over the circle. Each fight is different because the Mandalorian with greater honor dictates the terms of the engagement. But there will be no terms if we fight. Everyone else will deny you your lightsaber and the use of your "Force." But I want to fight you with all your skill. Victory isn't important in the battle circle. What is important is discovering more about yourself and your opponent.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «We eagerly look forward to this fight. Bralor, you are our champion. How would you fight the Jedi?» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalore: «You defeated Bralor in the battle circle? Either you're very lucky, or you're a lot tougher than you look.» [K2/Battle_Circle]

**[K2/Bridge]**
- Visas: «It was never a question of need. And I would follow you wherever your path leads.» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «No... do not harm her. I am the one... who has betrayed you. I am the one... who should suffer. I will return to you... but please, do not harm her... do not what you did to me... I beg you.» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «Then... then that is what must be done... my Master. My life... for yours.» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «Ah... then at last... I can die. Will... you... will you stay... until...?» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «Perhaps... yet... there is some hope in one's feelings... perhaps I was not as lost as I thought... If I could have cared for one such as you... more than myself... then... I would die a thousand times for such hopes.» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «I... I did not want to leave this life yet... it was weakness, but for you, I... I... My life for yours.» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «I saw a graveyard world, surrounded by a fleet of dead ships. I felt it through him... as I feel it through you.» [K2/Bridge]
- Visas: «Yes. It was enough. The death of my homeworld will always have its place within me, but it no longer dictates my future. I wish to learn the ways of the Force from one who has already faced that pain, and to use it to save the galaxy from those who would do it harm. Wherever you walk, I will follow you. My path is at an end.» [K2/Bridge]
- Admiral Onasi: «It's a little beat up, but it's still home. I wasn't able to be here to protect it when the Sith attacked the first time. This time, you gave me a second chance. I owe you.» [K2/Bridge]
- Admiral Onasi: «I served with him, like you did. And we had to part ways, like you did.» [K2/Bridge]
- Admiral Onasi: «Yes, wherever he went, your ship's been there. If... if you return to that place, if you find some trace of Revan...» [K2/Bridge] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Admiral Onasi: «I served with her, like you did. And we had to part, like you did.» [K2/Bridge]

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- Vula: «Excuse me. Loppak Slusk's appointment has arrived. Please open the inner door. There you are. Have a good day!» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Ithorian: «Then perhaps you will free me? I was placed in here by two large Gamorreans, and I do not know the reason why. They have not fed me, and they will not allow me to return to my herd.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «Good - it'll be that much easier to kill you. You're marked, human. Whether you're actually Jedi or not means nothing to me.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «Yes, but I'm not a gambler. You're worth enough dead, and it's easier to ship Goto a corpse in a cargo container than a live prisoner.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «You - what? I gave very specific instructions regarding that hammerhead and any of his cronies. Who let this idiot in? Matu, bring me whoever's working the door. Nahata, tell Chodo if he sends anyone else, we'll come after him. And Benok... Dust this foolish Jedi.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «Well, she'd better hope you succeed. I've grown weary of that pink tart's constant shrilling and plotting.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «You - what? I don't have any ship of yours, human.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «You and everyone else, human.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «I guess it's just like they say. If you want something done right, do it yourself.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «I see. In that case, that'll be the last mistake you ever make.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Not as soon as you might think.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «Come now, surely you can do better than that? The Exchange isn't trifled with lightly.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «I suppose you do make a bit of sense there. Fine, I will allow the Ithorians to do their business, but listen to me when I say this. The Exchange is not bullied. If you push, expect to be pushed back.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «That's too bad. Hopefully Goto won't have a problem if there's a few holes in you.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Loppak Slusk: «Negotiate? That isn't a word in my vocabulary. And anyways. You can't negotiate when you're dead.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «It a shame I have to turn you down after you asked so nicely. But no.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Is that supposed to frighten me? Let me let you in on a little secret. Fried squid there isn't half the fighter I am.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Unfortunately, that's not an option. It's a shame, but I think you've outlived your usefulness.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Atton: «Yeah, I just can't help getting into trouble when you're around.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «When you've come as far as I've come in the Exchange, it isn't by overestimating your own abilities.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Cantina]**
- Mebla Dule: «Oh, I'm sorry - I'm only here to relax and play. So, you game for a game?» [K2/Cantina] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Benok: «I guess you haven't heard the popular rumors then. Jedi or not, you're the talk of the station. Everyone from the TSF to Czerka Corporation is curious about you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Benok: «Perhaps you don't know who I am.» [K2/Cantina]
- Benok: «You haven't been on Citadel Station long, have you? Let me introduce myself. I'm Benok, the man in charge of Loppak Slusk's protection. You do know who Loppak Slusk is, don't you? The two gentlemen with me are Matu and Nahata, Slusk's finest men. Other than me, of course.» [K2/Cantina]
- Benok: «And I doubt you could cause any. Trading jabs with Luxa is hardly the entertainment I was looking for. Let's go.» [K2/Cantina]
- Bartender Droid: «We feature Citadel Station's most talented all-Bith band around the clock, as well as live Twi'lek dancers, you won't find any holograms here. Casual pazaak players should be able to find a friendly match in the game room, or if you're a bit more seasoned, we have daily tournaments with some of the best players in the Outer Rim. Cards too slow for you? Try your hand at Citadel Station's swoop track and see if you can challenge the all-time records. Last but not least, we feature only the finest cuisine and beverages from Ryloth to Coruscant.» [K2/Cantina]
- Republic Soldier: «I don't understand your problem with the Republic. It's not like Onderon hasn't benefited from joining.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «If you're asking me that, beautiful, you're better off not knowing.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Is that all? What a disappointment. But yes, what you were told is true, I can arrange meetings with Loppak Slusk, but you'd have to give me a good reason.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «That's hardly reason enough. Tell you what. I heard about what you did down at the docks.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «If you prove reliable, yes.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «I need to make a transaction with two bounty hunters that have recently arrived on Citadel Station. Very simple, weapons for credits. I want you to go in my place, as my representative. They're being very careful and I'd rather not be bothered. Here. This case holds the weapons they'd like to purchase. I was not told the place of the meeting, only to go to the medical unit in Residential 082 and await a communication.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «In exchange for the weapons' case? 500. You'll receive your cut from that amount.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «'Almost.' I had enough faith in you to know that you could handle any on-the-job dangers that popped up.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «At least the deal is done and paid for. Here - your share. I wouldn't expect you to work for free.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «I trust you - why else would I request your help with Exchange business?» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «And you will. I just need you to perform this one last service for me.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Look, if I wanted you dead, I think I'd send some armed killers. And Chano's harmless. He's just a technician. I wouldn't worry yourself.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Look, I don't care how you get the credits out of him. Just take care of it.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Good. I knew I could count on you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Already? It's a pleasant change to have someone who so diligently and successfully completes the tasks set before them. But then, I was fairly sure you were the capable sort.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «You killed him? And how do expect us to be able to collect credits from a corpse?» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Now I know you're not dull enough that you can't read through the lines. Slusk's no good for anybody's business - yours, Lorso's or mine. [[../Cantina#Goto» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Oh, too bad. I hope you'll come back when you change your mind. So long, beautiful.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «I knew you were my type when you walked in here. I wanted to clear up something with you about the bounty on your head.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Look - are you really Jedi?» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «What, besides his being your typically slimy Quarren? Slusk works for Goto, out of Nar Shaddaa. Now this Goto; he's rigid, ruthlessly efficient, and all he sees are numbers.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «If you're taking some sort of moral high ground, don't waste your time. Think how many men you'll have to kill, because you wouldn't stop Loppak.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «I can get you into the Exchange suites in Residential 082. They're west of the entrance. More than a few of the guards are in my pocket, so there shouldn't be too much resistance inside.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «You do that. Come back when you've made up your mind. So long, beautiful.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Oh, too bad. I hope you come back when you change your mind. So long, beautiful.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «What would you know about it, anyways? You didn't claw your way through the mass of faceless Czerka employees - to finally be rewarded with a meaningful posting, only to have it taken away by some woman who'd stoop to anything to get ahead.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «So you're back.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «What? What do you mean?» [K2/Cantina] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Corrun Falt: «Wait - are you talking about accessing the mainframe? The Czerka mainframe? You're crazy! What are you, some kind of slicer?» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «Means you can't get in remotely. You've got actually be in front of the mainframe to access it.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «This is a joke, right? You're CSD, aren't you? Loyalty test?» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «What are you gonna do? Torture me in the middle of a crowded cantina? Get out of here. I don't have to take this.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «[Failure] I... well... fine. 1000 credits and I'll find you a way in. No negotiating - take it or leave it.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «1,000 credits and I'll find you a way in. No negotiating - take it or leave it.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «So, do you have those 1,000 credits?» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «All right. Other than Jana Lorso, only B-4D4 has free access to the mainframe. He's a protocol droid, her administrative assistant. If you controlled B-4D4, you could walk right in.» [K2/Cantina]
- Corrun Falt: «Right, whatever. You could use B-4D4 to get into the mainframe.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «Nothing quite so plebeian as that. I believe Samhan or Dendis Dobo would be more than willing to do business with you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «That one? Ah, it must be about Ramana, then. Fine, give me your proposal.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «Then you will have to find another way.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «Must we be so rash? That is not the way to do business. If that is the way you wish to approach negotiations, I have no wish to deal with you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «It'll do you no good. Ramana has been outfitted with a transmitter. If you should kill me, I shall simply obliterate her. A waste, to be sure, but I feel you do not understand the nature of our relationship.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «Ah! Now that is an interesting suggestion. The situation, ah, what a set-up. Ramana, lost by Harra in a pazaak game, to be wagered by Doton in another? I do like the way you think. There is merit to your suggestion. Fine, I shall place Ramana as a wager in a pazaak game. Against a wager of 1,000 credits of your own, I think.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «A shame. I will leave you to find the funds to meet my wager then.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «Indeed? I hope you are skilled in the game, it has been quite some time since I have been reasonably challenged.» [K2/Cantina]
- Ramana: «As you wish, Master.» [K2/Cantina]
- Ramana: «Here you are. I hope that I will soon have fulfilled my debt to you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Ramana: «Harra? Even after what horror he has submitted me to? But it is not my choice, it is yours.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «You don't need my approval for that.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «I was unaware of his dealings. If it's true that he's cutting into the Exchange's territory, freelance, then we need to teach him a lesson. Boys, I'd like you to teach Doton Het a lesson about crossing the Exchange.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Some messages are best given with physical reinforcement. But if you insist, I suppose.» [K2/Cantina]
- Doton Het: «Yes, we'll see about that. Did you have something else, or did you just plan to throw threats all day?» [K2/Cantina]

**[K2/Command_Deck]**
- Atton: «Look, I don't mean to cast another shadow on this, but even if you could reach the ship you came in on, it wouldn't matter. You'll need the orbital drift charts to clear the Peragus asteroid field, unless you want to have the shortest flight out of Peragus ever recorded.» [K2/Command_Deck]
- Atton: «Oh, you mean with the huge crew we brought along with us? That's a brilliant idea.» [K2/Command_Deck]
- Kreia: «The two of you - be silent!» [K2/Command_Deck]

**[K2/Crew_Quarters]**
- Atton: «This was your room? When?» [K2/Crew_Quarters]
- Kreia: «We do not have much time. Whatever you intend to do, do it quickly.» [K2/Crew_Quarters]
- Medical Console: «[The kolto tank appears to be behind you, to your left.]» [K2/Crew_Quarters]
- Damaged Medical Droid: «You leave the droid alone for now.» [K2/Crew_Quarters] *(+6 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- Bao-Dur: «They are vitally important pieces of equipment on tectonically or environmentally unstable regions. But do you really need atmospheric sensors on Dantooine?» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «No worries. But what are you doing here? There are dangerous kinrath about.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «Then I bid you good day.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «And? Can't a mercenary have a hobby, something to wile away the idle hours between the carnage, blood, and ichor? Or does that not fit neatly into your prejudiced little definition of 'mercenary?'» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «Yes, I thought that you might be. Again, I recommend keeping that quiet. I won't betray your secret, but other mercenaries may be more opportunistic than I.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «Oh, it's you again. Still haunting the crystal caves? What do you want?» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «[Success] I will tell you my trade secret.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Ahrnell: «Sadly, I only have one gland on my person, which I need to keep the Kinrath from attacking me. I could extract another for you, but the process takes several hours.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «That would create quite a large impression of yourself, and the crystals would eventually grow back.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «The crystal responds to you! This is very rare indeed!» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «The crystal's bond with you is such that the stronger you become in the Force, the more powerful your crystal will grow. This crystal will make an excellent focus for a lightsaber.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «Quite the contrary. Picture yourself as a sieve, and the Force as water pouring into you. This crystal draws from the excess water that escapes the sieve.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «The crystal is in tune with you. It will use whatever water that pours through you, be it dark or light.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «You scoff at this gift, but many Jedi would greatly covet such a treasure.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Kreia: «No. The crystal has bonded to you. Another Jedi would have little use for it.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «We are highly trained veterans. And you're just a settler with a toy blaster. We're taking the Jedi to Nar Shaddaa, and that is the end of that.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «I follow my orders and nothing you can offer will change that. Besides we're going to get rich off the bounty for this Jedi.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «I'm not in a question answering mood. You're not supposed to be here. Leave or face the consequences.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «You've got some smarts. Just keep walking and don't tell anyone what you saw here. I don't want to see you back here again.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «I know that you settlers have a score to settle with Jedi. Trust me, this one's gonna suffer.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «Trust me. Jedi have a very bad fate waiting for them on Nar Shaddaa. Anything he's done to you, he'll more than pay for.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «I can understand how you feel, but he's worth a lot less to us if he's dead. So we're not going to kill him and we're definitely not going to let you kill him.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «If you think you can help keep him secure, take a stab at it.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Mercenary Captain: «For a settler, you're all right. Leave the cave and don't tell anyone what you saw. If you come back, we'll have to kill you. I'd rather not, so just go.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «I don't know why you think I'd tell you anything.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «Do you think I would tell you if I knew? There are enough of us left to stop you. And now that you have finally chosen to reveal yourself, you shall answer for what you have done.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «You are wasting your time. Anything I tell you will die with you.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «The Sith strike at us from the shadows, never facing us in open combat. They seek the extermination of the Jedi Order. Somehow, they are targeting us through the Force, and where we gather, they can sense it. But now, by dividing our forces, we have made you come to us so we can deal with you. I had suspected a Sith presence on Dantooine, but the destruction the Sith caused here made detecting Force users difficult - as you know. The deaths of so many echo, so much so that it is difficult to center oneself... but it also served to mask my presence, to prevent me from being an easy target. And forcing the Sith - forcing you - to come to me.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «We were not in hiding, despite what you believe. It is you, and all those allied with you, that hide and must be found.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «We did nothing to you. Accuse us if you will, but it will not change the truth. Cutting one off from the Force - such punishments are reserved for only a few. Although it was within our power, we did not inflict such a loss upon you. Your loss was a casualty of war, no more.» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Master Vrook: «Perhaps that is a question you should ask yourself. It is not something I can help you with, even if I wanted to.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «Perhaps it is Dantooine, but I do not feel any power in you - I feel nothing but what I felt in the Council judgment chamber on Coruscant, so long ago. Still, you and your... connections were often a subject of debate in the Council. It is possible that returning to known space, journeying with others, has caused the Force to stir within you again.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «You have seen the end of your trial? Perhaps you would like to explain how you came to this knowledge.» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Master Vrook: «That was intended only for the Jedi Council. If what remains of the Council chooses to tell you, then I will abide by their decision... and its consequences.» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Master Vrook: «Force Bonds? Yes... it is the bond that grows between Master and student. Are you saying you have bonded with another?» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Master Vrook: «Such bonds normally grant strength to both - but not to such a degree. You are deluded to think it would be lethal. Still... your ability to form such connections, to influence others, was always a subject of discussion, even when you were a student here. Perhaps it is a punishment of a sort. But a bond that ties two lives together... such bonds do not seem natural to me. And with your loss of connection to the Force, it seems such a bond would be impossible.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «Don't think that I can't sense the taint of the dark side in you. It is difficult to perceive with the Force here on Dantooine, but it does not conceal your nature completely.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «Right now Dantooine is at a critical moment. If Khoonda falls, then the Republic may lose control of this system. Still, I'm surprised you were able to get this far. Although you do have your Jedi training to fall back on.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «Did you think rushing into battle against the Mandalorians did anything but bring more harm to the galaxy? It only served to bring about a second war, more dangerous than the first. Countless Jedi died in both conflicts, and everyone who followed Revan and Malak died or were turned to the dark side. Except conveniently you.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «You were always difficult to read, as a Padawan and again at your trial - and perhaps more so now that we are here on Dantooine.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Czerka_Offices]**
- B-4D4: «Of course. She has been expecting you. You will find her through the door and down the hall on the right.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- B-4D4: «I was attempting to connect you with Executive Officer Jana Lorso. If you would like to speak with her, you will find her through the door and down the hall on the right.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- B-4D4: «I am sorry, but I do not know. I am only her administrative assistant. If you wish to speak with her, Executive Lorso is available at this moment.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I knew you'd come eventually. I am confident that we will be able to reach a working agreement satisfactory to both you and I.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Our primary concern on Citadel Station is the restoration of Telos' surface. The Republic is eager to revitalize Telos as a benchmark for other such efforts in other systems. While a generous amount of credits has been budgeted to Telos, the bumbling of Chodo Habat and his Ithorians has squandered away much of these resources. For a project of this size, judicious planning and thrifty spending of resources is a necessity, something Habat fails to understand. Nonetheless, Czerka remains optimistic. There are many jobs we need assistance with. For someone with your experience, you would be well compensated.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I can understand why you might be on their side, but believe me, you wouldn't be doing Telos any favors assisting those amateurs. Oh sure, Habat's intentions are good enough, but good intentions won't restore Telos. His expensive policy of relocating biological specimens from Onderon, without any planning will doom Telos in the end. I don't think Habat even realizes that at their current pace, they will run out of funding before even half of the restoration zones are up and running. Czerka plans to use some of the planet's own resources to help fund the restoration. These extra funds will greatly improve the project in the long run. Habat, on the other hand, can't see beyond his own selfish concerns.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Czerka has been managing the restoration project without the assistance of a droid for some time now. Habat has commandeered the new droid intelligence that was to be delivered to the Station. I'd like for you to meet the shipment at Dock Module 126, Shuttle Bay 2, and bring the droid back here, before the Ithorians foul things up again.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «'Requisition,' if it helps. As the Republic's fund for the restoration of Telos is now divided between both parties, it really isn't 'stealing,' as you put it. It will save the Republic time and money if we cut through the process of having a droid transferred to us. It will be better for Telos in the long run.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «If you're worried about the TSF, don't be. If they poke their noses into your business, I will personally take care of it. But I wouldn't worry too much. The TSF understands the importance of letting us do our work.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Don't worry, Czerka wouldn't expect you to work unpaid, and won't subject you to veiled threats and coercion like Habat. For your help in this matter, you will be paid the sum of 250 credits.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Very well, then. I hope to see you soon.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Excellent. I will secure credits for your payment immediately. Oh, and here. You might need this to get into the hangar.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Do you doubt that I will uphold my part of our agreement? Don't worry, when my word is given, it will not be retracted. Now, on to business.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I don't think you appreciate the kind of people we're dealing with. That isn't to say I haven't tried to bargain with them. But perhaps they'd listen to you, given your background. Whatever it takes to get the Exchange out of the picture, it's the end result that concerns me.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I realize it won't be an easy task, as Slusk keeps himself well protected. You would be well compensated for it.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I have a feeling they won't just let you march into their offices. Luxa is Slusk's second. She spends little time in the Exchange offices, though, preferring the cantina in the Urban module. She might be able to set up an appointment.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Good. I'll be sure to send some of my men in to make sure they don't have the opportunity to reorganize. You've done well.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Yes, of course. I will provide a shuttle for you to land on the planet's surface as per our agreement. The shuttle will be arriving shortly in Docking Bay 3, Dock Module 126. I have informed bay control that you are on your way. Again, Czerka thanks you for your help, and hopes that we may continue our working relationship in the future.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- B-4D4: «Entertainment? I will never understand you humans. But nonetheless, I thank you for your help.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Indeed - credits cannot buy loyalty, but greed can. And if there is one thing you can count on with people - mercenaries especially - it is their greed.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «As you might expect, it was credits they were after. Despite the fact Czerka has paid them more than adequately, they figured that Czerka could easily be coerced into increasing their 'fees.' They thought wrong.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Of course. I understand the importance of keeping a valuable asset like yourself happy. I believe these credits should be sufficient to repay you for your work. You are also welcome to anything found on the mercenaries' bodies as your own. They carried no Czerka-issue equipment, anyway. Now, if you will excuse me, this unfortunate turn of events has generated quite a bit more work for me, and I should be getting to it.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «And? If memory serves me correctly, it wasn't too long ago that you were wanted by Grenn and his cronies.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Right, you only destroyed an entire mining facility.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Be reasonable. I have given them a contract and my word that they will be safe from TSF persecution. Gentlemen, would you excuse us?» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «You will not undo work the work that I have done here. I do not break my word, and will not start here.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I suggest that you do, lest I forget about our agreement. Now, if you'll excuse me, I must see to some things so that Czerka may continue to be in the good graces of the Council.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Batono? Of course, he was one of our former employees. Let me tell you something. There are far too many like him. Do-gooders who believe that because of the things Czerka must do to remain successful and profitable, we must be evil. They do not see the impracticality of their beliefs, they do not understand the realities of the galaxy. If certain funds must be paid, then Czerka will gladly pay, if competitors removed, then Czerka will do its part to forward progress. Batono could not see this. Before leaving our employ, he stole many of our records, then began to stalk our movements on the station. Harassment, but conveniently overlooked - sanctioned, perhaps - by the Telosian authorities.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I am curious, though, as to why you are looking for him.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Thank you. As always, you continue to prove to be one of our most valuable assets. If you should locate him, you will be rewarded.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I see. Well, I suspect he's putting his nose into something or another. People like him can't mind their own business, always needing to pry into matters that don't concern them. I do know he spent some time in the company of the Ithorians. I assumed that was the reason for his efforts - that he thought by inventing stories of Czerka's evil, he could put Chodo Habat and his incapable assistants in charge. But if you are searching for him and find him, there would be a reward for any information of his whereabouts.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «If your conscience demands it, then by all means. Just remember, a reward is waiting for you.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- B-4D4: «I am sorry, but I cannot accompany you. I may only leave the Czerka offices for maintenance by an authorized Czerka or Czerka-contracted droid technician.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- B-4D4: «I am sorry, but I do not handle the maintenance contracts. However, this branch currently retains the technical services of Opo Chano, a resident of the station. Perhaps he could help you.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- B-4D4: «Those are legitimate credentials. I will accompany you.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Hello, B-4D4. Is there something you need?» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «More credits, you say? That's news I wouldn't mind reporting to the Sector Executive Officer. Please, B-4D4, access away.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «Hello, B-4D4. On your way, now.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Security: «T1-N1? What are you doing out here? You're not supposed to leave the mainframe. I think someone needs a memory wipe.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «B-4D4! I want you to pull up the maintenance records on that T1 unit. Find out the last time it was given a memory wipe and how it could have been outfitted with illegal weaponry.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]

**[K2/Czerka_Site]**
- Mercenary Commander: «Why would I do that, when I can just as easily take them from your corpse.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- Mercenary Commander: «Falt hired me to keep everyone out of the Restoration Zone... you in particular.» [K2/Czerka_Site]
- Mercenary Commander: «We'll see. You may have caught a few of my boys off-guard... but let's see how you fight now, "Jedi."» [K2/Czerka_Site]

**[K2/Dantooine]**
- Saedhe: «I just really need them. It's important. Please come to me if you find any of the atmospheric sensors. I'll pay you a thousand credits for each one you find, whether they're broken or not.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «It's technical. I doubt you'd understand it. But I'd really appreciate your help.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «That's great news. My offer still stands - I'll pay you 3000 credits for all of them.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «We were picking up off-world transmissions between the mercenaries and... someone, we don't know who. We know that the mercs are up to something, we just don't know what. We set those atmospheric sensors up to gather information. Now you know why we need those sensors. I'll pay you 5000 credits for all three of them. That's about all we can afford. You make money and help us out at the same time. What do you say?» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «Heh, heh.  Yeah, that's admirable of you! Thank you! I'll get this back to Zherron when I get back to Khoonda.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «Done - 5000 credits. That's all I can offer. We're not rich and you can tell why we need those sensors.» [K2/Dantooine]
- Saedhe: «Thanks for your help on this. Here are the credits as promised. I'll get this to Zherron when I get back to Khoonda.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- G0-T0: «If I may interject... the woman is placing an artificially high value on these goods, which would suggest that their value is significantly higher than we first believed. These goods will be withheld from you unless your offer matches your perceived value for the sensors. Otherwise, I am afraid our business is concluded.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «You wouldn't...? He's strangling the life out of us with his mercenaries. Who knows what he's planning. Please, whatever you do, don't let him know.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «I'm going to alert the militia and let them know you intend to sell us out to Azkul. You won't get away with this!» [K2/Dantooine]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Azkul: «Straight to the point. I like that. I will be equally direct. I am planning to take Khoonda, and you're going to help me.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You don't need to know the details. But I can tell you there are a lot of credits involved.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «According to my reports, I have four times as many soldiers as the militia, and I am committed to taking Khoonda. It is inevitable that I will succeed. If you wish to avoid my men eradicating the people of Dantooine, you will make it easier for me to take Khoonda. Of course, I will pay well for your services.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «There are many ways someone with your abilities can aid us. Before the action begins, I have two main tasks in mind for you: disable the gun turrets and the traps.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «Deal with the turrets however you see fit. Just keep them from pestering my men.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «Return to me when you when you are ready for us to begin our assault. I warn you not to keep me waiting.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Handmaiden: «Then I am glad, but I still disapprove of your actions. Using the tools of deception and manipulation will lead to you the dark side.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «Fine. What are you expecting, a reward? You'll get your payment when you see the job through to the end. The only thing I'm interested in hearing from you is that you are ready for us to begin our assault.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You don't need to know. Suffice it to say, people much more powerful than you or I are paying a great deal of credits to have the job done.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You will be paid generously after you've done your part and we've taken Khoonda.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You'd better reconsider. I can't have a Jedi interfering with my plans. There is a considerable bounty on your kind that I will collect on unless you're working for me.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Kreia: «You could have guided this situation to a better outcome.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «Our militia is effective at peace-keeping but isn't prepared for a full-scale battle. If you can do anything to ready them for the reality of it, that would be helpful. Besides that, look around Khoonda and see what you can do to strengthen our defenses. I know that we don't have the perimeter turrets online, and that alone could make a significant difference. Zherron says there is a considerable chance they will breach Khoonda itself. Anything you can do to slow them down could turn the tide of battle.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «I have instructed the militia and other Khoonda personnel that you are to be provided with anything that can be spared. Unfortunately, that isn't much.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «Here is a master card key that will open all of the security doors inside Khoonda. Anything that might aid you in your task, please use.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «Inspecting the perimeter, talking to the troops, and coming up with a battle plan. I've fought in a war or two, but I'm no general. In my experience people like you are better at strategy. You want a hand in making the plan, you got it.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «That's good to hear. Fill me in on the details when you're ready to finalize our battle plans.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «I'm sure Terena will be more than happy to pay you once the mercenary threat is resolved.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «When you're ready, come back and we can talk about the final plans to defend Khoonda.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «You sure? Once we're done with that, there may not be any time left before the mercenaries attack.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Defense Turret: «[Success] You've rigged the turret to overload the next time it fires.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Defense Droid: «[Failure] This droid's commands are tied to a centralized computer station. You'll have to use the computer to issue commands to this droid.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «Ah, you found my tool. Yes, I've been looking for that! Where did you find it?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «I have received terrible news. My brother's second wife is with child. She has become ill and requires medical aid. Her condition is life-threatening. The doctors of Telos will not treat her without full payment up front. But my brother doesn't have any money. Before the moon has turned, I must send my brother enough credits to pay the doctors. Believe me, I am normally a very honest person. But this situation has made me desperate. Please. You believe me, don't you?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «Oh thank you so much! In gratitude for your discretion, I will give you a discount on my goods.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «I can't afford much, but please accept 500 credits in exchange for your discretion.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «No. No! I cannot be imprisoned here. I must stop you!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Jorran: «I hadn't even thought of that. I know the militia will take just about anyone. I need the credits and would like working with people who'll actually watch your back. I want to think it through, but I think I just might go to Khoonda and sign up. Thanks for the idea!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Suulru: «Since you're asking me, I suppose I could. I figure I still owe you for helping me with my thief problem. Zherron has asked me several times before to join the militia. I expect he's going to be very pleased.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Dillan: «Well, since you're asking, absolutely not! I'll lie low until the dust settles.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «Thanks, however I must decline your gracious offer. We Sullustan are trusting, but not fools.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Akkere: «You are an excellent customer. I will talk with Zherron right away and offer my support for his cause. The settlers here have been most hospitable. I will help if I can.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «The Administrator's locked up in her office tight. Which means that the mercs have to go through us and Khoonda's defenses. Thanks to you, they're gonna have a tougher job. That's about it. Now we just need to dig in and wait for the mercs to come.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «[Success] All right, I'll assemble the men. I hope you can speak good.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «I don't have the words. That was really something. I think any one of them would take a blaster bolt for you now.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «You're much better with words than me. I think it'll really help.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «I was expecting something more, but you did better than I ever have. You reminded me of my instructor in the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «We could kill you. But I think you'd slaughter at least four of my men. Recruitment fees are 250 credits per man. So 1000 credits for us to avoid the hassle of replacing those men. Do we have a deal?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You look capable for a Jedi. Fine. A full squad of eight, then. 2000 credits.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You have no idea who you're talking to. I was going to spare your worthless life. I see I was in error.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «That isn't a possibility. This is going nowhere. You could've lived.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «I'm sorry to offend you. You are, of course, our savior and I am very grateful to you.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «Thank you again, brave Jedi.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Dopak: «It was pretty brave of you to stand up to Azkul like that. Your resolve made me finally take action against him.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Daraala: «I heard you were responsible for driving the mercenaries off this rock. That's good. Dantooine was getting to crowded for my tastes. I've still got some Jedi junk left to sell. You buying?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «Your moral dilemma is none of my concern. Thanks again for your assistance.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «I don't see why I should give you more credits.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You deserve more credits. You'll get fifty percent more.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Did you think a handful of mercenaries could hold a Jedi Master?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «You think you can learn my technique just by watching me? It takes a Jedi Master years to perfect it.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «It's not possible. You... you must be stopped.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «You have more questions for me?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «It is good that you bring word of him - but you must not speak of it to others. It will only put him and any other Jedi who remain in danger.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «We were not in hiding, despite what you believe. This threat is striking through the Force. There is a reason that we cannot detect it - it is somehow masking its presence from us.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Perhaps that is a question you should ask yourself. It is not something I could help you with, even if I wanted to.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Perhaps it is Dantooine, but I do not feel such from you - I feel nothing but what I felt in the Council judgment chamber on Coruscant, so long ago. Still, you and your... connections were often a subject of debate in the Council. It is possible that returning to known space, journeying with others, has caused the Force to stir within you again.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Such bonds normally grant strength to both - but not to such a degree. Are you certain? Perhaps it is merely what you believe. Still... your ability to form such connections, to influence others, was always a subject of discussion, even when you were a student here. Perhaps it is a punishment of a sort. But a bond that ties two lives together... such bonds do not seem natural to me. [[../Defense of Khoonda#Connection» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Why would they attack you? You are no Jedi - you cannot feel the Force.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Perhaps it is Dantooine, but I do not feel such from you - I feel nothing but what I felt in the Council judgment chamber on Coruscant, so long ago. Still, you and your... connections were often a subject of debate in the Council. It is possible that returning to known space, journeying with others, has caused the Force to stir within you again. Though it seems unlikely the Sith would bother with you.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «For some reason, I believe you. Still - I am curious as to how they would know to find you. Your trial was not public knowledge. Regardless, these Sith have struck, and you survived - this is more knowledge than I had before. And that means the Jedi may gather. If you can find others - or find some trace of this Sith threat, then we shall gather here on Dantooine. And from there, we may decide what can be done.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «I suppose there is something I should show you. It will keep you alive long enough to prove useful.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Depths]**
- Hanharr: «Your mercy is death to me. End this.» [K2/Depths]
- Hanharr: «You are weak, a child of the Hutt's moon, frightened, forever fearing the dark. You shall always be prey, Mira. If you do not kill me now, I shall hunt you through the paths of your mind until you die.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «Very well - but heed my words. Unless you turn back, the dark side will claim you completely, utterly.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Fear you? No - but I am concerned for you, yes.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «There is something I have observed, and now I feel I must say it. I have found your presence to be... inspiring. With your growth in the Force, you seem to have found your center, and throughout the dangers we face, you remain calm and focused.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I understand now why others followed you to war. Perhaps that is what leadership is - and it is something I have seen in only a few during my travels. In any event, it has been some time since I traveled with a Jedi, nor one so firmly upon the path. I wanted to thank you. I fear the stories that were spoken of you have misrepresented you. And if I have the opportunity, I shall reverse them whenever they arise.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Very well - my apologies if I have offended you. I shall remain silent.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I see. And because you went to war they cast you out?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Do you have a record of this trial?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Perhaps I shall examine it when I get the chance - with your permission.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I shall respect your wishes. My apologies if I caused any offense.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I appreciate your trust - thank you.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I don't think so - perhaps some of them felt strongly about your sentence, but I think something else concerned them. Again, the choice to turn away was yours, not theirs.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «You are right... but there is something more at work here.» [K2/Disciple]
