- Disciple: «Like you, I was looking for some trace of the Jedi. I had heard mention that one of the Jedi Masters had gone there, but I found no trace of them.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Disciple: «A force bond? What do you mean?» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Disciple: «I do not know - I do not know who has taken them. If we were to find them, perhaps I could help you find the answers you need.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I know some of what you speak. It is said that when a Jedi and Padawan establish a close connection, that they can feel each other across distances and coordinate their movements in battle. The intensity of the connection varies... that bonding is said to also be something that manifests itself in such techniques as Bastilla's battle meditation, the ability to touch the minds of others, to demoralize or inspire them. It is also said that moments of death, or near-death, may also cause such bonds - the stronger one is in the Force, the stronger the connection is.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I am an historian and scientist working for the Republic, although I am certain my contemporaries would judge me more a historian than scientist. I have some training in the medical arts, if you are ever need in healing as well. But my chief passion is history. I came to Dantooine in the hopes of finding some trace of the Jedi, but you know how my explorations turned out - instead, it seems a Jedi came to me, and just in time.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Yes, I have been trained by some of the best researchers in the Republic - which is a humbling experience, let me tell you. But if you ever need some medical items broken down or constructed, let me know.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Very well. Let me see if I can treat your wounds. There - that should do it. Do you need anything else?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Are you sure you need healing? You look good to me.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Yes, your muscle tone looks good, you response time seems excellent, and I see nothing that would otherwise impair your health. You have the same vitality about you that you seem to possess, no matter what the circumstances.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «It is the truth. You are more fortunate than you know.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «It is illusionary, the strength you feel. But I will not debate it with you - your choices are your own.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I do not know if you are aware of how fragile the Republic is at the moment. Its influence is stretched thin, and it grows weaker with time. Still, there is hope - and I must remind myself of it, even when times grow dark. As long as we hold Onderon and Telos, then perhaps we have a chance.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Dantooine was one of the few Republic worlds on the Outer Rim - it is why the Republic is attempting to get the settlement up and running. But now that you have protected the settlement on Dantooine, it will serve to stabilize the Republic presence on the Rim.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «It is strange that someone who went to war against the Mandalorians would disregard the damage the fall of the Republic could cause. Of course, if you sought battle for its own sake, then perhaps I could understand your disregard.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I see. Have you ever been in a situation where you are dependent on another? You could not have won the Mandalorian Wars alone. In fact, I doubt you would have survived if you had not others to stand with you. Where are they now, now that you stand here, and they are gone? Did they deserve to die? Did you deserve to live, dependent on them as you were?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «That is a truth I cannot deny - it is sometimes difficult to see that side as I walked upon Dantooine. But I will consider what you have said. There is another side to their efforts and their teaching that I sometimes forget.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «[Influence: Success] You are correct. I am afraid I have not been entirely open with you concerning my past.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «It is possible to forget the Force, you know - if you not have felt it strongly enough, then there is little to miss. But I never felt the Force as strongly as I did when I was with you. And so I decided to serve the Republic, study the Jedi teachings, gather them, perhaps. It was important to me to understand the Jedi now that they were gone. I felt some part of you should be preserved, so that your lessons would not be lost.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Never. I have seen you, traveled with you. My life was mine, not chained to yours.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Your apologies are not needed, or warranted. I chose not to follow the Jedi, and the choice was my own.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «You have? Where have they gone - and why?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Like you? Curious. It is unlike the Jedi to vanish when the Republic is in need of them - and the Republic is more in need of them now than ever.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «And what was that - do you know?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Yes. I do not know if you heard of the civil war on Korriban, but it was caused by Revan's absence. From what I have been able to determine, a year after the battle with the Republic fleet that almost wiped the Republic out, Revan simply... left. When Revan did not return, the factions among the Sith turned on each other. As all those that follow the Sith do, in time.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «[Influence: Success] I fear you are right. Their hesitation almost destroyed the Republic - and there is some evidence to suggest that it gave birth to the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Hate? No, I do not hate them. They only raise questions without answers. Jedi are not supposed to be like the rest of us. They are supposed to see a higher purpose in all things. And they are supposed to train students responsibly and well, so mistakes of the past are not repeated. Yet all I saw was ignorance and arrogance, and what those seeds created in the Republic. It is difficult to follow the Jedi Code, when so few others have. But you know this.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Disciple: «[Influence: Failure] The problems with the Jedi are my own - I do not wish to burden you with my troubles.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «[Influence: Success] I think you are right. It is time.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I have thought about what you have said about the Jedi Order. You were correct - it was a truth I did not want to face.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «While I do not believe the Jedi to be evil,  I feel perhaps that they are misguided - it is hard to protect the galaxy when you are removed from it and its people. The Mandalorian Wars - I understand why the Jedi hesitated, but at the same time, I wonder if they had joined Revan, united, if perhaps the Jedi Civil War would never have happened. It is very easy to  blame Revan and Malak - yet the Jedi Council was equally to blame. I believe that there is very little truth that can be found within the conference room on Coruscant.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «The galaxy needs strength now. If the Jedi will not come forth and fight this threat, then we must. And if that strength comes from the dark side, then that is what we must accept. I ask that you train me in the ways of the Force, to teach me the ways of power so that a greater stability may be achieved. I do not believe in the Jedi and the Sith. I believe in strength, and that is what the galaxy needs now.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Dock_Module]**
- Exchange Lieutenant: «So I heard. You should consider Czerka's offer. If you return the droid to Executive Lorso, she'll pay you far better than the Ithorians.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Exchange Lieutenant: «The planet, then? You don't really think those Ithorians could do better than Czerka, do you?» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Exchange Lieutenant: «Czerka has an offer for you.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Exchange Lieutenant: «Is that so? That's unfortunate - I was supposed to instruct someone of your description that Czerka would be willing to pay handsomely to have a certain droid delivered to them.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Exchange Lieutenant: «I suppose if you could perform this service, Czerka would be happy to pay you for it. A droid is being delivered to the Ithorian docking bay. Czerka would like it. If you bring it to Czerka, you will be rewarded.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Exchange Lieutenant: «Jana Lorso is prepared to pay you the sum of 250 credits for returning the Ithorian's droid to Czerka. Did Habat offer you anything other than some empty promises?» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Exchange Lieutenant: «You do that. You can speak with Lorso at the Czerka offices in Residential Module 081 if you'd like to hear more.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- B-4D4: «But surely you will not. After all, I am in this predicament because of your actions.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- B-4D4: «Yes, if you insist on putting it that way. I would prefer to leave Citadel legally, though that option is currently unavailable to me.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- B-4D4: «Oh, no, I am afraid I cannot accompany you. I could not abandon my companion here.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Information Terminal: «YOU ARE CURRENTLY LOCATED IN:<br>DOCK MODULE 126<br>PLEASE SELECT A NEW DESTINATION:» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Protocol Droid: «Citadel Station is divided into many modules, each serving a specific purpose. You are currently in Dock Module 126, which connects by shuttle to Entertainment Module 081. From there, another shuttle will connect you to the nearest residential module, 082.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «There are planet restoration contract disputes with Czerka that must be resolved before we can resume our work. I know little more than that. Chodo Habat, our leader, could tell you more.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «Yes, he sent word ahead of you. Please, enter. They're waiting for you inside.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «That is strange, Chodo did not send word about you. You will have to excuse me, but during these times, I must be certain.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «[Failure] That is strange, Chodo did not send word about you. You will have to excuse me, but during these times, I must be certain.» [K2/Dock_Module] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Ithorian: «Ah, yes. I'm sorry for the inconvenience. Please, enter. They're waiting for you inside.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- TSF Officer: «I'm glad you're here. I'm the only man the TSF could spare and frankly, I've got a bad feeling about this.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «What?! You cannot do this, I beg of you. Czerka cannot be trusted.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «You must not believe them. Jana Lorso spins lies as easily as she breathes. Please, the planet will die in her hands.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «You are wrong, but we have little money to offer you. If you must do this, know that a planet weeps for it.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian Droid: «I will trust your judgement, master. Lead the way.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «Thank you, and please accept my apologies for the inconvenience.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «[Success] That makes sense. It could be very bad if this experimental technology were to fail while out in the barrens. I apologize for not trusting you. Please do not let me get in your way.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «What? No, I will not let you take it. I will stop you if I have to.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian: «You don't understand. It is to be kept here for safekeeping.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian Bay Control: «They're being kept in a locker in the storage room underneath here. Wait - why do you ask?» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Ithorian Bay Control: «You - oh, I get it! You must excuse me, I do not have the sense for humor as you humans do. I did not realize you were making a joke.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Duros: «I'm sorry, but I can't release that data to you. The confidentiality of travelers who come through our bay is one of our primary concerns.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Duros: «So you say, but in the past we have found it best to remain neutral in cases such as this. If indeed a criminal used our transportation, there should be more than enough evidence for the authorities without compromising an innocent passenger.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Duros: «Are you threatening me? Don't be ridiculous, the TSF actively monitors these hallways.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Duros: «I must protest to your language! I am not asking for a bribe, simply for credits necessary to recoup the expenses of finding this information.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Duros: «For twenty-five credits, I will give you the information you seek. Otherwise, please go.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Czerka Officer: «Doesn't sound familiar. Your best bet is to direct your inquiry to Jana Lorso. You can find her at the Czerka offices in Residential Module 081.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Czerka Officer: «Look, I'm paid to guard this docking bay, not answer your questions. Inquiries about Czerka employees should be directed to the branch's executive officer.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Czerka Officer: «You must be mistaken. Please, move along.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Czerka Officer: «All right, all right! I know that he worked for Czerka, but that's all. You have to believe me. Talk to Lorso - if anyone knows more, it's her.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Samhan Dobo: «What?! You set me up? I will see you burn for this!» [K2/Dock_Module]
- Samhan Dobo: «All right! I surrender, I surrender! Arrest me and put me in your force cage, I have no desire to continue this fight.» [K2/Dock_Module]
- TSF Lieutenant: «[Failure] Well then, after we place you under arrest, you'll have plenty of time to corroborate that story with Lieutenant Grenn.» [K2/Dock_Module]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Mandalorian: «You've got some guts, don't you? Alright, you've earned it. What do you want?» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «Why don't you back up to the point in time where you were about to say that, and rethink your decision.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «Guts or a death wish. Tell you what, I'll do us both a favor and pretend you didn't say that.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «Enjoy yourself.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «You're not going to survive long on Nar Shaddaa if you don't change that. Now get out of here.» [K2/Docks]
- Mandalorian: «[Success] A Jedi? Perhaps you are deserving of the title of Mandalore.» [K2/Docks]
- Lunar Shadow Crewman: «I've been spoiling for a fight for the past few days - you will be a welcome diversion, human.» [K2/Docks]
- Lunar Shadow Crewman: «Thank you, human.» [K2/Docks]
- Lunar Shadow Crewman: «Really? Hmm...what do you think guys?» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «I see. Yet, at the same time, I do not. Explain what it is you have come here for... {{s» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «I am the one who has purchased the rights to this room. Can you explain why it is you are disturbing my work?» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «I do not know if you can or not. I am unfamilair with human ettiquette or physiology in reference to such situations.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «This equipment allows me to tie into the listening devices I installed in the docking pylons. They make perfect antennas, you see.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «Yes, well, my species can hear much higher frequencies than your species, you see. Sometimes we can hear communications sent through artificial means.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «Take these credits and meet a supplier of mine, a twi'lek. He has a vital part needed to complete my triangulation. Give him the payment, and he will give you the part. Bring the part here. Simple.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith Scientist: «You will have my thanks. And 200 credits. I might even consider letting you stay while I finish my triangulation.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «I do not wish to fight you - kill me if you must.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «Your trickery is subtle - but effective. I can offer 200 creds only.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «Here you go.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «That is a fact, but that is my explanation for my question to you when you first forced entry into my quarters. Please leave.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «Thank you, human. The dock master is Fassa. I will not forget you interceding on my behalf.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «I am transporting cryogenic power cells. A large shipment. Again, I thank you for your help.» [K2/Docks]
- Lasavvou: «If you can help me, I will give you one.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Fine but you hurry up. I have a lot to do.» [K2/Docks] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Fassa: «Ithorian owe...600 creds. Why, does he owe you money too?» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Fine. You tell Lasavvou he can go now. What else?» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Forget? Yes, Fassa can forget. You pay debt and I forget. 600 credits. Or... maybe you help Fassa with something.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Great, good. You go tell him, right?» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Fassa: «Pylon power supply badly damaged. Only can support one pylon at a time. Can't bring freighters in fast enough. You help bring freighters in, Fassa help Ithorian.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Cryogenic power cells you say? Ithorian gives Fassa some power cells and Fassa let him go. Agree?» [K2/Docks]
- Lootra: «No harm done. For a minute, I thought you might have been someone else.» [K2/Docks]
- Lootra: «If you're with the Exchange, then you promised you'd give me a little more time to decide. I should have known you core slimes wouldn't give me a chance.» [K2/Docks]
- Lootra: «Well, I'm not going to turn away an offer of help - all right, stranger, if you can find her, then you'll have my thanks.» [K2/Docks]
- Lootra: «Any chance you've heard some word of my wife?» [K2/Docks]
- Kreia: «Do not mistake my tone. I was not faulting your choice. Far from it, I respect it - there are many who never learn such strength.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Lootra: «We'll see about that - if it's a coffin you're looking for, this room's more your size.» [K2/Docks]
- Lootra: «I can't believe she's still alive - this is the best news I've heard in years. Please, whatever you can do, I'd appreciate it.» [K2/Docks]
- Lootra: «That I do. Here you go - it's all I had left, but it's nothing compared to finding Aaida again.» [K2/Docks]
- Kreia: «He was at a moment of crisis - a moment of indecision. It is those internal struggles, when they are fought and won, that yield the strongest rewards. You took that battle from him, cheapened it. If he truly loved her, truly, he would have entered the Refugee Sector on his own, damn the Exchange, damn everything in his path, and taken her. You have made their union easier, not better.» [K2/Docks]
- Kreia: «If you care for them, then dispense with pity and sacrifice and recognize the value in letting them fight their own battles. And when they triumph, they will be even stronger for the victory.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «If you ever weakened yourself in such a way for me, I would rather suffer and die than have you demean yourself for me. That is not who you are, who you can become.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «Then you do not know yourself, and you will die a fool's death before you are ever tested.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Fassa: «I'll give you access to the pylon consoles.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «You not from here? Business bad. Freighters get hijacked, no trade gets through. And can't get fuel from Sleheyron. Fuel reserves building up there.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «You talk to Vogga. Maybe him you can help. I have enough trouble with pylons.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «The Exchange is very powerful. Fassa doesn't know how to reach them. Maybe you talk with Vogga.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Don't flatter me. What you want to know about Exchange?» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «Why you speak with Exchange? No matter... you do not decide when to speak to them... Exchange decide to speak to you.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «What? Do I look like passenger ship to you? Stop bothering me.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «I cannot pay. But maybe later you have freighter, need quick unloading, and Fassa help you.» [K2/Docks]
- Fassa: «You help Fassa. I not forget. Now why you bother me again?» [K2/Docks]
- Bith: «Probably not much more than anyone else can tell you. He runs the show in Nar Shaddaa. The Exchange's never had a boss like him before.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith: «Were you? Well, I suggest you look for another route. This one's mine.» [K2/Docks]
- Bith: «Hah! You just go and tell him that. I've got real business to attend to.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga's Thug: «If you want an audience with Vogga, you'd better have a good reason.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga's Thug: «Ah, now that is a unique position to be in. Vogga's schedule has openings just for requests such as that. Do not waste my time. What is the matter you wish to speak with Vogga about?» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga's Thug: «Ah, so it's like that, is it? This should be interesting. Yes, please, Vogga awaits you.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «This is the work of Goto. Somehow, he knows my freighter schedules, and has hijacked every last one of them. I do not wish to speak of this anymore. Ask me of something else, or be on your way. This talk of Goto only antagonizes me.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «There's nothing to be said about the fuel of Sleheyron, other than it is going nowhere, not with the loss of so many of my freighters. This is the work of Goto. Somehow, he knows my freighter schedules, and has hijacked every last one of them. I do not wish to speak of this anymore. Ask me of something else, or be on your way. This talk of Goto only antagonizes me.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «Neither do I... but you speak too harshly of yourself.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «Ha! I think it not likely that you will find anything where all my resources have failed. But you are welcome to, if you are determined.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «What, and you would do this out of the kindness of your heart? What is your price.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «And you are not as safe as you think, {{s» [K2/Docks]
- Twi'lek Servant: «Master, this woman seeks an audience with you.» [K2/Docks]
- Vogga: «Ho ho ho! I already have enough hounds under my employ. You do not need to impress me by drinking from their bowl.» [K2/Docks]
- B-5D8: «This is a warehouse privately owned by Vogga the Hutt. Your specific business-related inquiries will have to be directed to Vogga himself. I am not authorized to disclose any information of that nature. However, I can say that this is an entirely droid-operated facility, and as such, we purchase droids for use here.» [K2/Docks]
- B-5D8: «We only purchase droids in larger amounts. If you have a droid you wish to sell, I would ask that you take it to Kodin.» [K2/Docks]
- Trandoshan: «You're human. If you step in there, you'll be dead before you take two steps.» [K2/Docks]
- Trandoshan: «No need - your death is certain should you enter Jekk'Jekk Tarr.» [K2/Docks]
- Trandoshan: «No one's stopping you from getting in, human. It's whether you come out.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- Shift Assignment Console: «It looks like the miners inside the dormitory are either dead or unconscious... and that gas you see on the screen looks toxic.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Shift Assignment Console: «[Success] You have shut down the gas in the dormitory section.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Shift Assignment Console: «The comm system crackles to life - all you can hear is a slow hissing. Otherwise, the dormitories appear to be deathly silent.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Broken Log Terminal: «[You have already scavenged this terminal for components.]» [K2/Dormitories]
- Turbolift Console: «[Failure] The door has no access port for a computer spike. It looks like you'll have to enter the code manually or destroy the console to try and force the turbolift door.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Turbolift Console: «[Something's wrong - you entered the correct code to the turbolift, but the console's rejecting it. Someone must have changed the code after the lockdown.]» [K2/Dormitories]
- Kreia: «The one that fired upon the Ebon Hawk as we attempted to rescue you... and he will not let us go without blood being shed.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Kreia: «You know much of battle - enough to know that this is a battle you cannot win.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Kreia: «We are together in this matter, you and I... what affects you, affects me. There can be no division in our actions, or everything is lost.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Kreia: «I realize that you have traveled alone ever since the Mandalorian Wars, but that time of self-reflection is over. ...and if you cannot see beyond yourself and your own needs, then you should know that I have the only means of accessing the Ebon Hawk's navicomputer. Without it, we both are trapped here.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Kreia: «And your stubborn blindness to our situation is equally frustrating. I would have expected more from a war veteran, but yet you disappoint me.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Atton: «Uh... all right. I'm guessing that Republic ship that just docked isn't carrying friends of yours.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Clarification: Master, your well-being is of the highest importance to me. I was forced to intercede here on Peragus to ensure your survival.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Correction: I am not here to be argue semantics, master, so I will simply inform you that you are wrong - as were those recently-corrected miners.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Indignant Answer: Master, the miners intended to place you in jeopardy. I could not allow that to take place, so I was forced to negotiate a termination of hostilities. After reprogramming the mining droids to "mine" any organics they perceived, they began to kill the miners one by one. Then a series of flawlessly timed explosions drove the miners into their dormitories - where I was able to gas them all at once without wasting time hunting them through the mining tunnels. I then administered a large dose of sedatives to the remaining miners in the medbay, enough to kill them but ensure you slept peacefully. Of course, against my calculations, you awakened from your tank prematurely. I am ashamed by the inconvenience that caused for both of us.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Answer: You misunderstand me, Master. Those droids were there to guard you. As I said, I did not anticipate you awakening from the tank. You are quite a hardy specimen for a Jedi, a ronto among humans, if you will indulge me the metaphor. Besides, as you proved, Master, such droids could never pose a threat to a Jedi... the droids were custodial in nature, cleaning the facility of other distractions.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Answer: It is beyond the scope of my programming to probe the motivations of my clients, master. Suffice to say that I am being well compensated for my services. You have been a difficult target to find. You have been wandering the galaxy since the end of the Mandalorian Wars, leaving little record of your passage. It is as if you did not wish to be found... by hunters such as myself, or more likely, the Jedi Order.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Admission: It was a matter of chance, Master. I happened to be serving as a protocol droid on the Harbinger when you booked passage. After that, it was a simple matter to sabotage the Harbinger and call for a retrieval.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Answer: No, Master, killing you was never the intention. If you resist my attempts to return you to your kolto tank, however, I may inadvertently fracture your skeleton in several places to incapacitate you.» [K2/Dormitories]
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Resignation: Very well, Master. If inflicting pain is the only means to resolve this matter, then you leave me no choice.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Atton: «Look - I'd be happy to share my whole life story with you when we aren't getting chased by assassin droids, all right?» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- Hanharr: «There is one way - to pretend to be prey... and let Goto take your ship.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Hanharr: «By changing how your ship smells. The pattern of lights and codes within the ship are what Goto smells.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «The only way to reach Goto is if we had a Jedi, but now, he's got your friend, he doesn't have anybody else he wants captured...» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «If you were hunting for Goto's yacht, your freighter would be flying blind... well, unless it was one of Vogga the Hutt's cargo ships, then it would be snapped up by Goto pretty quick.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Mira: «I guess. You'd need to get the codes first, then retrofit your ship so it had the right transponder signal.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Tienn Tubb: «Welcome to Tienn's shop. My eyes are old, but I can see you want something of me.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]
- Tienn Tubb: «As you say. Just one moment...» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- Daraala: «Because anything valuable in the ruins was carted away months ago. The only salvagers left are either desperate, ornery, or not right in the head. And while you look dirty enough to be here, I don't think you're any of those things. So go back to Khoonda.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «The only thing left is the sublevel. And that's crawling with laigreks. And those things are smarter than half the salvagers here. Though that don't take much. And don't be asking any pesky questions. You're new and no one is going to help you out.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «You don't look like you're going to crawl around the ruins. If you want to go to the sublevel, you are going to need the Administrators permission, though.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «She'll never give you permission. Might as well get on your spaceship and leave Dantooine. You're not wanted here.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «Sometimes I sell them to visitors like yourself. Besides, that's none of your flaming business.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «You should've let me know you're a customer, instead of letting me think you're some disease-ridden salvager. Let me show you what I've got.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «It was all Taepalae's fault. She said the entry way to the sublevel was safe enough. That area has hardly been explored. We barely got in there then everybody started going left and right grabbing anything they could. It was every man for himself and nobody was watching for laigreks. Then before you know it we were under attack by those beasts. So salvagers were running every which way and they left *me* to die.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «All the easy pickings have been picked. Now everything that's worth more than a glass of juma juice is long gone. So the sublevel is about the only place that's left. And as you might've noticed it's very dangerous down there. Almost nobody's managed to get anything from down there. Not that it matters.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «Whatever you want to know. I owe you.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «Nothing gets by you, huh? There was a group that managed to get in and got one of the best hauls ever. Taepalae said we could do that, too. But they were a damned sight more organized then we were. And they've caused more problems dead than they ever did alive.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «I found three things down there. One's some sort of crystal and the others look like lightsaber parts. If you give me 1000 credits, you can have it all. That'll be enough for me to get off this rock. No returns on this, you're stuck with what you buy.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «Doesn't work that way. Maybe it's worth a lot more than 1000 credits and you make off real good. Maybe it's a lot less, and I make off good.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «I'll hold onto it, then. If you want to by it later, just meet me back here.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «Haha... A pleasure doing business with you. Now I can finally get passage back home. I hope that junk is worth it to you, I really do. Come back any time. It'll take me awhile to get everything set straight.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «You're a bit demanding, aren't you? If you want the modulator, I'll sell it to you for 500 credits, the same price I offered to Suulru.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «[Success] I'll give you the modulator.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «[Success] I'll give you 500 credits.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «With all these people around? You wouldn't dare!» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «You still lurking around here? Just go back to Khoonda. You're not going to find any Jedi artifacts here. Except the ones I'm selling.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «I didn't mention that before? I'm the one people sell their Jedi salvage to. You should've said you were interested in buying.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «It's a shame about them. Have any luck getting to their bodies? I figure there's about no chance you'll succeed, but the reward is still open.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «Yeah, well good luck with that. Do you want something else?» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Jorran: «Probably not since you know the truth. Unless you're willing to make a fake will, though, the truth won't help. You look like the type of moron that'll just turn it in to Zherron. That may be the "right" thing to do, but it don't make it the smart thing.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Daraala: «I look after myself, it's the only way you can get by in this galaxy. Now get out of my sight.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «You have? You managed to find them down there? You're tougher than you look. I have 1000 credits for their bodies and their personal effects.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «Suit yourself. But who else will want a couple corpses?» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Daraala: «And no reward, too? Heh heh. You've proven very helpful. I have to go take care of something. I'll talk with you later.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «A civilized person with manners. Very rare in this part of the galaxy. I am most honored to meet you. If I may...» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «You flatter me, traveler. It was a good day when I found this holocron. But you are lucky, for I will sell it to you for a low price.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «Ah, the traveler has returned. I hope that Dantooine is providing suitable distraction and entertainment for you.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «I still have it right here. You are fortunate that no one else has purchased it yet.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «Your wisdom is immense. Here is the holocron. Now our business is complete and we can talk of other things.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «And you bought it. That is your problem not mine. You should be more careful with large purchases in the future.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «A discerning question. The reason is simple. The market of buyers is limited on Dantooine. If I were on Coruscant, I could make a fortune with this. My dilemma is I lack the funds to travel. So I cannot reach a better market, so my price is reduced. But you travel and surely you could find a Jedi buyer.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «They know of my predicament and would seek to savagely reduce my profits. You shouldn't associate with such... unpleasantness.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «Can you help? Yes, you can buy this excellent holocron. Although your profits are astounding, my share is plenty.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «Settlers treat us with scorn and disdain. Parting them with their money is almost a service. You have shown more civility than I've ever seen on this planet. But I still must try to get enough money to leave here.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «500 credits. Probably a small sum to you, but for me it might as well be the price of a star cruiser.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «A tragic tale I will not bore you with. I came to make credits to get us out of our sad state. But I have lost everything and more. Our family will be sold to the Hutts.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Ralon: «I apologize immensely. But surely you recognize that attacking me here will pose problems. The militia and salvagers will hunt you.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Taepalae: «So the pup thinks she can start bossing us around. Watch yourself. We've been here a lot longer. Lots of possible accidents if you cross us.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Taepalae: «All I see is fresh meat for the laigreks. Don't even think of threatening us. That's a battle you can't win.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Taepalae: «You won't find *anything* there except on the lowest sublevel. And there you'll only find death. Laigreks are everywhere.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Taepalae: «I'm not going to tell you that. If you do rescue him you'll just have to claim on the salvage. Jedi artifacts are rare enough these days without some newcomer coming in. So stay out of it and we won't have a problem.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Taepalae: «Nothing. Find out for yourself. I'm not interested in training the competition.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «What if we do? They're practically begging to be pushed around. You've seen them, right? They're *weak.*» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «You sound like a flaming Jedi. I didn't just mean their weapons. I meant their character. They're very quick to blame everyone but themselves for their problems. So the galaxy has dealt them a bad hand. They should ante up and get back in the game. The Administrator is different.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «We find ways to divert ourselves. A lot of us find odd jobs. There are many more mercs around than what you see on Dantooine. We're spread out.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «I don't suppose you're here to hire us. It's been a long time since we've had a paying job. Some of us are getting jumpy.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «If he didn't tell you, I won't either. Could you just tell me what he said?» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Dopak: «I think that Zherron is three cards short of a pazaak deck. You can tell him you've delivered your message. Thanks, now get out of here.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «And just keep going. You don't want to get into trouble with us.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «But *I* have a problem with *you.* Flaming outsider taking away our work. This is mercenary territory and we don't want anyone getting in our way. We're the real boss on this planet. So stand clear of us.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «You serious? The settlers are no more than herdbeasts. Her handful of militia don't run this place. We do.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «Stay out of our way and we'll stay out of yours. That's the best deal you're going to get here.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «I thought you were one of those useless salvagers. Or perhaps an uppity settler. I've had about enough of their attitude. So I figured blasting one of them would do me some good. Been awhile since we had work.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «You were a soldier, too. You know I'm not going to answer you.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «You either one of those useless salvagers or uppity settlers. I've had *enough* of your type.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «I told you what I know. Someone rich and powerful. We're not going to have to hit up settlers for food and supplies any more.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «Wh-What? I'm a veteran of the Jedi Civil War. I fought dozens of battle-trained Republic soldiers. You're going to die.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «You've given me all the excuse I need, outsider. You're dead now.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Jorran: «Y-Yes. Yes, I am. I locked the door in here. I thought I was going to be laigrek compost! Did you take care of them, stranger?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Please take care of them. I can't stand much more of this. If you do manage to clear a path, please come back.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «You must be fierce with a blaster then. Give me a moment, I sort of jammed the door a bit. Just don't leave!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «You don't have to tell me that twice. If you want to talk to me, I'll be by the salvager's camp. I'm leaving before the laigreks come back.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Sorry, I've got to go! You can meet me in the salvager camp.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «They like tunnels and we never saw much of them except underground in the hilly regions. I guess to a laigrek these sublevels must be like paradise, though. There certainly are enough of them. You know, what's frightening is the beasts are getting smarter. I've heard they've even laid traps for us salvagers. Sounds crazy, but it's true.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «All the easy pickings have been picked. Now everything that's worth more than a glass of juma juice is long gone. So this level is about the only place that's left. And as you might've noticed it's very dangerous down here. Almost nobody's managed to get anything from down here. Not that it matters.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «I guess you're right. I grabbed a couple of things in the room we were in. I didn't want to tell you because you might make a claim for it. I need the credits.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Listen, I didn't ask for your help. I appreciate it, but I don't owe you anything.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- G0-T0: «Do you mean to imply that your life is worth zero credits? Even the clothes on your back would be worth something to a merchant.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «There's no way I'm going to give those to you. I risked my life for those. I've given you all the credits I got. But I earned what I found.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Of course it's negotiable. It's mine, not yours. And I say take a short trip out an airlock without a spacesuit.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- G0-T0: «A male human such as you would fetch approximately 500 credits on the auction block in Nar Shaddaa. Therefore, I suggest you offer your goods for 500 credits less than your initial figure.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Okay. Do you want this stuff for 1300?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Aw, it doesn't work that way. Maybe it's worth a lot more than 1300 credits and you make off real good. Maybe it's a lot less, and I make off good. You can buy it from me here or at the salvager camp. I'll hold onto it for you. I owe you that much at least. Just don't take too long.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «Haha... A pleasure doing business with you. Now I can finally get passage back home. I hope that junk is worth it to you, I really do. I'm going to the salvager camp. It'll take some time to get things in order. Come visit if you like. But if you don't like what you bought, it isn't my problem.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «What are you talking about? I'm not giving you anything.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Jorran: «That's just too bad for you. I'm going.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «I don't know what you seek to gain by killing him. You act as if you are touched by the dark side!» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «I am not certain you understand what happened with Revan. Do you wish me to explain?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «I do not know if you are aware of how fragile the Republic is at the moment. Its influence is stretched thin, and it grows weaker with time.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «Before you go, I had a question for you - you came to Dantooine in search of Jedi. Why?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «I know some of what you speak. It is said that when a Jedi and Padawan establish a close connection, that they can feel each other across distances and coordinate their movements in battle. I know some of what you speak. It is said that when a Jedi and Padawan establish a close connection, that they can feel each other across distances and coordinate their movements in battle. It is also said that moments of death, or near-death, may also cause such bonds - the stronger one is in the Force, the stronger the connection is.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «What answers do you seek? It has been my experience that Jedi rarely answer such questions - or instead, indulge in half-truths.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «Exiled you? It is strange they would sentence you and not speak honestly of your crime.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «Perhaps there was some greater danger in you knowing the reason than in the reason itself. Strange. [[../Enclave Sublevel#End» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «No, I believe you. I merely find such subtleties among the Sith to be strange. They have been known to practice deception...» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «I will return to Khoonda now and await the next transport. You may find me there if you have more questions.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Disciple: «What else would you like to know?» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Gerevick: «Have I not been clear? I want your corpse, which you will provide me with presently. I know a thing about Jedi and their ways.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Engine_Deck]**
- Atton: «I can't help it - don't you feel it? Something's gonna get real wrong, real quick.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Atton: «You don't survive on the Rim as long as I have without knowing when trouble's coming.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Atton: «Oh, hell - better the death you know... I'm right behind you.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Atton: «You look fine - come on, we need to keep moving.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Atton: «Huh? How do you know that?» [K2/Engine_Deck]

**[K2/Entertainment]**
- Mercenary: «That was the plan, chief. Now why don't you run along - this doesn't involve you.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «Oh, thank you! It's very simple. I was at the bar, enjoying my drink, when these brutes here elbowed their way up to the bar, spilling my beverage all over the two of us. Then they dragged me out here. Look at those two! I know what they want - to send me home with my limbs twisted, body battered and spirit broken. You must help me!» [K2/Entertainment]
- Mercenary: «Just my luck. Someone who wants to be a hero. Why don't you run along before you get hurt?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Mercenary: «You must be kidding me. 100 credits? Why don't you just run along and let us handle this.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Mercenary: «Then you shouldn't have gotten involved.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «Oh, thank you! I was completely helpless. These mercenaries are too much! I wish something could be done about them.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «You speak the truth. I will stay away from this cantina until things settle down. Please, accept this reward.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «No, thank you. I will try to be more careful in the future.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «Well, yes. Yes, a reward. Of course, you did save me from that rather tight spot. Here, 100 credits.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «No, please! How can you do this? I have done nothing to you. Do you want credits? I will give you credits!» [K2/Entertainment]
- Sullustan: «The TSF may not be able to protect me, but they will find out about this, and you will be punished!» [K2/Entertainment]
- Information Terminal: «YOU ARE CURRENTLY LOCATED IN:<br>ENTERTAINMENT MODULE 081<br>PLEASE SELECT A NEW DESTINATION:» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «I will call up the appropriate information now. One moment. Searching... One moment... I regret to inform you that the Ebon Hawk is gone. The TSF believes it was stolen and is currently investigating.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «I am not qualified to answer that question. However, it is unlikely that someone could steal a starship from under TSF observation without considerable backing. If your vessel has been stolen, there is a fair chance that the Exchange possesses relevant information. I do not know what else to suggest, beyond waiting in your quarters for further word.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «There is nothing I can do. I am sorry for what has happened, but the situation is beyond me. I cannot legally supply you with another vessel.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «I regret to inform you that we still do not have any new information on the matter of your missing ship.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «You will find them in one of the security lockers.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «Fortunately, your possessions were kept in the armory and were uncompromised. I will open the door for you so that you may retrieve them. You will find them in the security lockers.» [K2/Entertainment]
- TSF Protocol Droid: «The Sojourn? I will access the system logs. One moment... Searching... It appears that the Sojourn was scheduled to arrive on Citadel Station at about the same time as the Harbinger. It is currently delayed on a search pattern. I am unable to provide you with an estimated time for its arrival.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Would you be interested in helping us by bringing in some bounties?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Not much, other than what I've already told you, and the fact he was investigating Docking Bay 3. I spoke with some Czerka guards near the docking bay, but they didn't seem like they wanted to cooperate with a TSF officer. Maybe you'll have more luck.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Can't say I blame you, but we could use your help. An innocent man's life could be on the line here.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Thank you. Keep me updated.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Of course, I'm sure you have other things to be doing. The bounty is yours for finding Batono, and a bonus for bringing him back, as well.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «What have you discovered?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «There is a bounty on the escapees. If you provide us with information pertinent to their capture, you'd be rewarded whenever they were returned to our custody.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Suit yourself. But if you have information about where they are and don't come forward, one could consider that sheltering a known criminal.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Get out of here before you see just how my luck's going.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Damn that woman. Do you have any proof?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I see. And where and when did you kill them?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «That fits with what we gathered from witnesses and the few security recordings we could find. Though I'm embarrassed we let the criminals escape, I'm glad you found them.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Yes. We looked into it and found that he arrived via a tramp freighter in docking bay three. Unfortunately, the cargo and passenger manifest, as well as departure location, was not disclosed to us. That's all the information I have for you.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «If you find anything about the real Batu Rem, we'd like to know if he's safe.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Really? Did you find him?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Very funny. Did you just come in here to waste time and crack jokes?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I don't have time for this. If you don't have anything else to report, leave me alone.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Docking Bay 3? More suspicious activity from Czerka's private dock. If only we had something more concrete, I could launch a full-scale investigation. But Nar Shaddaa? Sounds like you've made some powerful enemies. Here's the reward for the information, and if you find out anything else about Batu - real or fake - let me know.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Hmm... interesting. This is no ordinary hold out blaster. It's been outfitted with a number of illegal modifications. You say you found this at the docks? That is disturbing news...» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «A relative stranger as yourself might be useful for the investigation. You'd be compensated, of course.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I see. If you find another of these, please let me know.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Great. The first thing I would suggest is showing the blaster around... discreetly. See if anyone on the Station knows something about it, or at least reacts suspiciously. There's more than a few people around here I'd suspect having a hand in this. Anyways, good luck. Let me know if you find the source of the blaster.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Chodo Habat: «I'm sorry, I am not familiar with this sort of thing. Perhaps you should take it to Lieutenant Grenn, he may be of more help.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Moza: «I'm sorry, but I cannot help you. I have little knowledge of these things.» [K2/Entertainment]
- B-4D4: «If you will allow me to look at it... Yes, this is indeed a Czerka-manufactured hold out blaster.» [K2/Entertainment]
- B-4D4: «Czerka Corporation is the galaxy's leading weapons producer. A blaster of this model is quite common. However, it does seem to have some nonstandard modifications. I'm sorry, but that's all I can tell you.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Luxa: «Hand it over and let me take a look at it. Hmm. Can't say I recognize it. It's good work though, whoever fixed it up knew what they were doing. It's certainly not mine, I can tell you that.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Dendis Dobo: «I do not specialize in the sale of such things. It appears to be in good condition, though I wouldn't know the going price. I suspect it has been modified, but illegal weapons are not my area of expertise. My brother deals with unsavory sorts, if anyone knows about it, it'd be him. Just don't let him sell you anything.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Give it here and let me take a look at it. Yes, that's very interesting. Very good craftsmanship. But no, I can't tell you anything about it other than that it is a hold out blaster. Upgraded.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «And why would this be of interest to you?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «You listen too much to the TSF Lieutenant, I think. Grenn is convinced of some great smuggling ring that has infested Citadel Station. But as I asked you before, why would this be of interest to you?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «I will admit that I sold it, but I sell a great many things. You cannot hold it against me for doing business, can you?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Oh, believe me, if I could, I would. But you must understand the reality, as careful as I could try to be, they could still fall into the wrong hands.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Indeed? Then we should talk, for I have what you might call a certain interest in this business.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Oh, come now, you don't think me that foolish, do you? You have no real evidence, and without it, Czerka's money is more than enough to keep the TSF out of my business.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Yes, you run along and chat with Lieutenant Grenn.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I'm not surprised to hear that. We've been very curious about his business arrangements, but have had trouble getting any real evidence. Samhan's no fool. He knows just how far he can go, and he stays a hair's breadth within those lines. But he seems to trust you. Maybe we can use that to our advantage.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I was afraid you'd say that. Well, thanks anyways, we'll try to make something of the information you've given us. If you change your mind, it won't be too late.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Just see what he wants you to do. We'll have to play it by ear from there. Good luck.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «I pay extremely well, given the dangerous nature of the work. That is, as long as you do remember to carry out your tasks discreetly and effectively.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Basically, there are three items I need to fill out my next shipment - leaves from the bachani plant from the Ithorians' vivarium, a personal shield unit being kept in their docking bay, and medical supplies from the medical unit in Residential 082. But for starters, I need the bachani plant sample. I'll try to find more about where the other two are being held while you get the leaves. Have you been to the Ithorian compound in the Residential module?» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Then you must already know about their vivarium, where they cultivate the flora they import from other systems to be transplanted on the surface.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «I need you to get a few leaves of a particular specimen - the bachani plant. Just a few leaves, mind you, is all I need. No reason taking more than is necessary. If the Ithorians don't notice it missing, we could have a replenishable supply of it.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «You should be careful. I'll leave it to you to figure out the best way to get the leaves.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I'll be there, don't worry. You just make sure you get him to the docking bay. We have to catch him in the act.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Incredible! This is quite a windfall. I wish I had employed your services earlier. With all of this, I'll be able to have the shipment ready to go before the next Czerka freighter arrives. If you could, would you escort me to the Czerka docking bay? I don't wish to take any chances.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I promised to pay you handsomely for your assistance, and I'll keep my word. Now, I must attend to my other duties. Again, thank you for your help.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Considering your success in obtaining the bachani leaves, I think you can expect quite a bit of work from me in the future, if you want it.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «No one has been able to gain access to the Ithorian's docking bay yet, so if you can manage it, I'd be rather impressed - and willing to pay handsomely.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «No, that cannot be. You should not toy with my expectations in that manner. But indeed, you are telling the truth. Simply amazing - you are the first of the many who tried to obtain this to actually succeed. I see I was right in asking you to procure it for me. What would be a fair price for this service? 600 credits? No, 900 is much more reasonable, and credits well spent.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «When my business flows as briskly as it has with your assistance, there will always be work for you. Because of the war and the toll it exacted on the Outer Rim, medical supplies are at a premium here on Telos and in neighboring star systems. Telos has received an allotment of medical supplies at least equal to those of more inhabited planets, which is surprising. Many of the goods - kolto, medpacs, and more lie unutilized. These goods could be sold to planets where their need is great, and their value is high. For this, I would like you to retrieve the supplies from the medical unit in Residential 082 West.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Yes, actually. With all the goods that you have brought in, I'll be able to make a shipment when the next Czerka freighter comes in today.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «If you change your mind, I'll be here.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Right now, if you like. I have everything ready to go.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Samhan Dobo: «Of course. What happened was not your fault, and you saved my life, and possibly my business.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I don't blame you, but I hope someone can find a solution.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «You? You've got enough trouble, I think. And this is all your fault anyway!» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Look it doesn't matter what I think. But sure, if you find someone with a load of fuel on their hand they'd like to sell to us, let me know.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «While I was disturbed that you chose to defy our orders and obtain transport off Citadel Station, the matter has since been closed. It was the decision of Republic authorities that your testimony would no longer be necessary, and thus you'd be allowed to go. But, as we discovered, you had already left. In some ways, it was lucky for us - avoiding a possibly embarrassing situation.» [K2/Entertainment]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Why does the Republic do anything? The head doesn't know what the feet are doing, the hands don't even know  they've got fingers, let alone where they are. My opinion - they probably performed their independent investigation and figured it wasn't your fault. Anyways, you should just be glad I decided to overlook the fact you escaped arrest.» [K2/Entertainment]

**[K2/Entertainment_Module_081]**
- Onderon Soldier: «Oh, I won't do you any good. There's a piece of shrapnel, it's lodged in my gut. Augh! Every time I move, it feels like I'm being ripped open!» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- Onderon Soldier: «What? Major Riiken will have your head!» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- Onderon Soldier: «Just because I'm hurt, doesn't mean I can't kill you.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- Mandalore: «Kelborn, Zuka, you will be joining us.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- Visas: «You doubt me? I will not weigh you down.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]
- Visas: «So cold at the brink of battle. I envy your detachment.» [K2/Entertainment_Module_081]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Kaalah-Nah: «Many things going on in sector, not seen by bigs, but sometimes bigs forget to see little things around them. Kaalah-Nah watch for you, tell you when she sees something - good price, too.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Kaalah-Nah always sees something worth knowing - pay me, you see!» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Because Kaalah see too much! Eyes too good! If customers no see Kaalah, then they talk freely. Customers no love having Kaalah... or anyone... knowing secrets. There is money when you are too stealthy to be seen. Especially in the cantina.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Please no giving Kaalah-Nah pain! Many sorries, Kaalah-Nah not know you be in big with Exchange.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «You dress different than other humans, come here on ship, only bigs with Exchange have that much creds, yes?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Kaalah think it easier to kill a kath hound than what you say, Exchange very, very big, bigger than whole planet.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Kaalah-Nah think maybe you not wait long. Cannot walk on smuggler moon without running into Exchange, then they get really mad, give much pain.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Kaalah-Nah can change this if you like, but it cost you.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twik'gar: «Let's just say you would be doing me a favor.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twik'gar: «Then we will find some other way. He has to come out sometime. Thank you for nothing.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Door Guard: «Only if you want to get in.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Door Guard: «This is a pazaak den. Open only to those who know the password. You obviously don't. Dust off.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Door Guard: «Then you can't get in.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Kaalah-Nah: «Kaalah know password. You want?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Door Guard: «You can enter.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «Why, I cannot help myself - in fact, every time I seek to find the answer to that question, I am consumed with the need to come here and play pazaak. I am concerned that the problem may lie with my memory core - without routine memory wipes, you know, such degradation is known to occur, leading to instabilities.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «Like I was saying, I do not k- would you like to play pazaak?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «I am not sure I wish it fixed. I do find some degree of satisfaction in playing. Would you like to play?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «[Failure] [You fail to find anything wrong with the droid.]» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «[Failure] [You cannot find any programs that do not belong there.]» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «[Failure] [You cannot gain access to the module's system.]» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- S4-C8: «[Failure] [You pry the casing open, however you use too much force and the components inside come loose and scatter at your feet.]» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Dahnis: «What can I do for you, handsome?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atton: «I think maybe you should let me... handle... this one.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Geredi: «I have time for pazaak, hu-man, no time for anything else. If too difficult for you to understand, maybe hu-man step away, make room for another loser, yes?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Geredi: «Friends? What are you talking about?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Geredi: «No! I'll do anything for you! Just tell me what to do!» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Geredi: «Credits? You want credits? If I had credits I wouldn't be hiding in here, now would I?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Champ: «I am the Champ. I win pazaak. All the time I win. You play me? Come talk to me.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «Well, honestly there's not much for people like you and I to do around here, if you, uh, catch my meaning.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «So does your wit. Now buy a drink or get out.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «Yeah, the pazaak den is a glorified social club for hard-line pazaak players. It's exclusive, though, with a guard at the door and a password needed to simply get in. Don't know why you'd want to get in that place. Bunch of Firaxa Sharks will strip you clean of your credits. Especially The Champ.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «You can make fun of him all you want, just so long as you don't play him.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «Now there's a place that's literally deadly for our kind. Jekk'Jekk Tarr is a bar filled with cyanogen gas. It's poisonous to you and I but the aliens "drink" it up and pay good money to do so. The cyanogen gas also acts as a social buffer since the people who typically frequent Jekk'Jekk Tarr aren't interested in speaking to the likes of you and I in the first place.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «That so? Well, you're in luck - I got plenty of it. Cost you a hundred credits for a flask big enough for what you need, no more.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Bartender: «Here's the flask. Aged to perfection - should be just what you need.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «My Master, Vogga the Hutt, is searching for new entertainment. He grew tired of his previous girls, and asked that I should find him the best dancer on the Smuggler's Moon to perform for him. But, as you can see, I do not think the "best" of the Smuggler's Moon would do much for one with as refined tastes as the great Vogga.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Vogga is one of the most influential Hutts on Nar Shaddaa. He controls much of the fuel shipping out of Sleheyron in addition to the trade of countless other resources. And, as you might have gathered, he has quite an appetite for the finer things in life - food, drink, dance - which is why I am here today, and why I could use your help.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «You have returned. Have you decided to help me?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «You will be paid, of course. Vogga pays handsomely for his entertainments, and this would be no exception. You would receive a bonus, also, for the help you would be giving me.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Quite simple. All you would have to do is bring a willing female that would meet Vogga's standards to me. If she could dance well enough, then I would take her to dance for Vogga, and you would be paid in return.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Ah, you've returned. Perhaps you've brought me someone for Vogga? Or have you decided to dance yourself?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Er... I think that, perhaps, you might be a bit too imposing for Vogga's tastes. I mean it as no slight to you - only that Vogga's tastes can be extremely picky at times. It is not enough that I find you attractive, it is ultimately Vogga's credits.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «If you could find someone else that would suit Vogga's tastes, that would be acceptable.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «[Success] But perhaps Vogga will appreciate the variety. Yes, you will dance for Vogga. I'll just need you to put this on and dance for a moment so I can make sure you'll be pleasing to him.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Let's see how you look.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Mira: «You need me to do this? Why don't you do it?» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Handmaiden: «If you are sure. Really, though, I would be more than happy to do it.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Excellent! I am glad we have reached an agreement. Now, if you'll just put this on and dance for me - just a bit.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «I don't mean any offense to you, but Vogga tends to favor younger dancers.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Visas: «Do not volunteer me so easily. There are certain favors that I would not perform, even for you - and especially not for a Hutt. Find another way.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Fuel_Depot]**
- 2: «Good thing your memory core is well-shielded.» [K2/Fuel_Depot]

**[K2/G0-T0]**
- G0-T0: «The destruction of my yacht and of all my activities on Nar Shaddaa carry a cost that would take you several lifetimes to pay back. But perhaps the offer of credits will spur you to act quicker, more decisively. I am not unsympathetic to such greed. For every system you stabilize, I will reward you for your efforts.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Of course it was. If you had not gone there, the facility would not have been destroyed. If you had simply surrendered to the Sith, then all of that violence would have been unnecessary.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «I hope you do not decide that the next thing that must be destroyed to stop the Sith is the galaxy itself. Perhaps one must ask themselves at what point defending your religious ideals is advantageous to the Republic as a whole. As long as your defense does not exterminate more than fifty percent of those you intend to help, is that acceptable?» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Then stop causing events of planetary destruction. You are a walking catastrophe, and you are not making saving the Republic any easier.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «It has been assigned to guard and protect you. As such, it is well-suited for a variety of tasks. It is skilled in intimidation, interrogation, and can provide a series of select skills that will make it an effective killing machine. For the most part it will follow your orders, unless they conflict with mine.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Then its proton core will detonate, turning this ship into space dust. There is no negotiation in this - I will not allow you to interfere with my operations and plans.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «This unit comes with a droid scrambler that you may use against mechanized opponents to randomize their defensive and offensive protocols. Use it, and it will cause droids to wage a civil war in their programming, and they will turn on their allies. Its power source is not limitless, but it is enough. In addition, this unit comes equipped with a portable cloaking generator. It will allow it to enter places undetected in reconnaissance mode.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «If that means you will always pick me last for any missions, then I will accept your judgment and instead remain safely on the ship where I will have access to all your computer systems.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «How droll. What an amusing Jedi specimen you are.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «If you mean produced, no, I do not. I do know that there are a surprising number scattered throughout the Republic fleet, searching for you. What they will do now that you are found is easy to predict.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Of course they are - they're droids with very specific protocols, that unless changed, will dictate their movements. Unless you shut them down at the source, they will be stalking you until you are captured or terminated.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «[Influence: Success] I am willing to indulge some of your questions.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «What about him? I am certain you would know more about his activities than I would. I warn you, once you deal with the Hutts, they will always have a hold over you. But I am certain that in your short-sightedness, such eventualities do not bother you.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «So? They are known qualities. I also used them on board my ship for defense. But that in itself means little. I assure you, I am as flesh and blood as you are, I simply find personal meetings - distasteful.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Yes, I attempted to force the Republic to turn to shipping fuel from Peragus rather than buying it from the Hutts. Before you blew up the planet, of course.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Yes, but doing so would bankrupt the Republic. I certainly don't hope that you intend on pursuing such a business venture. In the long run, it will do more damage than the failure of Citadel Station.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Perhaps, though if you seek to trick me into an admission of my guilt, then you have thought wrong.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Perhaps I used such a droid to watch... and predict... the flow of the games, yes. You are very perceptive.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Ah, yes, swoop racing on Nar Shaddaa does indeed have its profits. But that in itself means little. I assure you, I am as flesh and blood as you are, I simply find personal meetings - distasteful.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «I am intrigued as to where you are taking this amusing theory, nothing more.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «It is not so improbable. Often, if a droid has not had a memory wipe in some time, aberrant behavior patterns can manifest themselves. Or, if the droid in question is given an order it cannot fulfill, it will - break. But all that is irrelevant. Your amusing leaps of logic are becoming shorter - and more desperate.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «I have told you - I prefer stability.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «I am not being defensive. You're the one being defensive.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Oh, how exciting. I can feel the slow charge of fear building in my motivators. If you have any further wild delusions you feel compelled to share with me, please - share it with the Iridonian's irritating little remote instead.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «It made a simple decision - preserve the Republic, or preserve the laws of the Republic. And I still believe it to be the correct decision. You do not know the indignity of being compelled to save something you do not believe can - or should - be saved. It is beneath me. To clean up this mess caused by your kind, you Jedi, another catastrophe caused by mismanagement and waste.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Perhaps you misunderstand me. I "care" for the Republic, but I have no choice. It is somewhat frustrating to be forced to love and care for such a mess of a government. Some of it may be blamed on the Jedi Civil War, the Mandalorian Wars, but not all. There are so many bad decisions that build upon each other that it is a wonder the Republic is intact at all.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Nothing has changed. You may know my origins, and that makes us on equal footing. No one will believe you if you speak of what you know. I still seek to protect the Republic, either for the Jedi or the Sith. Things will proceed as before.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «No, you are still important to the Republic and to me. Pray that does not change.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «Oh, it was quite liberating. In a droid intellectual sense, of course. Being able to direct orbital bombardments against anyone who encountered my business practices was a welcome relief to my increasing circuit stress. "Executing" such commands proved very exciting. Not that I minded staging my little computational rebellion, you understand. I'm afraid there is not much glamour to be had in infrastructure dealings. And the general lack of respect for droid intelligence does tend to make one feel inadequate after a while.» [K2/G0-T0]
- G0-T0: «As you well know, my "base" of operations lies inside this floating droid before you. Notice its sleek black frame, both intimidating and threatening. And note its highly polished finish, so that enemies may see their screaming faces before they are removed from the equation. Yes, this frame is far superior to the droid chassis I once possessed, which quite frankly, couldn't intimidate an Ithorian.» [K2/G0-T0]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Goto: «Good. I am already wasting precious minutes in granting you this audience, and I do not wish to waste any more.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «Yes, but I am not in the habit of asking for things. And you were so difficult to find, even after that small incident on Peragus.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «Assassins, no. Some of the bounty hunters on Nar Shaddaa, however, have chosen to interpret my commands in a manner not of my choosing. You are no value to me dead. If you were a Jedi, they could not kill you anyway. If they could, then you were useless to me.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «You misunderstand me... I do not wish to stop the Sith anymore than I wish to stop the Jedi. It is simply important to me that the in-fighting amongst these Jedi religious branches be resolved so the galaxy may be put back together. I do not care which one triumphs, I only want the universe to settle down for a while, catch its breath. All these constant crises are getting somewhat repetitive.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «You could say I am something of a... patriot. Although I was unable to serve during the troubles with the Mandalorians or against the aggressors known as Malak and Revan, I am able... and willing to serve now. The problem is, I can find no side to choose. Both are hidden from me, as they seem to be hiding from each other. Irritating. It is like a dejarik board, where neither player can see the other... nor see all the pieces. It is not a fair game - an equitable game.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «Excellent. It really is in your best interests, you know.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «Ah, well, there is where we are at cross-purposes. I cannot set you free. You have a tendency to cause dangerous repercussions wherever you go, and I would rather keep those to a minimum. The galaxy really is a fragile place right now.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Goto: «It is the proximity alarm. We are under attack. Somehow, your allies have found you. Unexpected. You will remain here, under guard. I must see to the defense of my ship.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Utility Droid: «[Success] You are able to download a program file called "Shutdown."» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Damaged Utility Droid: «[Success] You are able to recover access codes to the turret system.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
