- Mandalore: «Ask around the camp. Someone else might have something you can do to lend a hand. I also promised you a reward. Here you go. This should help you fend off some of the local beasts. You're well on your way to earning some real respect around here. Keep it up.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «When you were last here, you probably didn't even go to Onderon. The Republic knew that Onderon's fate would be decided on its jungle moon, not the planet itself. To begin with, when you talk about Onderon you're really talking about the ancient walled city of Iziz. It's the only inhabited settlement on the planet.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «I don't blame you. They're a sad people. I have some things to look into. I'll talk to you later.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «No, just you. You're no Mandalorian. I don't know why Mandalore has let you roam free, but I don't trust you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «You again?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «Blasters, energy shields, armor, and other fighting gear. All in good condition - though I doubt you could afford it.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «Yeah, I do. I don't know why Mandalore would let some outsider roam around our camp without an escort. If it were up to me, you would've been shot on sight. Manadlore must think you're something special.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «I won't - as long as the Mandalore's orders stand. If he wants you dead, though, then I'm going to be the first one gunning for you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kex: «It's bad enough I have to stand here and pass out supplies while the others go on patrol. I'm not going to disgrace myself further by being your guide. The only advice I'll give you is when you're in the jungle, shoot anything that moves. Then shoot the things that don't move, just to be sure.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guide: «You want to hike through this jungle for hours, go ahead. But I can get you where you want to go a lot faster and safer than you crashing through the undergrowth like a drunk boma.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guide: «I'll guard your vessel, then. The path to the camp hasn't been patrolled recently.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «If you want to pitch in, go ahead. Zuka hasn't been able to get the damn thing up and running. You can find him in the eastern side of camp, just south of the hangars. Look for the sparking wires, and he won't be far.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «We heard reports of a zakkeg wandering the paths around there. They're a good challenge - for Mandalorians. If you see one, stay out of its way. Without us to back you up, you'll be dead in seconds.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «You, fight a zakkeg? If you survived such a battle, you'd gain much honor.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «Strong words. It would be amusing to see you try to kill such a beast. Try the outlying jungle paths, if you're determined to seek one out. And bring me back a trophy from your kill, as proof of the deed.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «Really? And where is your proof?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «You obviously have a small understanding of our ways. Hearsay is not acceptable proof of your accomplishments. Bring me a trophy from your kill.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalorian Guard Captain: «I'm impressed. Too bad you're a Jedi. You'd make a formidable Mandalorian.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Xarga: «You're wrong, it is my business. While you're a guest in this camp, you can start earning your way.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Xarga: «If Kumus wasn't dead he'd be back already. If you find his body, just bring it back.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Xarga: «Do as you will, but you won't impress anyone here by scavenging through the jungle for the gear of our fallen brothers.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Xarga: «Take your time. Corpses have a lot of patience.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kumus: «That's true. I was indeed fortunate that you came along when you did.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kumus: «I forgot you are not a Mandalorian... we do not typically take payment in exchange for life.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kumus: «I have little to offer except my gratitude. If that is not enough, then perhaps it would have been best if you'd left me to die in the jungle.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kumus: «[Success] Very well. Here - take this. It is worth less than my gratitude, but perhaps it will satisfy you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «You're trying to help Mandalore? If you can fix the power problem I'm having, I'll put in a good word for you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «You change your mind? I could use some help.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «I'm trying to get the relay dish operational, but several components are broken and need repair. Without the relay, we're vulnerable to attack, especially right now. Your space battle may have attracted unwanted attention, and our shuttle's sensors can't cover the whole moon.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Broken Wires: «[Success] You have fixed the broken wires.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «You managed that? I thought that was a lost cause. Now if I can only get the phase-pulse converter and the power working, the sensors will be operational again.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «That's the best thing I've heard in weeks. I'll be sure to tell Mandalore about your help. Look, I have some extra parts and computer spikes. Take them - you can probably make better use of them than I can.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «That's the problem with cannoks. They'll leave you alone if you keep your distance. But if you turn your back on the pests, they'll eat anything they can get in their mouth.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «Just gut every cannok you come across. You wouldn't believe the things you can find in those little beasts.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «Hmmm... no Mandalorian would lower himself to this task. Forget it, then - if you happen to come across a converter, then let me know.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «All right, then. Come back when you have the converter and we can see about getting these systems working again.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «Let me see... Well, it looks like the phase-pulse converter's been broken into three pieces. The cannoks must have been fighting over it. This piece is in good enough shape to repair. If you find more of them, bring them to me.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «You do? Let me see... I don't know how, but these pieces look in good enough shape to repair. I'll have the telemetry computer repaired in no time.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «That's pretty mercenary of you. A lot of us have been down that road recently. Here's some credits. I appreciate the help. Now I have some work to do.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «I'll be sure to tell Mandalore how much help you've been. I know you didn't ask, but here's a reward anyway. Mandalorians are expected to do anything they can to help. But you're a stranger, and you didn't have to help us.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Zuka: «All right, if you say so. Again, you have my gratitude.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- Kelborn: «You received my message, then? A man named Kavar wanted to get a hold of you urgently. He said that the Queen had arranged safe passage to Onderon for you. But I don't know how good their offer is anymore.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kelborn: «He wouldn't tell me anything. Just that he wanted to see you. He said it was urgent. But that doesn't matter now.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kreia: «You... you are correct.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kreia: «You must choose who will lead the expedition through the jungle to find our enemies and defeat them.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kelborn: «He said he wanted a meeting with you on Iziz, that it was urgent, and that you'd know what it was about. But things have changed.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «General Vaklu is prepared to make a generous offer when you meet. But before that we need you take care of the Sith Lord's base of operations.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «The Sith Lord has been using an ancient tomb on Dxun as their base in the system. That tomb isn't too far from your encampment. You need to assemble a group that can eliminate the Sith at the tomb. We'll coordinate with Kelborn when you're ready to strike. After they're attacked there, the Sith will turn on us, so we'll need you here with us. You'll have to divide your forces. We need your skills on the planet. You should select someone you trust to lead the group to attack the Sith camp.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «I look forward to working with you in person.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Mira: «I'll do it, but you're still my bounty. You won't lose me this easily.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kelborn: «Mandalorian warriors will go with you to find the enemy camp. Just let me know when you want to head out. If you need supplies, talk with Kex. After you get into the jungle you might not have another opportunity to stock up. Let me know if you want to go now.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Kelborn: «As you wish. Mandalore has arranged for special transportation to get to Iziz.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Captain Gelesi: «Everything seems in order. You're cleared to go.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Gelesi: «You're cleared to go.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Gelesi: «There are reports of Republic smugglers arming dissidents in the city. Checkpoints have been set up throughout the city to prevent more civil disturbances. There are two types of starport visas. The type you have is an individual visa, good for just you and any pre-registered staff and family that accompany you. The Office of Emigration also issued open visas.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Gelesi: «I don't make the law, off-worlder. Only the Queen or General Vaklu can issue starport visas. It's a lengthy process, costs money, and there's a long line ahead of you.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Gelesi: «I wouldn't pay the beggars near here much mind. Just ignore them and go about your business.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Vaklu Soldier: «I don't have time to answer your barbed questions, off-worlder.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Vaklu Soldier: «[Success] I have the... wrong man. Yes, you heard me right. We have the wrong guy. So let him go. But watch it, both of you. If I hear even a hint you're up to something I'm coming back.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Vaklu Soldier: «What do you mean?» [K2/Merchant_Quarter] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Vaklu Soldier: «I like the way you think. Men, let's take the back way to the barracks.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Vaklu Soldier: «Hmph. Men, let's take this spy away. Another word out of you and we can make the trip very uncomfortable.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Terlyn: «Captain, you're back? Please tell me you have an open starport visa.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Tolas: «You have the look of someone used to adversity. A cut above the rabble around here. I have a business proposition for you. Everyone around here begs and pleads night and day for open starport visas. Very touching how well they act out their lies. Or truths. It doesn't really matter. What does matter is credits. Hard currency. I offer you 2000 credits if you find an open starport visa. You won't find a better deal.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Tolas: «So you're back. My offer still stands 2000 credits for an open starport visa.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Tolas: «Does it matter? Truly matter? No. I have no sad story, I just know people who will pay a great deal for an open starport visa. The credits are your share of my profits. The visas are very rare, but I have a feeling you may come across one.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Tolas: «I only know of one person who might offer something comparable. And what's going on there is illegal. I'm a legitimate trader, so there's no risk. There is only one thing ultimately more important than money - and that's your life. She's trouble, I wouldn't deal with her.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Tolas: «I want nothing to do with her. If you want to get involved with that sort of person, you'll have to figure it out on your own.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Tolas: «No, I don't think so. It's really very simple. We don't have anything more to talk about unless you get an extra visa.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «A drexl is a flying beast. Huge creatures with teeth the size of your arms. Lethal and skin like ferrosteel plates. Somehow the beast-riders figured out how to tame them years ago.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «Certainly. Things are quiet right now, I have some time. What do you want to talk about?» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «That's a long story. You sure you have the time? Most people don't care for an old soldier spinning a tale or two.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «I don't blame you. My grandson gives me that same kind of look when I start getting too long-winded. So what do you want to talk about?» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «These are troubled times. You've probably heard that General Vaklu and Queen Talia are having some... disagreements.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «Times are too complex for a simple soldier like myself. Sometimes I made the mistake of answering questions honestly. If you say anything bad about a superior officer they call that disrespecting the rank. Do that too many times and it's not long before you're a civilian.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Gegorran: «You are? Wait... This is not so. You think to make a joke on Gegorran. Haha.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Gegorran: «You have returned. My stock is filled with weapons I can only sell to offworlders. Please take a look, the finest bargains in all of Iziz.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Gegorran: «General Vaklu is concerned about well-armed insurgents. They've had to get arms from somewhere. Not from me, of course, but all sales of weapons have been severely restricted. I can only sell to off-worlders like you that have the proper clearance. And now offworlders can't come here because of the blockade and searches, but it's a wise policy.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Ponlar: «Yes, you. It is up to each and every one of us to turn the tide. To take control of our future!» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Ponlar: «Leave here, off-worlder. My words are meant for the sons and daughters of Onderon. Your type continues to plunder our world. And we have nothing to show for it. Begone!» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Onderon Soldier: «Thank you, off-worlder... We may have to hold them off alone until backup arrives.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- G0-T0: «Proceed with your "plan," then.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Mandalore: «Shrewd. I hate to lower myself to joining this mob, but your plan may work.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «Your help would not be without its compensations. Speak to me later if you change your mind.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «I am a strong supporter of General Vaklu. The Merchant Quarter is an important area. Not only financially but strategically, as well. It is one of the few districts that is directly connected to the Royal Palace. The Queen and her advisors are well aware of this and have ensured that the Captains here are extremely loyal to her. This presents complications for Vaklu. If you could arrange for them to be removed from their command, you will be richly compensated.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Mira: «You're wrong, everything is about money. It's just a question of how much.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «You're back to visit me.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «You can't attack them directly. The entire city would be after you if you assaulted their checkpoints. You may have to be subtle and patient. They don't have to be killed, just removed from command.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «For each task you complete, I am prepared to pay you 2500 credits. Plus you will earn the favor of some powerful people. We could make your job much easier here.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «Your efforts are appreciated, and success will be richly rewarded.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Gelesi: «We stand at the checkpoints all day long. It gets hot out there, and my feet are always killing me. But now I'm in charge and everybody's problems are mine. So I have to deal with all the people begging to get out of the city. Every one of them's got a story. Let me tell you, there are a lot of sad stories out there.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Bahima: «Of the many spiritual duties a bartender requires among my people, what you are asking is not one. Perhaps we could talk of something else later.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Panar: «And right when I thought you were boring, you've gone and got interesting. Go on.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Panar: «He owe you money? Actually, it's better if I don't know. Just give me a name and 500 credits and we got a deal. We'll pay this guard captain a visit and turn the old hydro-spanner on him a couple times.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Panar: «That I can do. All I need to know is who and whether or not you got 500 credits. With those two, we can do some business.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Panar: «Pleasure doing business with you. He's right over there, isn't he? Just wait a little while.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Onderon Soldier: «Yes, tell them what you really think, Riiken.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «[Failure] I'm doing what I feel is right. Are you? That's something that each person has to answer for themself. This topic is leading nowhere except for trouble for me. So help a guy out? Let's talk about something else.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «Then my recommendation is to bide your time. Eventually an opportunity may present itself. He does occasionally patrol the run down part of the Merchant Quarter by himself. Perhaps your paths may cross then.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Bostuco: «I can't imagine it would come to a civil war. But you might be right. Maybe the reassignment was for the best.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Devaronian: «I have pushed away the human sentient. My apology to you.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Visas: «Are you so certain? Vaklu is strong, capable, and is willing to take risks to get what he wants. The world of substance has little place for absolutes of ideology.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Devaronian: «I agree with you, human sentient. Queen Talia has good thoughts and instincts. She will be a great leader.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Bostuco: «[Failure] I'm the one in charge here, off-worlder. If you try to get past me, then you'll have the entire Iziz garrison on you in seconds.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Bostuco: «I told you before, off-worlder, the Sky Ramp is restricted to military personnel only. Now move along.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Mining_Tunnels]**
- Atton: «Wait, wait. Don't tell me you're taking that hatch down into the mining tunnels... are you? That explosion I heard came from below. There's probably nothing down there except superheated rock and collapsed blast tunnels. You'd be an idiot to go down there.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Me?! You're the Jedi - if one of us has to go, then it's you. Guardians of peace and justice throughout the galaxy, last I heard.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «There's no way I'm going down there. You might as well lock me up again if you freed me just to throw me into a nest of gundarks.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «I'll take coward over stupid any day. I'll keep the comlink on just in case you need some help... not that I'll come running, mind you.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «You're either really brave or really crazy - or both. All right - I'll try to monitor things from up here. Be careful - the only thing moving down there is likely to be mining droids, so don't be playing hero too hard. Uh, not that I care what happens to you or anything. I just don't want to be trying to get off this rock by myself.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «I'll keep the comlink open... I may be able to guide you through the tunnels from up here. Don't know if the signal will hold if you get too deep, though.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «What did you say? Your signal's crammed with static.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «If you can handle yourself, fine - I'm just looking out for my own skin. Atton out.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Dammit! Uh... I mean, good, good to hear it. No sense in you running around half-naked. It's...it's distracting... I mean, for the droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Yeah, it's like a military-issue energy shield, except it's designed to protect the miners against lasers... and heat. Should work against the droid mining lasers. It won't last forever - and certainly not against multiple laser hits - but it may buy you some time if you get ambushed by a battalion of droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Just equip it on your wrist, and then you can activate it like a medpac. Again, it won't last forever, so make it count.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Uh, just one more thing - I've narrowed down some of the ID signals, and if the numbers are right, you're sharing those tunnels with a battalion of mining droids.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «You could, if you have the skill and a stealth field generator. These droids rely on thermal sensors, primarily to detect fuel deposits. The good thing is, that explosion down there kicked up so much heat and steam it may blind them... a bit, but not much.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Yeah, it's possible some of the droid models may have mining shields on. If so, the shields may absorb laser fire. You can usually tell when a shield is active - it'll make an electrical field around the target. If they activate a shield, the best thing you can do is hit them with a melee weapon or try to burn out the shield with continual fire. But that could take a while, and it leaves you a target.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «There's got to be some central controller down there. See if you can find a terminal by the main access shaft: that'd be governing intelligence.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Those mining droids, especially the excavator models, are designed to arm and set sonic charges for mining. Chances are, if they still had charges after they went rogue, then they may have set them to try and kill the miners... and you. If you see one of those excavators, watch out - they may use their undeployed charges as projectiles.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «There was one in the emergency supply crate near the entrance. And one of the miners down there may still have one they couldn't switch on before the blast hit. Look around. If you can't find one, there's nothing much you can do except try and avoid the areas where the steam is thickest.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «Sensor...? Must be maintenance drones. Watch out, those little pests will try to repair the mining droids if you don't gun them down first. Still, it's odd they're still active after the explosion - they don't have the same shielding as the mining droids. Be careful of those droids, I don't like this.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Central Controller: «[This will overwrite all current commands on the droids. Do you wish to continue?]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Central Controller: «[This effect will cause the droids to open all locked doors on the level. Do you wish to continue?]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Central Controller: «[Judging from the droid schematic displays, it looks like Atton was right: the droids rely on thermal sensors to detect fuel deposits. You could easily blind them by raising the temperature.]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Central Controller: «[Note that this will also prevent droids from being able to shoot each other if reprogrammed to mine each other. Do you wish to continue?]» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «The containment fields in the mining tunnels are shutting down - you need to get out of there before they vent fuel to the surface of the asteroid through the tunnels.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]
- Atton: «I may be able to keep it contained until you get the turbolift to the fuel depot, but not for much longer. I'm locking down the turbolift to the administration section now to keep the blast from spreading. If you've got anything left to do down there, make it quick, because where you are is going to get real hot, real soon.» [K2/Mining_Tunnels]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «I was going to ask you that... it's just that your face, it's like you haven't been getting much sleep. What's wrong with you?» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Your face... you, well, you have this glow. I mean, not a real glow, but... It's like you're calm, at peace? But it's more than that. You haven't been chewing on spice, have you?» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Okay, maybe not spice. Maybe someone just jammed a power coupling up your ion engine and switched it on full.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Oh. Well, it shows. It's like you're hooked up to a power coupling, it's weird. I mean, not bad weird, just weird.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «You know. Hooked up a power coupling, you know?» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Did you get out much as a Jedi? I was asking if you two had been... you know, intimate.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «All right, all right. Keep your robes on. Here you go.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «No arguments here. Still, as long as you accept it, the place isn't so bad. Better than sitting here... in silence.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Well, it's not how most people see it. You kind of have to let the place grow on you... like a fungus.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Well, the war happened, the first one, against the Mandalorians. Had family right up until the end. It's not really a new story - you hear it all over the galaxy. It's what happens after the wars are over that you don't hear much about.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I think so. After Revan crushed the Mandalorians, planets throughout the Republic were flooded with refugees. I was just one of the others. Me, I got passage to Nar Shaddaa. From there... not much you can do, so I became a bounty hunter.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «When I was young, yeah. They mostly used me to carry ammo packs and munitions. Toward the end of the war, they needed everyone they could get. They taught me to fight, to hunt, to survive. I was part of their squad, even when I was young. Everyone served as part of the unit, and I felt like I had a place there. After Malachor, it didn't really matter any more - the Mandalorians lost. Bad. But you know that.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Yeah, I know. I saw the worlds they left behind them during the war. That kind of stays with you - I haven't forgotten it. What happened at Malachor - they, they probably deserved it.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Should I be? Maybe I should ask you if you're happy about all the Jedi who died on Malachor V. Maybe it felt like you lost family there. But I doubt it.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Not if you know your target. Usually you get a holo or rough description, and then you just sort of listen to it, get a feel for him or her. Then start walking.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «This is a little hard to explain, but Nar Shaddaa's got a flow, a life to it. You see all kinds of aliens and life there, and it's got currents.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «If you know your target, you can feel them, know where they're going to go. And sometimes, you know where they're going to be before they do. I'm good at finding people. Because when they're lost or out-of-place... it's like something's wrong, inside them. And that's why I hunt.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Oh, really, is that it? How you could ever possibly hope to understand is beyond me. Jedi don't have family. I know what happened at Malachor V, and I know the Jedi didn't care about life there.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Get away from me. The next time you come and ask me a question, I swear I'll shoot you in the head and dump you out the airlock.» [K2/Mira]
- Atton: «I've known people like her. Maybe without the rocket launchers, but sort of the same. Maybe without the plunging neckline and the boots. I wouldn't let anything she says bother you - it's a wonder you cracked her attitude at all. She's cold as the ship's hull.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Whatever - but you were the one asking all the questions. You deserved it.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «There's a lot of lost people out there, scattered ever since the Mandalorian Wars. Sometimes... it's like you can almost hear them, like an echo, calling out for each other. And maybe, just maybe, by finding them, I can start putting the galaxy back together.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «That's what I think sometimes. It all seems so hopeless - there's too much damage, inside and out. It's all you can do just to survive.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Maybe. We'll see. I don't even know why I'm telling you this. But you're not getting anything else out of me.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «[Influence: Success] Do you really want to hear this?» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I don't remember the name - something with too many 'k's and 'y's. Sounds like you're gargling ronto spit when you say it.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Well, before you get too proud of him, Hanharr figured Czerka had the right idea.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Anyone who paid credits - and sometimes he just hunted humans for sport. The ones who survived he sold to the Exchange, to the Hutts, to anyone who'd buy bodies... living or dead. He and Vogga used to do big credit transactions - that Hutt really liked the look of unwrinkled humans for some reason. Didn't make him too popular with the other Hutts, let me tell you.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I don't pretend to understand it, but among his people, they have these codes of honor. But somewhere along the line, Hanharr's got twisted. His people form these things called 'life debts.' If you save the life of one of them, they pledge themselves to you.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «A life debt to Hanharr is a death sentence; he'll hunt you until you're dead. When I saved his life, it meant he had to kill me. And so he kept chasing me in hopes I would die. I think the fact I showed him mercy after hating humans for so long, that was something he couldn't stand.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «[Influence: Success] Maybe one day I'll let you.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «All right. But I doubt you're going to show me anything I don't already know. And when you show me, don't act like a tourist. It attracts predators.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Are you going to train me?» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I've endured worse. I can deal with this. Can you train me to do what you do, with the Force?» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I want to become like you. I want to be strong, and I don't want to be afraid or alone any more. I don't want to keep running, and looking, and never feel like I'm finding what I'm looking for. I'm tired of being hunted.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I am ready. I shall not fail you.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I have to tell you - what you showed me on Nar Shaddaa... it's changed my view of things, on the galaxy. For the first time, I feel in control. I feel... powerful.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «I... wanted to say thanks. For what you showed me on Nar Shaddaa. It's going to take some time to let it sink in, but... But thanks. I feel... alive.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Sure, they're easy. That's why I dress like this. When they're looking down to check you out, you can usually smash them on the base of the skull, or deliver an uppercut that knocks them flat.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «It's simple - when you want a man, you jab him with a Bothan Stunner, then while he's screaming in pain, slap some stun cuffs on him. Then starve him for two or three days until he becomes open to suggestion, then double-check his bounty and see if he's worth anything.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Call it what you want. Me, I love my targets.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Why, you trying to be my mother? No thanks, already had one. Somewhere.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «It's more than age. I thought I'd seen a lot, but you look like you're a hundred inside.» [K2/Mira]
- Mira: «Run away? You're the one who couldn't handle me - I'd kill you.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Atton: «It means "glorious jewel" in Huttese, but don't let that fool you. It's the central breeding grounds of the Hutts - Nar Shaddaa orbits it. Nal Hutta's as slimy as the Hutts, lots of swamp and bloated gas. It's where those slugs reach out and grab chunks of the galaxy. Trust me, we're not going to go anywhere near the place unless we want to be washing the stink out of our clothes for the next few years.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «Maybe a little, but landing here means we didn't have to transmit our ID signature, and you know what trouble that always brings. In fact, while we're here, we should get those signatures changed. Wouldn't make us such a target when we enter a new system.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «Yeah, in all its glory. Don't get your hopes up from what you see here, though - as soon as we hit the main sector, that's when the smell... and the mobs... can get pretty bad.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «Well, that means finding either a bounty hunter, a ranking member of the Exchange, or someone willing to talk - none of which are too appealing. Bounty hunters and the Exchange are going to want to shoot you, and someone who is willing to talk is willing to talk to anyone, which means trouble.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «All right. If you have any questions, just ask. We should be able to leave the ship here as long as we want, no one supervises these landing pads anymore.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «[Success] It'll be fine where it is? Yeah, I guess you're right.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «That's none of your business. All you need to know is that they're going to be firing up their quad lasers if they find your ship squatting there when they arrive.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «Eh, well, heh-heh! Don't let me hold you up, then, hm?» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «Hmmm. Well, I got your ship, and that's worth more than that if you decide to cheat me. All right, it's a deal.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «Done! Pleasure doing business with you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Quello: «Eh? My trash heap? Hnh. Fine, land there! My trash heap's all that's keeping your ship from making the final plunge. And trust me, it won't be long in coming, I promise you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- Atton: «I can't keep us in one piece much longer. Sorry to spoil your fun, but we've got to land now. I'm going to put us down on that jungle moon.» [K2/Onderon]
- Atton: «Well, the space battle's still going on overhead. Since they were so eager to use our hull for target practice, I doubt they're just going to forget us. I don't like it - Onderon is about as far from the Core as you can get and still be in the Republic. But even out here the locals have heard of us. We're lucky I was able to find this place to land - looks like something has cleared away the jungle in a few spots around here.» [K2/Onderon]
- Atton: «Until the ship is repaired, we're not going anywhere. Unless you can find another route to Onderon, we should sit tight.» [K2/Onderon]
- Atton: «Well, if you go, be careful - no telling what other ships were forced down in the battle.» [K2/Onderon]
- Atton: «The repairs are coming along. I hope to have the Ebon Hawk ready by the time you finish your business here.» [K2/Onderon]

**[K2/Pazaak]**
- S4-C8: «[You have defeated S4-C8.]» [K2/Pazaak]
- S4-C8: «[You have taken all of S4-C8's credits.]» [K2/Pazaak]
- Dahnis: «Are you ready?» [K2/Pazaak] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Dahnis: «You handled yourself well. Care to try again?» [K2/Pazaak]
- Geredi: «You beat me for last time, hu-man! Take your credits and go!» [K2/Pazaak]
- Nikko: «We haven't played before, so you should know I don't pull any punches. Every game, I play to win. You prove your mettle and I might throw in a bonus one of these rounds. But enough of my cautionary advice, let's play!» [K2/Pazaak]
- Pato Ado: «You are? That is good to hear! Unfortunately, I save my armor components for special games. Perhaps after a few hands...? I have many credits. We could play for 250 credits a game, if you would like.» [K2/Pazaak]
- Pato Ado: «If that is what you would like. Shall we play now?» [K2/Pazaak]
- Pato Ado: «This is good news. If you win you get the components. If I win I get 1000 credits. Is this acceptable?» [K2/Pazaak]
- Pato Ado: «May your journeys be equally fortunate, friend!» [K2/Pazaak]
- Pato Ado: «I was hoping you would say that. Let's begin!» [K2/Pazaak]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- HK-50: «Unnecessary Clarification: We merely wished to cripple your vessel. Once we tracked your coordinates, we were able to deploy several droids in this location.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- HK-50: «Self-Evident Answer: Wherever you try to run, we will be there, armed and ready.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Handmaiden: «I will not warn you again. Drop your weapons, or we shall take them from you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «The loss of your weapons shall be a temporary thing only - and it is necessary. There is much to be gained here... without violence.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Your concern is noted... your friends have not been harmed. They have been detained, for their safety. I find it... unusual... that you are traveling with others again. I had thought you had forsaken the company of others after the war. Or is that why you are here?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Yet here you are. Perhaps you do not know yourself as well as you think. Regardless, your arrival here begs an explanation. Have you come to face the judgment of the Council, as you did so many years ago? Are you finally willing to admit that we were right to cast you out?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Why? Because you turned your back on us... and the Order. You followed Revan to war against the Mandalorians. The very war that made Revan a Lord of the Sith... and ruined you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «The Republic asked the Jedi Order for aid against the Mandalorians, that much is true. Yet that aid did not mean rushing into battle - giving in to aggression, your hate, your passions.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «So you said, so long ago. I didn't believe it, then, and I don't believe it now.» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atris: «You sought adventure, you hungered for battle. You could not wait to follow Revan to war.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You, and all the Jedi who followed Revan, caused the Jedi Civil War - it was because the need to wage war burned within you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «There was much about that day that was difficult to forget - your words, your defiance - and when you stabbed your lightsaber into the center stone. I have kept it - so I would never forget.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Indeed. A lightsaber is the mark of a Jedi. When you turned your back on the Order, it was not yours anymore.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I suspected as much. You cared nothing for the trappings of the Jedi... and you cared nothing for the Order, even on that final day you came before us.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Life is filled with choices. And when you are forced to make one, you have the Jedi teachings to guide you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «So your choice was to meet the aggression of the Mandalorians with more aggression? That is not the Jedi way!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «And you were ruled by hate and aggression! That is not the way of a Jedi!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Every choice we make, whether we know it or not, sends echoes through the Force. It can awaken feelings, ignite passions, hate, anger, fear - where none existed before. By meeting aggression, by serving as an opponent against which the Mandalorians could test themselves, you fed their hate, their lust for war. And it sent a terrible echo through you. And because of it, you and those Jedi who met them on the battlefield lost their way... and you turned on us.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Without you and the other fallen Jedi to support them, to feed their lust for war, Revan and Malak's crusade would have been over before it began.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I know you betrayed the Jedi teachings. All that you had been taught, you threw at your feet and crushed them beneath your heel.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You do not kno-» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «How dare you? The Mandalorian Wars should have been your grave and Malachor V is where you should have died!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You are wrong, just as you were when you defied the Council.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «And that would have pleased you? So be it.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You see shadows where there are none, and hate where there is none. You are blind, as always.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Your ship - ah, the Ebon Hawk? It is not your ship. Unless you are admitting to the destruction of the Peragus mining facility.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «We are having some trouble with the navicomputer... but I think with your cooperation, willing or otherwise, that will cease to be an obstacle.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «If it is your ship, perhaps I should be questioning you as to what happened - and why you destroyed the facility and murdered all the miners stationed there.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «A facility of over one hundred and fifty personnel - all dead before you arrived. A childish story to mask your crime. And with the facility destroyed, you think there is no way to confirm your story. But I will pry the truth from you, I promise you that.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «At last your true nature shows its face. Serving with Revan truly corrupted you. I promise you this, murderer... I will see you tried and punished for what you have done. This is not the Jedi Council anymore - it is I who is judging you, and I will see justice done.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «It was too much to hope that you may have come here to finally admit the Council was right.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «A lightsaber is the mark of a Jedi. When you turned your back on the Order, it was not yours anymore.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You do not wish to fight for it? You surprise me. If you do not wish to fight, then answer my questions. What - happened - at - Peragus?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Ah... an accident. Something beyond your control.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «No, your crime is much more than that.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Telos was a test, to see if the Republic could mount a restoration effort on the Outer Rim. When it fails, the Republic will not finance another. The other Rim worlds devastated by the Sith will remain graveyard worlds, devoid of life. And that is the magnitude of your crime.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I have not known you since you left to serve Revan in the Mandalorian Wars. The only question that remains is how far you have fallen.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Indeed? And how do you plan to seek your revenge on me? Or do you intend to take revenge on all the council as well?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «So... it is as I suspected. You have already done enough. The dead Jedi, the murders - you have much to answer for, monster.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Jedi across the galaxy have been slain or have vanished. A darkness devours them, a new force shrouded by the power of the dark side. You are allied with them, as I suspected. You shall answer for your crimes, after I tear the knowledge of this new threat from you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You would say anything to avoid judgment... yet... I sense, somehow, that you speak truthfully. You are indeed ignorant of these events, or but a pawn.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Arrogant as always, even against the truths the galaxy throws at you. The Council was right to banish you - and you were wrong to return.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You speak truly... you have encountered the Sith. I can feel the scars on you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Peragus... what would they want there? They can't have been looking for you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You? If they thought you a Jedi, the teachings of the Sith blind them, indeed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «There are no more Jedi... except I. Like you, they turned from the Order, and now only I remain.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «We shall see... for now, the perspective on your situation has changed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You shall find them north of here, in one of the old irrigation chambers. They have not been harmed... they are free to leave as well.» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atris: «Take your ship, then - I don't care where you go, just leave this place, leave Telos.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «Then I shall send you on your way... it is now time for you to depart.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Prestige_classes]**
- Kreia: «The time to hide your presence is coming to a close, and you will need to increase your training. You are ready. Your training must increase, and there are higher mysteries you must learn. But only you must know the path you will take, I cannot choose it for you.» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- Kreia: «Very well, I shall wait. Return when you are ready.» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- Kreia: «It is not some great test you require to be what you strive to be - it is only your decision to find that path that matters. From here on, you guide your destiny. But in order to take the next steps, you must face your past and put it to rest.» [K2/Prestige_classes]

**[K2/Prologue]**
- Skip Prologue: «[Do you want to skip the Prologue?]» [K2/Prologue]
- Communications Console: «[Success] You have sliced into the Ebon Hawk cockpit computer, bypassing the main console functions.» [K2/Prologue]
- Communications Console: «[The door in the corridor behind you opens, allowing you access to the rest of the ship.]» [K2/Prologue]
- Security Console: «[Success] You have sliced into the Ebon Hawk security system for the port aft section of the ship, which includes the Engine Room, Utility Lift, and the Garage.» [K2/Prologue]
- Exposed Wiring: «[Success] The door is opened. You can now access the starboard dormitory through the garage.» [K2/Prologue]
- Hyperdrive: «[Failure] You do not have enough Parts. Search around the ship to find more Parts. If you can gain access to the Garage, you can use the Workbench there to create Parts.» [K2/Prologue]
- 3C-FD: «[Success] You have fixed 3C-FD. Now he will join your party.» [K2/Prologue]
- Workbench: «[This is a workbench. With a workbench you can break down objects in your inventory into components. You can then use the components to create repair kits, weapons, armor and upgrades.]» [K2/Prologue]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- Mandalore: «What the hell was that? I told you to wait!» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Missile Bay: «[You have retrieved the proton core from the missile bay.]» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Missile Bay Control: «[This could detonate prematurely, killing you. It would be best to leave it alone unless it is an emergency.]» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «You are right... the choice was mine. And you knew what would happen to me.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «You think to lie to me still? I will not be deceived again. I told him, you know... what the old woman told me. I told him of the Academy here... of the Jedi here. I... could not have kept it from him, even if I had wanted to.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Mandalore: «He means that Jedi witch you travel with – Kreia.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «He will create nothing... he will end this place. Your old woman has killed this planet, and with it, countless other worlds that depend on its survival.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «Waiting for you? No, he does not wait for you.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «There is nothing to be done except wait. If you go to him, he will destroy you... and your last moments shall be of shadow... and pain.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «There is nothing to be done. By coming here, you are already dead.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «You are right... as long as he exists, Onderon is in danger. The galaxy is in danger. What would you have me do?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Mandalore: «Go report to the target site... Colonel. I'll let my men know you're coming... and if you try anything stupid, they'll shoot you dead.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Kreia: «This matter has come full circle - and there is something there that you must hear, if you are to understand.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «Yes, afraid for you. As I always have been.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «Killing... your answer to everything. You defeated the Mandalorians during the war, only to become one of them.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «No, we do not believe so... but it is tied to you somehow.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «We cast you out of the Order because you followed Revan to war. There was no other reason.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «You already know the answer - you've noticed it in those who travel with you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «Have you noticed that when you act, others follow?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «It is not an easy thing to explain... surely you are familiar with Force bonds.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Kavar: «We do not know. But it is not the first time you've felt the weight of so many lives.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «We did not cut you off from the Force. You were merely deafened to it, because of that last battle of the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «Yes... you can feel the Force, but you cannot feel yourself. You are a cipher, forming bonds, leeching the life of others, siphoning their will and dominating them. It is the teaching of these new Sith, to feed on others, on other Force Sensitives. They are symptomatic of the wound in the Force. You are a breach that must be closed. You transmit your pain, your suffering through the Force. Within you, we see something worse than merely the teachings of the Sith. What you carry may mean the death of the Force... and the death of the Jedi.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «So you think. It is not the strength of a Jedi you feel.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «What if other Jedi went to war as you did, suffered the same events, and emerged as you did. What if there was a crucible that trained such Jedi to consume and kill?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «This must be done. As long as you feel the Force, you are a danger to those around you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «And so here lies all that remain of the Jedi. It is a powerful thing you have achieved here... you have brought death again to Dantooine.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «Is it as you expected?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «You are correct - time is something that neither of us has. But for now, we must speak.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «Oh, yes. Your hate has destroyed them - you should be proud of the blood you have spilled, the lives you have ended. But before enjoying this victory - there is something we must discuss first.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «Although I did not expect them to still live, I had hoped you would learn something from the Jedi Masters as they fell before you. Not just of battle, but of yourself... and the Force.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «I must know if killing them, if revenge brought you any measure of satisfaction. If seeing them dead has settled the disquiet within you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «To the Sith? No, not to the Sith - perhaps not in the way you would think.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «It is important to me - and I wish an answer, not more questions from you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «It was as I thought. You have failed me. Completely, and utterly.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «The Handmaiden took her. She thinks Kreia killed you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «Are you surprised? All that talk of hatred, manipulation, and standing on your own two feet - sorry, you don't get any more Sith than that. Still, if we were all judged by who we were in the past, I don't think you'd understand who we are now.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «That's what I was afraid you'd say.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Disciple: «That is an odd answer. What did you mean by it?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Disciple: «I simply do. There is nothing I can show you as proof, except give you my word. Something happened within the Enclave. What is it?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Disciple: «Then they do not understand you. That is the danger of being a Jedi. When one seperates themselves from others, chooses to lead a life of isolation, denying what makes them a feeling being - it is easy to make such judgments. And such judgments, I believe, are made in ignorance. There is no danger in what you represent - other than your humanity. You change others, but I do not believe it is due to the Force. I believe it is because you are a natural leader - and because you feel connected to the people around you. Where they look at you and see the death of the Force, I look at you and see hope for all life. And that perhaps a life lived without the Force is not the punishment it is believed to be.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «I would die for you. When I tell you my life for yours, it is my choice. And if there is an ending between us, where my sacrifice can save you, it will be because it is my desire, not your will.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «I simply do. And sometimes, there is no reason that can be given. What did you learn within the Jedi Academy?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «Then that is the danger of their beliefs. They do not understand you, what it means to be human, to lead. The feelings I have for you are because of what I see, what I hear in your voice, all that tells me you are a natural leader. I follow because I believe in you. I would die for you because I believe in you. And where they look at you and see the death of the Force, I look at you and see hope for all life. I look at you and see that perhaps a life untouched by the Force is not the punishment it is believed to be.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «He awaits you at Telos. If you go there, you must face him. And when you do, he will wound you as he has wounded me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «I ask you... I beg you. To stay here, with me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «I know. But I could not let you go without asking you, this last time.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «If you will not abide my presence, and I cannot turn you from your course, then there is one last thing I must tell you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Exchange Thug: «No lookers, keep walking and you get no trouble.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «Hnh? What you say?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «Exchange business? Exchange find you, human, not other way 'round.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «Your name not worth weight in mynock droppings, human.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «Exchange business not your business, human. Go away, before you can no longer walk.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «[Success] Very well. Human answers for human - but we see him again, we break him and you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «All right. What do you want, then?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Whatever your reasons - thanks.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Huh. You don't. He comes to you, if he's got reason to - either because you can help him out, or because you're making trouble. Either way, it's not a good thing.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «A lot of space lanes cross at Nar Shaddaa, and once here, it's hard to move on. Even fighter pilots from the war can't get work at the docks - the freighter crews are full up. There's no work here and no way out, unless you want to become hired muscle for the Exchange, or work in a Hutt slave camp.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Jedi Master? There aren't any Jedi Masters in the galaxy, let alone on Nar Shaddaa - this place is a cesspit, with the Exchange and the Hutts stabbing their claws into everything. But you know, I did hear something about a bounty on Jedi, though. Something the Exchange posted. Doesn't matter, though. Aren't any more Jedi around, so no one's gonna be collecting that bounty.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «You're lying... or mad. A Jedi wouldn't come to th...» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Not much to say, except it's a high price. You'd need to find a bounty hunter to get more, and they've been pretty scarce lately. You might have more luck trying to find someone connected to the Exchange - and I don't mean some spaceport muscle, I mean someone connected.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Well, they tend to lay low. To be honest, it's like with bounty hunters. You may need to do something to get their attention.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Friend, you did promise some credits in exchange for information.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Look, one other thing, it's obvious you're new around here; I can tell it just by looking at you. Nar Shaddaa can be a rough place, and easy to get lost in. If you want, I can keep an ear out for ya, let you know if I hear anything.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «I'll do that, then. I'll come seek you out if I hear anything I think you might want to know.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «F... forgive me, stranger... but if you had some credits you could spare, it would be a great help... please.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Forgive me, stranger... please, I beg you, do not kill me!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «And what would you rather do?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «The Force binds all things. The smallest push, the smallest touch, sends echoes throughout life. These acts of cruelty may have more severe repercussions than you know... or can see.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Thank you, stranger - I won't forget your kindness.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «The Force binds all things. The slightest push, the smallest touch, sends echoes throughout life. Even an act of kindness may have more severe repercussions than you know or can see. By giving him something he has not earned, perhaps all you have helped him become is a target. Seeing another elevated often brings the eyes of others who suffer. And perhaps in the end, all you have wrought is more pain. And that is my lesson to you. Be careful of charity and kindness, lest you do more harm with open hands than with a clenched fist.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «Good. Mind what I have said. Use your power, but in its proper place.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «Very well. But mind what I have said. Use your power, but in its proper place.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Tienn Tubb: «Ah, good, good - business is always welcome. Eh... what kind of business you in, human?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Tienn Tubb: «Ah, yes, you may avail yourself to it, it is no trouble. But please return the hydrospanner to its proper place when it is done. And the fusion cutter. And the plasma torch.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Tienn Tubb: «It is not a matter of payment, it is a matter of equipment. Transponder cards are permanent once written. I have none. I would need the new transponder card you wish to use. Or a blank card and an ID signature. Bring me this and we can discuss payment.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «That is unfortunate. It seems I misjudged you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «Very well, but if you change your mind, please do return.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «I was wondering if you might be able to get him back - not for sentimental reasons, mind you. The utility droid in question has some plans of Master Tienn's stored in its memory core. The plans are really quite necessary to be retrieved.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «Oh, no, they're simply schematics for a new prototype droid shield. Quite fascinating, really. One of his rare moments of genius. Of course, it occurs to me that I shouldn't have said that to you just now, since it was intended to be something of a secret.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «Oh, thank you! I knew you were a helpful sentient as soon as my photoreceptors focused on you. If you retrieve my predecessor's memory core, then I would be most appreciative.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «You have returned. Have you been able to retrieve IT-31, by any chance?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kodin: «Stop talking, prodding, poking merchandise! You want buy, trade, sell, you talk to me, not droid.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «You threaten over droid? You flying on spice, juma, scath? No droid, no deal!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «[Failure] No deal! Try threat, try kill, Exchange kill you! Kodin deal with Exchange, deal with Kodin, and Exchange deal with you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «Try threat, try kill, Exchange kill you! Kodin deal with Exchange, deal with Kodin, and Exchange deal with you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «Ghastly. Seeing such a core... without the frame floods my receptors with some feeling approximating nausea. It is as if you were holding a human brain before me.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «I am but a simple maintenance-assistance droid. I fail to see what I could possibly offer you for your efforts and your subsequent extortion.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- TT-32: «Very well, take these parts from my motivators and vocabulator. It should provide you some amount of credits should you sell them.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «I am Vossk, once of the bounty hunter's guild, now no longer. You have nothing to fear from me.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «Ask your questions - I have time enough for that.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «You cannot. There is no base, no territory in which they stake their claim - they are scattered throughout Nar Shaddaa and the galaxy.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «Credits are the blood that beats in the heart of every bounty hunter. Make enough trouble, get a price on your head. Make it so high that the scent drives them mad, and no one can resist wanting to kill you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «Nothing is gained without risk. If you seek danger, be prepared for it to be at your throat.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «The easiest is the Exchange... no one else has the credits to pay enough to break the truce.  There is much danger in that... it is like baiting a Wookiee, you will succeed, but it is unlikely you will have all your limbs at the end.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Vossk: «Draw out the bounty hunters, let them collect the bounty, then you will find out.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «Much scrap, useful junk, litter moon. Kodin gather, patch, sell. You want parts, junk, droids?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «You make mistake, error, not smart. If you right in head, you trade, sell, exchange droid.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «No, you sell droid to Kodin, you not buy back! Deal done!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kodin: «You break deal, you deal with Exchange! You want droid? Talk to them!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «He is fine. What can I do for you?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «Yes, my name is Lupo Shar. And you are?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «Go out back and talk to Modo. He'll set you up.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «You would? Wonderful! Who would you like to bet on?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «Very well. How much would you like to bet?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «Let me just make you a betting slip...» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «What? Legal? Who do you think you are questioning me?!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «And I am sick of your off-color face. Leave now.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «Fair? You speak of fair when they are shutting down my account?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Lupo Shar: «[Failure] I never even took your credits! Get out!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Nodobo: «I knew you would have understanding. You not local and can see the City for what it is.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Nodobo: «Yes, offworlder. A droid's body can withstand forces much greater than yours or mine, so Lupo removed the inertial reducers. With less weight, the droid's swoop can go much faster and maneuver much quicker to avoid the fans' blasts.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Nodobo: «There are fans along the course that will blow you up, down, left, or right. You must compensate for them or else you will crash.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «A pleasure. Might I ask what you are doing here?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «And I don't remember asking you to join me.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- HK-47: «Statement: Our frames are designed to withstand considerably more trauma than you meatbags.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «Well as my father says: "You can shave a Wookiee several times, but you can only skin him once."» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «I was hoping you'd ask me that.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «The droid is kept in a cage in-between races so as not to damage itself. You see, Lupo is notoriously cheap, and did not spring for any software upgrades beyond the piloting program.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «So it is "us" now? I see you are smarter than you look, offworlder. I made a generous bid to Lupo for the gallery and he accepted. But only under the condition that his droid can still race.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «Not so fast. If Lupo sees that someone has tampered with the droid, he will get it fixed and change the cage's codes. We must destroy it on the first try. If you are not up to the task, you will render my codes useless.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «[Success (15)] You sound like you know what you're about. Here are the codes.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Borna Lys: «Find a way to cripple or even destroy Lupo's droid and the races will return to normal again. You can race fairly and I can buy up the gallery.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «You notice that there is an unusual looking module attached under the droid's left arm.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Success] You have successfully interfaced with the module.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- C9-T9 Interface Computer: «[Success] You have reprogrammed the droid to think it is in a race and about to lose. This activates the droid's self-destruct system.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Modo So: «Maybe someone could buy the gallery from you, Lupo.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «50 credits? You must take us for fools. That's not enough to spare him... or you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «That is enough to spare him. And you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Rutum: «Jedi Master? Are you crazy? There aren't any more Jedi running around, let alone Masters. But you're serious, aren't you? All right - well, look, if I were a Jedi, I'd be where I could help people. As for where that happens on Nar Shaddaa, I have no idea.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Rutum: «The bounty hunters don't have any one place where they stick around. Best thing I can tell you is if you want to find one, get a bounty on your head, and then wait.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Rutum: «Are you serious? All right, but I must be doing something right for you to come along and bail me out like you did.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Rutum: «I will, I promise. And I must be doing something right for you to come along and bail me out like you did.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Exchange Thug: «He owes us credits. Credits he says you have.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Rutum: «It's your life. Me, I'm laying low for a while, maybe they'll forget about me.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «Struggles and battles are the way of the universe, the pitch and yaw as empires fall and rise. And at the risk of seeming shallow, such struggles keep me in business. By your stance, I can tell you have seen many battles, and you are not ready to lay your weapons aside.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «:: It is Nar Shaddaa, the true Nar Shaddaa, that you feel around you. It is this moon, with the metal and machines stripped away and the currents of the Force laid bare. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «:: I am surprised you can feel it. I feared the damage to you had deadened you to such perceptions... What you feel is the echo of the minds of these creatures within the Force. Their anger... their greed... their desperation. It is life. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Kreia: «:: Have you learned nothing? Healing is manipulation, and if you do not realize it yet, then you will discover that an act of healing depends largely on your perspective. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «That schutta not-male Rodian woman sell cred-junk to you? She snatch my business, take it from me!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «Cannot offer good-cred, but shave credit off stock, make good-cred worth better-cred, make worth your time?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «You make promise, say you do this, then hunch-step away looking for exit ramp? No! You say you do this, make offer, I accept, no hunch-step now!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «Not-male Rodian female not gone! You do not-job!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «You deal with not-male Rodian female. Until you do, you receive no good-cred, better-cred deals with me. Go!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «So many problems beset Oondar, it is a wonder he has time to mention just one. I am indeed flattered by his attentions. Your business to me is more important, however. Whatever discount Oondar has offered, I shall match it, if you will permit me to remain and do business without incident.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «Make offer bigger? I give you better-cred weapons, better-cred armor already!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «I make better deal than better-cred weapons, better-cred armor. I not-tell Exchange you break deal with me, then they not-break you. What I say, you do! Get rid now of not-male Rodian female!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «You be scared soon. No merchant on moon do business with you when they hear you break deal with merchant who do business with Exchange. They not break you in pieces, they make you hungry with no best-cred equipment, no best-cred armor.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «That is not an offer I can match. I have given you the lowest discount I can. It seems you must make a choice. Both of us cannot remain in business here on the smuggler's moon. If either of us backs down, then we will not have the credibility to deal here again. The choice between us is this. Oondar can offer better weapons and armor than I. Such items shall give you a quick, deadly edge over your opponents. I am still building markets, connections. It is unlikely that I will ever have the connections Oondar has, but we will only see with time. So that is the choice before you. The quick, easy path that leads to what you want, now - or the longer road, where you may gain nothing at all.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «Such a huge crate lifted off my back! Breathing easier now, can narrow eyes on work, make profit. You get better-cred deals on weapons and armor.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «Very well. But I did warn you. There may be nothing down this path that you have chosen.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Oondar: «This I not-need! She even steals my thugs from me! I give up - she can keep this hole, I not-need it. As for you, you make Exchange not-happy, hope you live more than hours before they learn it.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «Let me know if you make progress towards establishing a new market.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «Simply keep it in mind is all I ask. Now, is there anything else I may help you with?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Geeda: «That is good news, indeed. I have heard there are many opportunities to be found there. I thank you for your intervention.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «No, I don't think so. You and me, we got some business to settle.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «So you admit it's not yours? How did you come by it, anyway? Who did you buy it from?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «We'll see about that. Watch yourself - Nar Shaddaa can be a rough place.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Atton: «What? It's our ship! I mean, your ship, that I fly.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «No hard feelings, I hope. Don't worry - you shouldn't have any trouble finding transport off Nar Shaddaa on your own.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Ratrin Vhek: «I'll be going now. Get used to the solid ground beneath you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- Exchange Thug: «Heh. You funny. Now pay toll. 50 credits.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Exchange Thug: «Yeah... you can go in.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Exchange Thug: «That's too bad. Guess we take what you do have.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Geriel: «I won't turn away your help... thank you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Geriel: «Thank you for trying...» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Handmaiden: «I admire your bravery.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «Why do you tell me this?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «[Success] Very well. I'll give the rats a little more space - if you will deal with the diseased one.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «I didn't ask you to cure him. I asked you to take care of him.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «Well, that may be, but watch yourself just the same. The Serroco crew fights first, and talk later. They won't bother warning you before shooting.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «All right. I don't want any trouble with you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «Oh, hello again. What can I do for you?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «You've got the Serroco refugees over on the skyward side. They're veterans from the Serroco campaign and don't take kind to strangers.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «Good luck to you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «And you haven't already? You weaken yourself by pretending that morality is important to you. You must learn to recognize your true self.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kreia: «Then you will come to realize its wisdom.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kreia: «Ah, you are learning quickly.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kreia: «It is a waste of time to reason with you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «If you could convince either the Exchange or the Serroco to give us more space, that would really help a lot.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «[Failure] It's amazing your species has survived this long.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Hussef: «How could you give Nadaa to the Exchange like that?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Disciple: «I know that you are right. But it is difficult for me to accept. I am still learning from your wisdom.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «You are kind to offer, but unless you can return my husband to me, there is little that you can do.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «Wh- Lootra's here?! Please, you must help me get to him!» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «'Deal with the Exchange?' You might as well wrestle with a planet. This branch of the Exchange is run by Saquesh, a Quarren with ties to Visquis himself.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «Visquis is the Exchange boss here on Nar Shaddaa, at least in this sector. You don't want to cross him - there's usually a battalion of hired guns lurking in the shadows behind him.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «Please... don't cause any trouble on my behalf. If you harm any of the Exchange here, they'll take it out on us a hundred-fold.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «Wh- how did you do this? I barely know you, and you've brought me word of my husband, and then a free path to him... it's almost too good to be true.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «You've given me my husband back, stranger. My husband - my life. Thank you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Nadaa: «[Failure] That would doom us both. There must be another way... could you please help us?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Nadaa: «You would? Oh, thank you! Please hurry. If he's already delivered her to the Hutts, it will be impossible to find her.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Nadaa: «Thank you! But... won't he track us down and kidnap her again?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Nadaa: «Thank you... thank you... Please hurry! After the Hutts have her, it will be impossible to find her.» [K2/Refugee_Quad] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Nadaa: «Oh, thank you! Please hurry!» [K2/Refugee_Quad] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Adana: «What, are you Saquesh's boss or something? I thought you smelled even worse than him.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Adana: «Whatever you say...» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Adana: «Then maybe you ought to keep a tighter leash on his tentacles, vac-brain. Because he's running this sector into the ground.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Adana: «Really? Oh, why didn't you say so? Oh, sorry about those things I said to you. You do look a little like a Kath hound, though.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Nadaa: «Thank you so much for returning my Adana to me! Thank you! But won't he track us down and kidnap her again?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «You are a most compliant human.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «What else do you want?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «Adana? Yes, the feisty one. Nadaa's daughter. She is not your concern.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «This is not news to me -- I am well aware of everything that is happening in the refugee quad. Still, the Exchange thanks you for reporting this. Gonlo! Take care of it.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «Why are you interested in this matter?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «I'm discussing the girl with one of the Hutts. I won't wait for your return to complete the deal.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «You are incorrect. The debt is 1000 credits.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «[Failure] You are welcome to try.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Adana: «Aww... I wanted to see you thrash him. Well, thanks!» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kahranna: «You're the one! Good. My family and I are anxious to leave, and I have the payment for you. 500 credits.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kahranna: «[Failure] No, the arrangement was for 500. You're not the spacer. Leave me alone, you Gamorrean.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kahranna: «Thank you! Here's the 500 credits. Please hurry back to us.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kahranna: «I hope not... but you may be right.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kahranna: «Thank you! To be honest, I don't even know his name. Some guy named Planore said he had a pilot friend who would take us for 500 credits.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kahranna: «Thank you... I'm beginning to lose hope that we'll ever get out of here.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «You are uncommonly wise for a human. To offer before asked saves us both... difficulty.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «The Exchange appreciates your cooperation. What else does the human want?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
