- Twi'lek: «Come back when you do. You will want to hear what we have to say.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Twi'lek: «Your human companion, the male one, we have seen him here before.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Odis: «Because if you need a crew, I'm your man. I flew during the Mandalorian Wars, and again during the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Odis: «I don't care if you think so. I know what I've done and who I am.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Odis: «You need a pilot's license to fly the freighters here. They're tough to get without sponsorship.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Odis: «Thanks for your help. I'm still looking, too.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Thug: «You've made a dangerous enemy today.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Thug: «That's a laugh - look, blow outta here like space-dust or you'll be a new stain on the cargo hold.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «We don't need your help. Now get out of here before I space you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «Do your worst.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «We can hold our own against them. Not that it's your concern.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «You're not going anywhere.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «Do you now? Suppose I were interested. What's this plan of yours?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «[Failure] That would be suicidal. Almost as suicidal as your making that absurd suggestion.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Serroco Leader: «Well, that's too bad. Is that all you wanted to say?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Mira: «You got it. It should be all set.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- HK-47: «Affirmation: As you wish, Master.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Airspeeder: «[This airspeeder could quickly transport you to other areas in Nar Shaddaa. It is fully assembled.]» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Bao-Dur: «After you assemble it, I'll double-check your work for you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kaul: «I can give you a tip on how you can get some good loot.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Kaul: «I've done a little scouting out of the Exchange camp. I think there's a killing to be made. They have containers filled with goods. With a stealth generator and some basic security skills, you could rob them without the guards even noticing.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «Is it humor? Such a human indulgence... and look where your filthy people are now.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «I am Saquesh, the Overseer of this sector, voice of the Exchange... you would do well to show some respect, human.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «And how would you know?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «You have already offered to convince [[../Refugee Quad#Sold to the Hutts» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «Excuse my misinterpretation. Your language is so... clumsy for communication.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «You ask too many questions, human. It does not concern you. Are there other matters for us to discuss or will you leave?» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Saquesh: «You misunderstand me. I do not loathe humans because they are humans. I loathe humans because they are weak.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Rescue]**
- Hangar Control: «The orbital drift charts are constantly being updated; if you download the charts now, they will quickly become obsolete. Download the charts just before takeoff.» [K2/Rescue]
- Hangar Control: «3C-FD appears to have been destroyed many days ago. Its motivator still holds a charge, however, and at the risk of destroying the containers next to it, could be overloaded by remote. If you had any mines, you could blast open the door without damaging the contents inside the security hold if you attached a mine directly to the door.» [K2/Rescue]
- Fuel Control Station: «This fuel line deposits fuel into docked starships. Since no ships are currently docked at the Peragus facility, it is sealed. There appears to be some sort of metal case lying inside the fuel pipe, but you can't make it out.» [K2/Rescue]

**[K2/Rescue_T3-M4]**
- HK Protocol Droid: «Condescending Retraction: I believe you will do your best, master. This facility would have to be nailed down with a droid-level precision to prevent your escape.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Placating Retraction: Oh, of course, master. Please excuse my choice of words. I did not mean to imply that you would have no choice in the matter.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Hesitant Answer: Ah. A T3 utility droid would be a common sight in this facility. It is indeed curious that I have not seen many since my arrival. However, I feel I must inform you that (droid predjudice aside), T3 models exhibit excessive individualism when not routinely memory wiped. This individualism can become such a nuisance that even a droid such as myself is tempted to reduce them to their base components - if not crush them into slag. But enough of my seemingly-irrelevant tangent. Where did you leave the droid, Master? That would logically be the best place to look.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Hesitant Explanation: That has been the subject of considerable discussion since our arrival here, master. Many have attempted to claim you and this unit as salvage. I was crudely interrogated concerning our brief history together on board the Harbinger... before its communications, weapons, and engines suffered the cascade failure that disabled the ship.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Speculation: It is possible you were incapacitated and locked in the well-shielded cargo compartment as the Harbinger was being systematically crippled, master.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Clarification: Yes, Master. No doubt the flurry of destruction on board the Harbinger somehow drugged you into a stupor from which you could not awaken. Most curious.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Placation: Merely a turn of phrase, Master. The implication that your state was due to the result of ingesting large quantities of juma juice was unintentional. I meant to communicate only that you were somehow rendered unconscious before you were locked securely in the cargo hold.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Recitation: Following the unusual set of coincidences that led to the cascade failure in the Harbinger's systems, we were boarded by a small freighter with unknown ID codes. It appeared that this freighter had been attacked, and the captain wanted to study it. This freighter appeared to still be spaceworthy. Your cargo compartment was breached, and you were taken on board the freighter shortly before the Harbinger's systems began to go critical. I, too, managed to board the freighter before the Harbinger's destruction. We were most fortunate to have survived, master.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Evaluation: Master, I do not know. Judging from the damage, it had been attacked by a much larger vessel. And when it attempted to escape the Harbinger with you on board, it was fired on again.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Clarification: By bounty, I refer to your life, Master. It would pain me to see you damaged in any way. That is why the arrival of this "Ebon Hawk" caused me considerable distress.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Answer: I do not know, Master. I found it curious as well. Perhaps it was due to a spectacular failing of your model and function.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Surprised Answer: Why, I told them, master. You are the exiled Jedi who served with Revan in the Mandalorian Wars, are you not? I hope all that has happened has not been the result of a miscommunication. If so, then the problem lies with the coreward databases, which are notoriously spotty.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Answer: All that has happened, has been because they believe you to be a Jedi, Master. They debated what to do with you as you lay unconscious in the medical bay. One group seemed intent on selling you as property. The other group opposed this.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Many miners began to join you in the medical bay as a cascade of flawlessly timed detonations occurred in isolated gas pockets in the lower levels of the facility. The explosions herded the miners into emergency sections of the station, quickly and efficiently cutting them off from communications and facility control... but sadly enough, not the ventilation systems.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «You see, the explosions had damaged specific sections of this facility's ventilation systems, causing a slow, lethal build up of toxic fumes in the dormitory level.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Admission: I cannot and will not attempt to change your mind, Master. I would urge you to consider that your kolto tank treatments may have caused some disorientation.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Answer: I was merely commenting on the idea itself, Master, not the execution of the idea. Though that, too, was inventive. Besides, Master, those miners intended to murder you. Or worse. Any complaints they would have at being murdered would be the highest form of hypocrisy.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Theory: You could walk across the surface of the asteroid to the dormitory airlock, but such a route would be extremely hazardous, and I do not wish to see you damaged.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Warning: Master, continued exploration of this facility may place you in unnecessary danger. I encourage you to return to the medical bay and wait for retrieval from a vessel that is no doubt on the way even as we continue this pointless conversation.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Weary Resignation: Very well, master. But there is very little that I can do. You see, the airlock is sealed by a code.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Correction: Oh, I already possess the code, Master, but I am afraid that it will do you no good.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Recitation: You need to open the airlock, and use the construction scaffolding on the exterior of the asteroid to make your way to the dormitory section. Of course, in order to accomplish that, you would need to unlock the maintenance console. And without the maintenance officer to speak the voiceprint protocol, that is impossible.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Placating Answer: Oh, of course, Master. I shall endeavor not to forget such descriptors in the future. If I do, I assure you, it is merely a result of memory damage I have suffered since our arrival.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Answer: Master, you cannot. You are trapped here, just as I am. There is nothing to do except patiently wait for whatever the future has in store for us.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «There's ways to beat voiceprints, though. If you can get samples of his voice and the right words, you should be able to fake the voiceprint code, if you know what it is. You know, I overheard the maintenance officer getting chewed out by the security officer. You might want to check the security office on this level and see if there's any voice samples stored in the logs. And maybe there's samples of the code in the maintenance officer's holo logs, if you can find a way to record them.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «That's a start - see if you can find any recordings where the maintenance officer is speaking, and try to piece together the words to unlock the voiceprint.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Irritated Statement: Master, if you insist on echoing everything I say, this already tedious conversation is in danger of becoming even longer.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Alarmed Objection: Oh, Master, no. I might inadvertently speak such a command near a console and accidentally unlock something I shouldn't. I was merely speaking such to prove to you that I could - it is a trait I am quite proud of.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- HK Protocol Droid: «Confused Query: I am sorry, Master. Were you speaking to yourself?» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «Yeah? Did you blow it up?» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «Yeah well, watch your back. Droids can't be trusted.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «There may be some emergency route to get to them - still, maybe we should leave them where they are and try to get to the hangar. If they wanted to sell you to the Exchange, we should make a clean getaway while we can.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «You mean like they did to you? You're pretty forgiving.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Maintenance Station: «Although the fuel lines are currently sealed because no ships are docked with the facility, they would open automatically if a ship did arrive. That would mean you would have to board the docked ship and go through the fuel line to reach the hangar, however, which would be an extreme means of gaining access.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Maintenance Station: «Strangely enough, this console has been voiceprinted. Not only does it require a code, but the code has to be spoken in the user's voice, most likely the voice of the maintenance officer. Until the voiceprint ID is given, you cannot give commands through the terminal, though you can search through the records, cameras, and perform checks on various systems.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Maintenance Station: «The droid maintenance area has been sealed by the emergency lockdown. However, new programming has been introduced to keep it from opening even if the lockdown is cancelled. There's no way to shut down the field that you can see from this terminal. This means anyone inside the facility is effectively cut off from the hangar bay.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- Atton: «Why don't you answer the terminal? Doesn't sound like they're going to leave us alone.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- TSF Officer: «Excuse me. You have a caller - Moza, representing the Ithorian planet-restoration interests on Telos. Lieutenant Grenn's cleared him, if you'd like to speak with him.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- TSF Officer: «He says he wishes to speak with you on behalf of Chodo Habat. That's all he will say.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Moza: «Are you familiar with the restoration project on Telos?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Moza: «The surface of Telos was destroyed during the Jedi Civil War a few years ago. This - Citadel Station - is part of the Republic's planetary restoration initiative. Citadel Station uses energy fields to seal off portions of the planet's surface, then generates and controls the weather patterns over each area. Once the weather in a zone is stabilized, new animal and plant life is introduced to restore the ecosystem. Recently, however, we have run into contention with the corporation known as "Czerka." Perhaps you have heard of our recent troubles?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Moza: «Chodo Habat is a powerful priest, our spiritual leader. He sensed something upon your arrival... a disturbance, an echo in the Force. Chodo felt you might be able to aid us. He bid me tell you that if you could help heal Telos, it may be possible for him to heal you.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Moza: «I am unclear as to what Chodo means by this. He says the echo he felt upon your arrival suggests that you yourself are damaged. He can feel the pain through the Force.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- B-4D4: «Good day. I am B-4D4, administrative assistant for Czerka Corporation's Citadel Station Branch. I am attempting to connect you with Executive Officer Jana Lorso. May I put you through?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- B-4D4: «I am sorry, but I do not know. I am only her administrative assistant. May I put you through?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- B-4D4: «I will relay such to Executive Lorso. I am sorry to have inconvenienced you. Good day.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- B-4D4: «Thank you. I will connect you now. Good day.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «I understand that you were approached by an Ithorian earlier. Doubtless he tried to obtain your help, attempting to purchase it with imposed guilt and veiled threats...» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Yes. They play at being downtrodden innocents, having come to you because of these "Jedi" rumors that are going around the station.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Oh, I'm sure you've heard. That you're a Jedi, one of the last, wanted by the Exchange and who knows how many other organizations for reasons good or ill. But that's of no interest to me. The Jedi and your standing with them have no bearing on this discussion.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Oh, and I suppose he didn't mention the Exchange? "Helping" you with any problems here? In other words, the Ithorians won't lead the Exchange to you if you work with them?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Well, no matter. My apologies - I never intended to waste your time discussing Ithorians.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «I believe you're a person of influence. Someone I'd like on my side, rather than aiding the Ithorians, whose quasi-mysticism and bumbling foolishness is standing in the way of progress and profit. I'm not asking for your help, though. I'm offering you a job. Work for Czerka, and be handsomely rewarded. You'd be helping yourself. If you're interested, please visit our offices here in Residential 082. B-4D4 will know what to do when you arrive.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «I'd rather discuss that in person. I'll be more than happy to answer any and all questions when you visit our offices.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Excellent! Good luck with that messy investigation, and I hope to see you shortly.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «The Sojourn is already en route; likely not more than a few standard days. Feel free to use these quarters during your stay.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «The vessel's I&D is complete. Please visit the TSF station in Entertainment Module 081 to complete the necessary paperwork at the front desk. The Ebon Hawk should be transferred from the impound docks by the time you're free to leave.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «After filling out the paperwork, it'll be transferred with your ship, along with your confiscated weapons and armor.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Hey, everyone needs to get lost once in a while. Get away from something, you know? It's no big deal.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Look, this place is a waste. If Telos wasn't doomed before Peragus blew, it is now - even more so if your 'friends' from Peragus come calling.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «It is difficult to say. I feel we came to Telos for a reason, but we may have spent too much time here already. Even if the Harbinger was destroyed at Peragus, more Sith could already be on their way. Still, there is a chance we might learn of other Jedi here, on the planet's surface. Jedi who might help us restore your abilities or sever the link between us.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Makes sense. I'll follow your lead.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «What? No, you must be mistaken. You're probably looking for the previous tenant or a neighbor.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «Dol sent you? Can't say I'm surprised, he was the one who wanted me to come forward in the first place.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «No, wait! You can't tell Czerka where I am. They'll kill me for sure.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «Fine, then. Here's 500 credits. Take them and forget you ever met me. And if Grenn really did send you, tell him thanks.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «Don't be too surprised if I'm not here when you get back.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «Easy to say that, but you're not the one Czerka will send its assassins after.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «You might be right. I don't think Czerka has gone so far that it's willing to openly clash with the TSF. But Czerka has eyes everywhere. I'm not even sure I can leave this apartment safely.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «You? Look, I'm sure you can handle yourself, but I think this might be a little out of your league.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «What would you know? It's more attractive than the alternative.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «You're right, of course. It's not easy to sit around in this apartment, afraid of anything and everything, jumping at shadows. But it sure would feel good to put a little fear into Czerka. See how Lorso likes a few sleepless nights of her own. All right. You've convinced me. Can you escort me safely to the TSF office?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «A Jedi? Jedi or no, Grenn wouldn't have sent you if he didn't think you could get the job done. All right, fine. But we need to get moving.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Finally. He's eluded Czerka's best agents, and I'm glad to hear of your success.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Yes, you will be going now.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Yes, of course, that's what I meant. Finish this business with him. If you could bring him here, I could discuss a method of payment with him.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Are you sure? I would be more than willing to compensate you for seeing this business through to the end.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «I could certainly use your assistance, if you are offering it.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «So this is how it ends... When Czerka kills this planet again, you'll remember this moment, believe me.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Mercenary: «Well, it's been a pleasure doing business with you. Executive Lorso asked me to tell you that your payment is waiting with her. I think it'd probably be a good idea for us to get out of here before the TSF starts asking questions.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Jana Lorso: «Hm. That was very proactive of you. In that case, let me thank you for taking care of that unpleasantness for me. I am in your debt.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Batono: «Then it's time for you to earn your keep and protect me.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Mercenary: «So you say. But I'm stubborn. So we're going to try just in case.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Information Terminal: «YOU ARE CURRENTLY LOCATED IN:<br>RESIDENTIAL MODULE 082 EAST<br>PLEASE SELECT A NEW DESTINATION:» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «Looked like you were doing more than just looking.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «Figured what? You'd just break into my apartment and take everything I owned? Are you crazy?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «Oh, and now I'm supposed to be scared of you, on top of everything else.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «Yes, I will. I'm not going to let you push me around in my own place.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «You again? Did you not learn your lesson the first time? Get out of here.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «Why not? You didn't seem to have a problem taking everything else.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Telosian: «What? You are crazy!» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Corrun Falt: «Is that a threat? I could have Czerka security here in seconds. You don't scare me.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Corrun Falt: «Stalling? I don't know what you mean. You're the one who's stalking me!» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Corrun Falt: «Wait! I'll help you! Just... just don't hurt me. All right. Other than Jana Lorso, only B-4D4 has free access to the mainframe. He's a protocol droid, her administrative assistant. If you controlled B-4D4, you could walk right in.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Corrun Falt: «Nearly all the Czerka technicians are planet-side these days, working the Recovery Zones we've taken from the Ithorians. We've contracted out the maintenance for the office's protocol and utility droids. The guy, Chano, lives in the apartments in Residential 082. Unit 2B, I think. If you had his credentials, B-4D4 would leave with you willingly.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Corrun Falt: «You won't be going anywhere, I think.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Harra: «You are half right in your guess. I did at one point have a gambling debt, but it was repaid, and there is no creditor that looms over me.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Harra: «I... I used Ramana - the girl of my heart - as collateral. I have a weakness for pazaak! You must understand... my hand was unbeatable. There was no way I could lose.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Harra: «You would do that for a stranger? Like I said, the amount is not small. Doton Het demands 2000 credits for her freedom.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Harra: «I would have little to give you. I would give you all the credits I have saved, but that is hardly enough. You would have my word I would repay you for your kindness.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Harra: «Truly? Then I wish you good luck, for no amount of pleading, begging or otherwise has worked for me.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Harra: «As I told you. Doton Het is a hard man to deal with. A thought occurs to me. Given the nature of his business, it is possible that he is working without the sanction of the Exchange. I understand that the Exchange's reach is considerable here on Citadel Station. Perhaps they would have some interest in Doton.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Ramana: «Thank you, you have given me a new life, and that I am thankful for.» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Harra: «What are you talking about? Aren't you going to set her free?» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Harra: «You can't be serious! Ramana, let's go.» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Doton Het: «Yes, yes! Let me go, you can have the girl, take her!» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Trandoshan Bounty Hunter: «Please extend my thanks to your employer. Because of the TSF's regulations, acquiring the tools of our trade has become rather difficult.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Trandoshan Bounty Hunter: «You are a new hand at this, aren't you? A suggestion, then - if there is something you are not told, you do not need to know it. Questions can be dangerous in our line of work. However, in this instance, I will tell you what our business with your employer is. The TSF's regulations as to the possession of weapons aboard the station make it impossible for anyone to arrive armed. Thus it is necessary for us to purchase weapons from a supplier on station. In this case, the Exchange.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Trandoshan Bounty Hunter: «Indeed. If you will hand the case to me, my associate will provide you with the credits agreed upon.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You would kill me for that? I'm sorry, but I do not have the credits, so I cannot pay you.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You... you would do this? It is many credits – 2500.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You see my problem?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «Do you have a solution?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «Thank you. Now I can finally leave this planet and start a new life. I am eternally in your thanks.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «No, you are right. Perhaps I am overreacting. I am just very nervous, to be going to Nar Shaddaa, where I incurred my debt. Hopefully I will find quick passage to another system.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «I cannot believe you would kill me for so small a thing.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You think I indebted myself to them by choice? I had no other options!» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «Then you cannot have them! They are my work, my life. Why do you even need them?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «It is no concern of yours.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «Eh... "I do not have any droid technician credentials to give you?"» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You... you would do this? So I could repay you later? It is many credits: 2500.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You do not have those credits to lend me, do you?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «Thank you. Now I can repay my debt, and you can take my droid technician credentials.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «I will. Thank you.» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Opo Chano: «No I do not have them yet, but I promise I will repay you when I have them.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «What?! You would kill me for them?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Opo Chano: «You are a terrible human. Here, take them. Go. Leave me to die.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «That was ill done. What possessed you of the need to have him killed?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «I can see that. However, had you left him alive, we might have had use of him in the future. It is far better to have such a person, even as pathetic a creature as Opo Chano, to take advantage of in the future. Only kill when there is no further advantage to be gained, and no disadvantage to yourself.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «I'm dead if I do open it, human. You're not getting in.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «If you don't have any business with the Exchange, move along.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «I still can't open the door for you.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «Yes, but I can't let you in without an appointment.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «You mean you'll try.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Vula: «That door can't be opened from there. Is there something I can do for you today?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Vula: «I'm terribly sorry, but I don't show you as having an appointment. If you need one, they can be made through Luxa.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «He's not expecting you, so you're not getting in.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «Sure you are, human. Now beat it.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «I hope you know what you're getting into, human.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Vula: «Luxa? Well, let me open the door for you, then.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «Nonetheless, you should heed my advice.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «Be that as it may, it would be best if you avoided such needless entanglements. You are too valuable to be caught up in the struggles of this planet.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «True, he may be able to help us locate the Ebon Hawk, but still, you must be vigilant.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Kreia: «I cannot force you to listen to reason, only hope that you will grow past these infantile delusions of right and wrong.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «Look, if you don't have an appointment, you're not getting in.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «Try Luxa, his second. You'll find her in the cantina in Entertainment 081, most likely.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Is it really worth it though? I don't know if we can trust Lorso, either. I mean, it takes something to ally yourself with the Exchange.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «That woman's a shark, but if you say so, I guess it'll be okay.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Well, if you say so, just watch your back.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- Information Terminal: «YOU ARE CURRENTLY LOCATED IN:<br>RESIDENTIAL MODULE 082 WEST<br>PLEASE SELECT A NEW DESTINATION:» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Greeter: «Ah, you are the Jedi Moza spoke of! I will open the doors for you now, and inform Chodo Habat of your arrival. He will be pleased to see you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I apologize if you took offense to that. The running of the restoration project consumes all of my time, and I sent Moza, my most trusted assistant, and hoped you would not feel slighted. I see that I was wrong, however, and for this, once again I humbly apologize.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Yes. I am a priest of my people, an adept in the Force. I sensed an echo within the Force upon your arrival... it is a subtle disturbance, unless one is actively listening for it. I suspected you were one of the remaining Jedi, and hoped that you could help us. This is why I sent Moza to seek you out.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I understand, and hope I have not offended you. Perhaps you might help us just the same.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «The echo I felt upon your arrival was not a natural thing. It spoke of a great pain, not of the body, but of the spirit. It is as if you once felt the Force flow through you, then it was torn away, brutally and quickly. The echo comes from the hollows where it once flowed through you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «It is a most unusual wound you carry. If you would permit me to examine you, perhaps I could help restore some of what was lost.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Tell me - do you know of the problems our restoration efforts face?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «At great expense, my herd has acquired a second, somewhat lesser droid intelligence to take its place. It will arrive at Citadel Station shortly. The Telosian government has offered a small escort, but I had hoped that you might see its safe transition from the docks to here. Might you aid us in this matter?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Telos' fate hangs in the balance. For your help, I will attempt to aid in your healing. Perhaps that which was lost can be restored.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I felt an echo within the Force upon your arrival. It was not a natural thing. It spoke of a great pain, not of scars, but of the spirit. It is as if you once felt the Force flow through you, then it was torn away, brutally and quickly. The echo comes from the hollows where it once flowed through you. It is a most unusual wound you carry. If you would permit me to examine you, perhaps I could help restore some of what was lost.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I had heard a ship was missing, hidden somewhere on Telos. Those of my herd are familiar with the planet - we could help search for it. Perhaps this would be payment enough for your services.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «My thanks. I will send word to my people. They will be awaiting your arrival at Bay Two, Dock Module 126.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Moza: «Forgive me, Chodo, but the human - I could feel his suffering. I did not feel it until he stood before us, but then it filled my senses. Have you ever felt such an intensity before?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Ah, you have returned.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «What you say is true, but up until now we have had little success in dealing with them.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I have tried to meet with the leader of the Exchange here, a Quarren called Loppak Slusk. He has refused my every invitation, and allows no one inside the Exchange suites. I fear he does not take us seriously. The Jedi, though, are renowned as warriors and diplomats both. Perhaps you might speak with Loppak, show him the value of a restored Telos.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «So you said. Again, I am sorry.» [K2/Residential_082_West] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Chodo Habat: «I am most grateful for your assistance. I will guide my thoughts towards your safety - and Loppak's, as well. I wish harm upon no one.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I sense that your meeting did not go peacefully. It saddens me greatly that blood was shed, it was not my intention to cause harm. Still, I am again in your debt. Until we find a more fitting way to commend you, please accept this humble gift. It came from a lightsaber that belonged to a Jedi once of my herd.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «That is most generous of you, {{s» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «For the Restoration Project to continue in earnest, the Telosian government must be made to see the cancer that Czerka has become. Their security division is merely an army of badge-bearing thugs. They supply weapons to the local black market. They steal Restoration Zones and land illegal salvage teams on Telos. But Czerka has imbedded itself within the government, using Telos' own laws to protect itself from censure or investigation. Legally, the Telosians can do nothing. If you can bring Czerka's corruption to light, perhaps the Republic could intervene and cast them out. This is what I would ask of you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Telos' fate hangs in the balance. For your help, we will aid you in return in whatever manner we can. I have heard that the ship you arrived on is missing, hidden somewhere on Telos. I know one who could help you find it, and a means by which you could travel to him. Perhaps this would be payment enough for your services.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Ah, you have returned. You have decided to aid us in our cause?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Bribery? No. I would not know who to contact. And I am worried. We have been taken advantage of many times of late. Can you guarantee the bribe will work?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «There is an employee of Czerka who may be willing to assist us in our cause. He spends his time in the cantina in the entertainment module. I'm sorry there is not more that I can do, but I hope for your success.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «...then it could access the Czerka mainframe manually, gather the files, and give them to us. Quite brilliant. Do not concern yourself with the reprogramming - my herd contains technicians who can perform the procedure once the droid is brought here.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «You are to enter the Czerka offices, access the mainframe, download those files which clearly indicate the extent of Czerka's corruption, and then return them here. Your programming still will not allow you to harm sentient beings, but we have allowed you the capability to lie to accomplish your mission.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «You do not? What do you not understand?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Protocol Droid: «I hadn't heard this. Thank you for informing me, B-4D4.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Opo Chano: «B-4D4? What repairs has Lorso sent you for this time?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Opo Chano: «I assure you they're all necessary, B-4D4.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Opo Chano: «You must be mistaken.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Ah, B-4D4. Have you acquired the files?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Good. You are free to go.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «He has been our ally in our struggle against Czerka. If you tell him that I sent you, he should be willing to help.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I will allow you to use one of our orbital shuttles. It has no hyperdrive, but will allow you to descend to the planet's surface and make a return trip to Citadel Station. I must point out that this is illegal. Landing on the planet's surface without permission is forbidden, and we no longer control that Restoration Zone. Please be as discreet as possible. The closest shuttle is in Bay Two of Dock Module 126. I will call ahead and have the ship prepared for you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Before you leave us, there is one last thing.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «An orbital freighter is waiting for you in Bay II, Dock Module 126. Bao-Dur should be in RZ-0031. He'll help you find this ship of yours.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Moza: «Many of my herdmates lie dead. They have threatened to kill Chodo, and I fear they will see it through. You must help us, we can turn only to the Jedi, the TSF is powerless. They are holding Chodo in his room, and have sealed the door behind them. You will need a passkey to open it. I will give you this passkey, but I am stuck in the plant room and I can hear the men hammering on the door. Please, you must help us!» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Greeter: «Moza was with the new plant specimens when he tried to contact you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Moza: «Thank goodness you've come! There is no time to lose, you must go to Chodo's aid. Take this passkey, it will give you access to his room.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Moza: «We have already provided you with a shuttle to reach the Restoration Zone as well as many credits. Our herd is poor, we have little more to give.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Moza: «Thank you, {{s» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Mercenary: «And now Chodo Habat, you will die along with your herd.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Indeed. May you have safe travels, until we meet again.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «You must understand, my herd does not have unlimited resources. Perhaps in the future, when Telos is restored, I can repay this new debt to you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «We owe you everything. Telos owes you everything. But there simply is nothing else we can give you, lest the Restoration Project fail for our generosity.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Consider it a debt to be repaid in better times, friend. I thank you for your understanding.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Until we meet again, may your travels be safe.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «It would not be the first time Lorso was mistaken. I assure you I have never known of a man named Batono to spend time in our company here.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «[Success] I believe what you say is true, and it gladdens me to hear that it is Lieutenant Grenn who is searching for him. I am sorry that I spoke untruth to you before, {{s» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Very good. You see, Batono came to us for protection, and we found him an apartment where he could hide. Our security technicians ensured that he would not be found by Czerka's security division. You will find him in apartment C1 in the eastern portion of the Residential Module. I will give you the passkey to enter. Give him my apologies for revealing his hiding place, and my explanations. I hope he will not be too angry.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Ithorian: «That would be the third one from the right. Feel free to look at it, but don't get too close to it - we have a security system that will trigger an alarm if you move too close to it.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Plant: «You take a sample of the plant.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Ithorian: «Why were you doing that?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Ithorian: «You will stay here while I summon a TSF officer to clear up this matter.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Ithorian: «I am asking you to stay here, so that I can clear this up with the TSF. Unless you have something you are not being honest about.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- TSF Officer: «Are you crazy? Step towards me and keep your hands where I can see them.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- TSF Officer: «Perhaps I shouldn't have made it seem like you had a choice. Now, surrender or face the consequences.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- TSF Officer: «I didn't want to have to do this, but you've left me no choice.» [K2/Residential_082_West]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- 2: «Who are you?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Atton: «Beats me. No one's supposed to be here but a Czerka research team. I can't say they'd be happy to see us, but shooting us down...? I can't imagine Czerka having us shot down by a bunch of scientists, either. You know, I caught a glimpse of what looked like an AD tower when we flew over the compound.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «Trust me, there's no way you're going to slice into the shield network. Just leave it to me.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Rodian: «And why would I? When we saw you, we could not believe our luck. If we could collect Goto's price on your head, we wouldn't have to worry about ourselves for quite a while. But we couldn't do anything, in our compromised position. We made plans while working here, but we couldn't imagine that you would deliver yourself into our hands.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Rodian: «Don't you know? The Exchange is paying handsomely for the head of a Jedi knight. Your friend here, just happens to be one. But that's of little importance. All that matters is that we take them back to Nar Shaddaa, and collect.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Kreia: «That is good. As a breeze may swiftly turn to a gale, you are slowly beginning to be reopened to the Force.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Colonel Tobin: «I'm afraid it is. Now if you will excuse me, we have one last barrier to take care of.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Riiken: «That's just great. Don't put yourself through too much trouble. I can catch up on some thinking I need to do.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Riiken: «You don't need to tell me about that. Thanks for coming. Vaklu's troops aren't very good hosts. If you could get me out of here, I'd be doubly thankful. Should be pretty easy to do.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «Yes... A reason... Th-there are so many to choose from. But you are a Jedi, you couldn't kill me...? I surrender. Have compassion for me.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «I take pride in my skill, and your constant avoiding my careful plans caused some measure of... distress.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «Of course not. It's obvious to me, at least, that General Vaklu is not going to win today. So survival is my first concern. I sense that causing you any more.. complications... would be counter-productive to that goal.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «But not now... right? I will take heart from your lesson. You are a wise Jedi.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «I... believe I am still connected. You can do what you like with the console. I am done. I will wait here for your troops to come. I will put up no resistance.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «Do what you will with the console. I surrender. Long live Queen Talia.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Riiken: «The slicer is still logged in to his account. You should be able to just get whatever you need.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Riiken: «Security terminal taken care of, I take it? With you on our side, I think we may have a chance in this war after all.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Kadron: «So you're the Jedi, eh? Well, the comm chatter we've intercepted is filled with reports about you. You fought right through their flank on the Sky Ramp? We desperately need someone like you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Kadron: «I'm Queen Talia's captain of the guard. You can call me Captain Kadron. But we've got little time for more than introductions.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Kadron: «The other security room is the furthest room down the south hall. You're probably going to have to fight a legion of Vaklu's troops and the flaming Sith. But from what I've seen, you're up to it.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Kadron: «Feel free to take anything from the armory lockers in the next room, if you haven't already. This is no time for niceties.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Kadron: «Talk to the corporal. He can take you quickly to the palace entrance when you're ready to go to the south hall. May the Force be with you!» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Kadron: «You have?! Oh, son of a murglak...! Corporal, use this code to open the outer door to the throne room.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Royalist Corporal: «Do you want me to guide you to the Palace entrance? We can leave immediately, if you like.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «You're too late, our pet beast is about to breach the force field to the Throne Room. The Queen will be dead in moments!» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «Your time is at an end, Talia. Your people have abandoned you. And now your life is forfeit.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Mandalore: «You did the right thing. Leaving Vaklu alive would be foolhardy.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «You're a fool. Your companion is right. Fine, send me to your best detention cell. I will be free within the week. And vengeance will be mine!» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Queen Talia: «I have thought about it. Either alternative is filled with evil, but I must think of my people first. Vaklu, you said you wanted a trial.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Queen Talia: «But he will rise again. He has too many supporters. I do appreciate your counsel, but he must be killed.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Queen Talia: «But the price if you are wrong...» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «The difficulties you surmounted to get here. You must have many questions. You deserve answers.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «I told the other Masters that our only chance to figure out what was happening to us was to find you - and try to understand what happened to you. I don't know how much you know, but this threat that's striking at the Jedi... it's attacking us through the Force. Vrook didn't believe me. But he was willing to travel to Dantooine, if only to help the settlers there... and perhaps protect what was left of the Jedi Enclave. Whatever, the reason, having us all drop out of sight I thought might make the enemy more bold - but then you happened. You came back, and you became a new target for whoever was attacking us.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «They were places touched by war. And we thought there was a chance you would return to these worlds, if only to try and make peace with what happened there during the war. But now the Sith have revealed themselves, that means the remaining Jedi will gather on Dantooine. From there, we can counterattack.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «We knew that someone was preying on us, hunting us. Finally the Jedi decided to take action, and called a secret conclave on Katarr to decide what must be done. Before the meeting could even begin, all the Jedi on the planet were killed, along with the Miralukas and all other life on the planet. Their deaths could be felt throughout the galaxy. So the Council decided that we must not present ourselves as a target again. The consequences to others was just too great, that we should use our resources to find who was responsible and deal with them. I think you're the only one who's made progress.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «You've had more success than I. General Vaklu's men and their allies effectively kept me pinned inside the Palace. I felt all along that Vaklu had contacted the Sith. But the identity and purpose of the Sith, I can't say.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Whatever I can tell you, I will.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «It's a long story - but there's a reason the Jedi have scattered across the galaxy - we've come to worlds such as this one. Worlds touched by war, or great tragedies, can be felt within the Force... strongly. We thought by traveling to such worlds, it would help conceal us from the eyes of the enemy. And they were places we thought we might find you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Scattered, searching for you. Going to places where they thought they might cross your path. You were our last hope.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Yes, at least, that's what I asked them to do. I believed you are the key to this whole war.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «All those lives during the Mandalorian Wars - and all those you served beside. Too much death leaves echoes in the Force; it is the price for having such connections. I suspect that is why you chose to accept the Council's judgment, to wander beyond the Rim. And why you traveled with no one, and did not stay in any place too long. I have thought of you since your trial, and there are times when I wonder if being connected to the Force is always the gift it is believed to be.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Cut you off from the Force? Why do you think the Council was responsible for that? No, I'm afraid the cause of your condition was as great a mystery to the Council as it is to you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «You always had deep connections to the Force. I am glad to see that it is once again your ally. When I first sparred with you during your training as a Padawan, I could tell that you were different. And it wasn't just your strong connection to the Force.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «So you heard what we said? These questions I cannot answer. This is something the Council must answer, not I. You must understand . This is hard for me, especially after all you've done. But it is necessary.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Did you know that when I was training you I considered making you my Padawan? I didn't. The demands of the Jedi Council were too great. But I considered you a friend. I even tried to convince you to become a Jedi Guardian. We could've used someone like you. So the decision that had to be made was not easy. But I cannot say anything more.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «That sounds like the bond that often forms between Master and apprentice. But to that degree... ? You always did form connections to others, strong ones, even when you were a student. But what you are describing is beyond me. I'm sure others in the Jedi Council would be able to assist you. If you could find them.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «There is something I would like to teach you. It is a technique which, I believe, will aid you in the trials ahead.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «There is one to the north and one to the south. I expect you will face heavy resistance both ways.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «Then we deal with Talia and her hand-picked soldiers. And you'll have to face Master Kavar. I admit... I used to doubt you could defeat him. But Master Kavar is no match for you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Colonel Tobin: «He's fending off the royalists that are trying to get to us. He's left command of this area to me. I will let him know when we are close to breaching the throne room. Good luck to you. We will keep the entrance secure.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «Indeed, this is so. I am worthless compared to you, Sith Master. Had I but known, I would gladly have joined you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «I always prided myself in being above politics. But after the... incident in the cantina... with Colonel Tobin and the deaths of so many patrons. I decided that Queen Talia may have been right. But I didn't mean to get in your way.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «This... is hopeless. I won't grovel any more. You are evil, your allies evil. I would rather die fighting you then... Just, die!» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «You... you are just evil.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kiph: «I thank you for my life, mighty Sith.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «I told the other Masters that our only chance to figure out what was happening to us was to find you, to try to understand what happened to you. What a fool I was.  And now you are no doubt seeking to destroy the Jedi. But the remaining Jedi will gather again very soon. This, at least, you cannot prevent.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Telos? But Telos was destroyed during the Jedi Civil War. I don't know what you mean.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Atris? But I thought she died with the rest on Katarr. You've met other Jedi - I can tell. But you won't find me an easy opponent.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «I will not give you the benefit of my wisdom, dark one.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «[Success] Very well. Perhaps my knowledge will sway you from your dark designs. I was one of the only Council members who believed in you, and part of me still does. So what do you have to say for yourself?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «I know that, all too well. I came here to find them, to trace them to their source. The war on Dxun and Onderon - I had thought that perhaps the tragedies that occurred here were concealing them. And now here you are.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «You always had deep connections to the Force.  But such connections can corrupt one's spirit, as has happened with you. When I first sparred with you during your training as a Padawan, I could tell that you were different. But I did not expect you to fall so far.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Do you really think I would tell you, even if I knew? If that's why you are here, then you've come here in vain.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «What do you expect me to say. You defied the Council. You followed Revan to war. I know why you did it, but in so doing, much more harm was done. All those lives during the Mandalorian Wars - and all those you served beside. Too much death leaves echoes in the Force; it is the price for having such connections. Apparently, your self-inflicted wounds would not heal. And now that they've festered, you come looking to the Jedi for vengeance, as if we were somehow responsible for the results of your actions.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Cut you off from the Force? Why do you think the Council was responsible for that? Your misplaced blame explains much. But the only one responsible for your present state is you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «So you heard what we said? These questions I cannot answer. This is something the Council must answer, not - not I. You must understand - this is hard for me. Especially after all you've done.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Did you know when I was training you that I considered making you my Padawan? I didn't... the demands of the Jedi Council were too great. But I considered you a friend. I even tried to convince you to become a Jedi Guardian. We could've used someone like you. So the decision that had to be made, was not easy. But I won't say anything more.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «That sounds like the bond that often forms between Master and apprentice. But to that degree... ? You always did form connections to others, strong ones, even when you were a student - but what you are describing is beyond me.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Kreia: «Note the strength of his Force powers. He is desperate to finish you quickly, so he uses a [[../Forms#Force Potency» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Damn you. You must die. I will not have you teach others the secrets of the Jedi.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «I will not forget this. Do what you will.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «Because I had faith in you.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Disciple: «Is that so? Well, I hope your actions contributed to Onderon's continued cooperation with the Republic.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Disciple: «Very well. I'm sure you are equally disinterested in talking about what happened within the Temple of Freedon Nadd.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «You have returned. Why?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You will find them in the main irrigation channel room in the northern part of the plateau interior. The particle emitters there that once governed the flow of water to Telos can double as force cages.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «It is not part of my duties. Free them yourself, then you must leave this place.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «We have some training in dealing with Jedi. You would have posed little threat.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «He has had some Echani training. He masks it well, but when you were in danger, his mask dropped into a stance we know well.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You are the one who is mistaken. It requires you know what to look for.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I do not know. The Echani forms are known to be taught to military special forces throughout the galaxy. If the source is a mystery to you, perhaps you should ask him. It would be wise to know those you travel with.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Your ship is stored in the hangar. Atris has given you permission to leave this place - and not to return.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «She is meditating, and she does not wish to be disturbed. Your presence here has troubled her, and I hope you do not choose to trouble her further.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «But were the choices you had dictated by your training? A curious question, difficult to answer.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «She predicted you would say as much - she seems to know you quite well. Perhaps you are the one who knows nothing.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «[Failure] It implies nothing. She lets you walk freely knowing that we will deal with you if you make any attempt to harm her or this place.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris speaks of you, but "Jedi" is not the term she uses. She says you followed Revan to war, the same Revan who took the Mandalorian flame and brought it to the Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Perhaps you believe that, but there is often little difference in one who wages war and one who follows another to war.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «[Success] There is truth in what you say. Yet many such artifacts are sealed away in Atris' chambers. But if you wish to see a few of them, speak to the last of the Handmaidens. She has seen some of these objects, and has an... interest in such things.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I will not speak of them... ask her yourself, if it matters to you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You are no Jedi, Atris has made that clear. When you went to war, you gave up the mantle of the Jedi and became something else.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I assume you meant no insult. It is forbidden for us to become Jedi, even if we possessed the awareness of the galaxy that the Jedi possess. We are here because we are not Jedi. That is our purpose.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Spoken like one who has fallen... if we had been there, you may never have fallen at all.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I would have struck you down as you entered this place if Atris had not stayed my hand. Though you are not a Jedi, you have been sickened in Revan's service. If for a moment I felt you could bring harm to Atris or this place, then I would end you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I do not expect a criminal to understand - you have existed outside the circle of the Jedi for too long.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «It dishonors me they would say such a thing to an outsider. But I cannot deny the truth in what they say. My thoughts are not always focused on training. Perhaps once having known the ways of the Jedi, you may understand what occupies my thoughts.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «You must understand. If we do not maintain our vigilance, then we risk a second tragedy, one greater than that of the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «[Failure] I will not. Atris has made her orders on the matter clear, and she says you are no Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I am aware of this. I do not doubt your combat prowess, as my half-sisters do. There are many who went to war against the Mandalorians, and few returned. You were one of them.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I did not mean to praise you with my words. Perhaps it is your interpretation of them that needs to be viewed in perspective.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «It is standing atop the summit of a great mountain, the winds tearing about you, then finding yourself buried alive - trapped, helpless, and alone. It is like having the energy of youth... then feeling the cloak of years fall upon you, and knowing you are weak, fragile, and a thing easily discarded. It is knowing what you want to say and never finding the words. It is a chorus, replaced with silence. Hearing teachings without meaning. It is like a beloved pupil to whom you have shared everything, sacrificed everything, and then having them turn from you... and forget all you were.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Oh, yes. We train extensively in various combat styles, and we have not had another target for some time. You may prove a pleasant diversion.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Very well. Follow me, and we shall see if you have the endurance to learn the most basic of our teachings.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «All duels between us shall be without armor of any kind. There shall be no restrictions upon our movements - or upon yours. Your feet are not to leave the training mat during the battle. If they do, you will lose. Also, this is not a fight to the death. Restrain your instincts when we fight, and we shall do the same.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «The fight will be with hands and feet only - no stimulants, shields, weapons, or other items. Also, do not call upon any Jedi techniques during our contest. If you do, then the battle will be over. In turn, I will not use our higher forms, for this is only an opening battle between us - a test of each other's strength.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Very well, I shall honor your request.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «There is nothing more that we can teach you from such contests. Such battle would only be for battle's sake.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «If that is what you wish, then we shall honor it. We shall see how you fight against a group of us. We shall take you in a pair, using everything at our disposal. Use whatever item, weapon, or Jedi technique you wish - we shall match it.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «One does not sweat in the polar regions of Telos... so all that will break will be your pride.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Then our last battle shall be the five of us against you. Use any item, any weapon, any technique you wish - you will need them.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «I... felt as much. Quite strongly, in fact. I suspected there was something from your past here - something unresolved.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Whatever her charm, or lack thereof, you must deal with it. Unresolved events from our past can create wounds in the present, and the future.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «There is a Jedi here, perhaps. In that, you are correct. Yet there are no students, and this woman... this Atris... surrounds herself with those who cannot feel the Force. Curious.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Ah... killing such a one could prove difficult. And unwise. Yet she... distracts you, that is clear. And that may be reason enough. Such distractions could prove fatal against the enemies we face.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Direct action is not always the best way. It is a far greater victory to make another see through your eyes than to close theirs forever. Yet all that lives feels. And where something feels, there is weakness.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Why you, of course.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Things went that well, huh? You make friends wherever you go, don't you?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Nah. I was just complaining. Heh. I'm with you until things start going better for you. We need to stick together, you know? And who knows... I might be able to help you out of a tight spot at some point.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Oh, that. Don't tell anyone, but you wouldn't believe how many fights you can prevent by just pretending to know that stuff. I mean, it doesn't compare to wearing a lightsaber, but then again, that doesn't seem to help you much.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Enhhhh... wh... Y... you damn Jedi are all the same... give you a little power, and it rots you all the way to the core. Stay out of my head! I mean it.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «What, you ready to continue interrogating me? I think I've got some childhood details you haven't tried to rip out of me yet.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Malak: «All life is a test. And sometimes the student can be a teacher, too. You know it even now.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «The Council seems content to watch, to debate, while entire systems fall to the Mandalorians. If we don't act now, there may be no Republic army to assist in the future. I sense you will join us. What are your reasons?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «You should trust in yourself - and in your instincts.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «You have learned much. You no longer need to obey their hollow teachings.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «So knowing all that would transpire, would you still follow Revan and I? Excellent.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «She didn't join us that day, but in time she came to our way of thinking. And even before then she wavered and wondered what would've happened.  It is a familiar path... there were those who wished to follow you to war, yet remained behind. They came to hate you for the choices they wished to make.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «More power than the Jedi can ever offer you.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «Are you so certain? Every step along the way we did what we thought was right. Perhaps the same path lays before you. But the time of words are done. Now it is time you experienced the full power of the dark side.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Republic Captain: «We already lost half the men just getting to the path. They've got the rest of the company pinned down by the crash site. You can't possibly ask the troops to go forward. If you ask us to charge, will it make a difference? Will our sacrifice mean something?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «You've revisited the dark moments of your past, and now you must face the present.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «Your confusion is natural. The others and I will help you understand.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «Atton, I've had enough of your snide contempt!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «I will be honest with you. I have fallen to the dark side. Does this change your decision?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «You of all people would judge me so? Am I not worthy of redemption?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «So you will do nothing? Apathy is death. Worse than death, because at least a rotting corpse feeds the beasts and insects.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «We have been linked together in this manner for a reason. The dangers you faced here should have illuminated your need for fellowship.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «The dangers you faced in this tomb were real, but these images of the past served to prepare you for your future.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «You overestimate the power of the tomb. Any change you feel is coming from within yourself. Instinctually, you know your true path. Trust in your feelings. They will lead you in conquering the many challenges that the future holds for you.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «Surely you have felt what awaits... events are shaping themselves about you, seeking to draw you into their center.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Kreia: «Search the room you are in. You should be able to unlock a passage that leads outside the tomb.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- Computer Panel: «LEVEL ONE TEST. MESSAGE FOLLOWS...<br><br>The Level One test contains 5 questions. At the end of the test you will be told how many questions you answered correctly. To proceed to level 2, you must correctly answer 4 questions. It is recommended that you study the learning material in the library before proceeding.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- Darth Sion: «But you do not know her as I do. You have not survived her teachings, as I have. And you have not bested her in battle, as I have. You are nothing. Yet still she walks with you, is willing to sacrifice herself for you!» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- Ancient Terminal: «There are six core modules (they labeled them A-F), and one is dangerously unstable and needs to be replaced. The Sith have run a diagnostic report with each module to indicate which other modules are fully operational. Four of the diagnostic reports have a single error on them. Two reports are completely accurate. The unstable module's diagnostic report is one of the two that produces accurate results. The reports are:<br>A: feb, B: dce, C: eba<br>D: fac, E: fdc, F: acd<br>Which module do you replace?» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Ancient Terminal: «The terminal powers up. You hear an unlocking sound from the container nearby.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Ancient Terminal: «The equation is:<br><nowiki>(6 * 2) _ 8 _ 9 _ 1 = 13</nowiki><br>What do you use for the second operator?» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Ancient Terminal: «The equation is:<br><nowiki>(6 * 2) - 8 _ 9 _ 1 = 13</nowiki><br>What do you use for the third operator?» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Ancient Terminal: «The equation is:<br><nowiki>(6 * 2) - 8 + 9 _ 1 = 13</nowiki><br>What do you use for the last operator?» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Ancient Terminal: «Equation is:<br><nowiki>(6 * 2) - 8 + 9 * 1 = 13</nowiki><br>Do you power up the terminal?» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Mira: «I won't tell you how to do your job. But I know that I wouldn't get near it.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Dark Side Energy: «The tugging sensation doesn't abate. Wild and dark emotions surge around you. You don't know if you can maintain your mental balance in this storm.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Dark Side Energy: «You maintain your serenity through the worst of the emotional storm. The dark side has diminished, but is still present. Though the strain of the effort has momentarily drained you, you feel a renewed strength that you can use to defeat the evil in this Tomb.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Dark Side Energy: «[Success] You maintain your serenity through the worst of the emotional storm. There is no temptation here for you any more. You have resisted the Dark Side.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Dark Side Energy: «The waves of anger consume you, growing larger and larger. You feel raw power permeating your very being. Comprehension dawns upon you. You have learned something deeper about the nature of the Force.» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Dark Side Energy: «You maintain your serenity through the worst of the emotional storm. The dark side has diminished, but is still present. You are drained by the effort. Though the strain of the effort has momentarily drained you, you feel a renewed strength that you can use to defeat the evil in this Tomb.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Ancient Terminal: «[The door to the inner tomb has already been unlocked. There is nothing more you can do with this terminal.]» [K2/Sith_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Sith Master: «An adept has managed to come so far? Incredible. You have within you the potential to wield the Force. I am not speaking of the ways of the Jedi or their flawed teachings - their Order was rooted in weakness.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Sith Master: «The Jedi teachings say that one should never use the Force in anger. To deprive themselves of such a weapon is absurd. The Force is there to be used. That is the truth of the dark side. Use your emotions, use your rage, and channel the Force. Use its power to break your chains and forge your own destiny.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Sith Master: «Just give in... let the walls around your emotions fall. Let vengeance guide your hand, let passion fuel your power. You feel it, yes?» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Sith Master: «Yes... you can see it, you can feel it around you. There are no limits to what you could achieve! Join us!» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Sith Master: «A shame... instead of freedom and power, you have chosen death. So be it.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]
- Xarga: «After you left, your squad...» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/Sky_Ramp]**
- Zuka: «Minor systems. Nothing you need concern yourself with.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- Mandalore: «You should've seen it when the sky was filled with them. Their fighters crackling like small novas.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «My mistake, I am not familiar with your... ways. I am a leader of common men during an uncommon time.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «I trust that Colonel Tobin has made adequate arrangements for you. I understand you've already sent your people to deal with the Sith on the moon.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «I know that we promised you the Sith and Jedi artifacts located in our Royal Museum. I thought that they would be priceless to you. Do you require anything else? I understand we've asked you to take tremendous risks, for a cause that isn't your own. I will make good on payment.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «That is part of the plan. A vital part. That isn't a matter of payment. So, is there something else you need?» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «The Jedi would twist them to the Republic's way of thinking. I can see your point.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «When Queen Talia is dead and the Royal Palace mine, I am willing to give you 10,000 credits.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «You can have... anything that Queen Talia has on her person. There are royal relics that may have some value to you. Or have some power for your kind. I only want things that are badges of the monarch.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «Now that we are done with the matter of your payment. We need to talk about what needs to be done.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]
- General Vaklu: «The Sith are very powerful, but it is apparent to me they rely on the Force too much. Their knowledge of warfare and tactics is weak. Their arrogance is their greatest weakness. We will severely diminish their strength by the time we get to the Palace. My troops should be able to hold them off until you arrive. Their army must be destroyed, otherwise they would forever be a threat to Onderon.» [K2/Sky_Ramp]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_East)]**
- TSF Officer: «All right. Everyone, provide covering fire for the Jedi, then advance on the Sith positions. We'll be right behind you. Good luck.» [K2/Suburban_(082_East)]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_West)]**
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Look, can't we leave the past behind us? We need to deal with the situation we have now, and whatever I might think about you, I'm going to set that aside. But now, we have a different problem.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Chodo Habat: «After you left the Station the first time, the situation of Telos became greatly improved. Eventually, the Telosian Council acceded to our complaints, and Czerka was ordered to cease their operations. From what I understand, they have relocated to other rebuilding planets in the Outer Rim. This is regrettable, and I hope that their influence does not spread far. Things looked so promising, and now it looks like our efforts may all be for naught.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Chodo Habat: «No, I will not leave this planet. I will stay here to the end. Moza, you should leave, however. If Telos is lost, another planet will need the help of our herd.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Chodo Habat: «Truly? But how can you hope to defeat a force so vast and powerful. I'm afraid it is too much for a few to accomplish. Perhaps it is the destiny for Telos to suffer, for it seems the Sith will not relent until it is destroyed.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Mandalore: «Don't worry about me, I can handle myself just fine. Look after your own health.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Major Riiken: «Major, actually. And I have you to thank. Queen Talia saw fit to promote me for my service.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Major Riiken: «In that case, if you find my men and they are in need of assistance, please help them.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Vaklu Leader: «All right, but remember. If they fail, you'd better hope you're not on the station. It's a long drop to the surface.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]

**[K2/Swoop_racing]**
