- Race Master: «With the recent influx of repulsorlift technology into the swoop racing circuit, our track features vertical challenges, demanding an even higher level of skill and precision from our pilots. Improvements in the construction of bikes - well, should you invest in it - have enabled us to introduce new types of obstacles, making the races even more exciting for the fans. And for the pilots, of course.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Race Master: «The track's all yours. There's a 20 credit fee and you can run as many times as you want.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Race Master: «Suit yourself, the track's not going anywhere.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Race Master: «[Success] Well, I suppose so. You seem like a pretty good racer, and if you put up some good times, it'll attract more interest in the races.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Race Master: «Oh yes, of course, your prize! Your reward for achieving the best time is 500 credits.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Race Master: «Your entry free is waived champion. Have fun on the track.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «Mark...et. Marketable face. You have a very unique face that will be easy to market to the fans.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «Welcome back, what can I do for you?» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «You can, but be forewarned: the Heavy Lifters are operating today. You must jump to avoid them as they move cargo.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «The track we use is actually an old sewer system that runs beneath the city: under the refugee camp, around the methane bar, and through the docks. Do you know about the other swoop bikes and the fan blasts?» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «That was a terrible accident! I am so sorry, but you must finish the race for your time to qualify.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «Very well. Are you ready?» [K2/Swoop_racing] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Modo So: «Oh yes... I almost forgot. Here you go, 500 credits! This is your split of the winnings.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Modo So: «[Success] I will give you all of the winnings.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Qimtiq: «You can rent a swoop bike to race on my track. A full field of competitors is good for everyone. 100 credits must be paid for an official run on the track. Payable at the beginning of the race, not after.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Qimtiq: «The first round is for those new to the circuit. A proving ground if you will. The second is for any who have proven themselves. The third round is the domain of the professionals, or very talented beginners. Only veterans of many years can reach that rank. There are some exceptions to that. There was someone during the Jedi Civil War who quickly made it to the third round in Manaan. But that was a fluke.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Qimtiq: «Galactic standard swoop racing is based on timed trials, a swoop biker trying to make the best time, one at a time. Swoop racing is about speed and reflexes. There are five gears, and switching optimally is very important. Just pay attention to your indicators to know when to shift. Reflexes are important in dodging the many obstacles on a track. And also running over swoop boosters that are on the track. Boosters will increase your speed dramatically for a limited time.» [K2/Swoop_racing]
- Qimtiq: «I am excited to see how you perform in the races. The entry fee is 100 credits, there are no exceptions.» [K2/Swoop_racing]

**[K2/T3-M4]**
- Bastila: «T3, you have been with us since Taris. Without you, we would never have escaped that place... and for that, I thank you. I'm leaving this message inside you because I have seen glimpses of the future... and the bond that he and I share does not allow him to hide everything from me. More of his memories have returned - and they trouble him. He has remembered something, something on the edge of the galaxy, and he believes that he must go there to end it. But I'm afraid for him... afraid that he may not return. I need you to be the beacon, T3. If he is lost out there, on the edge of the galaxy, if he finds whatever terrible thing he has seen, then he may not survive. If he doesn't make it back, then I need you to return to the Republic, find help. If you cannot find me, then seek out other Jedi, the Republic... I can't lose him, even if he believes he is protecting me.» [K2/T3-M4]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Then you shall fall.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Do you care so much for her that you have come all this way? Perhaps you have feelings after all.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «This is a weapon of a Jedi. You shall not have it.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «The old woman you traveled with, finally made me... listen to myself, to the galaxy. She said that you would come here, and that you would face me in battle.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «She said you were the last obstacle to my enlightenment. If I wished to truly face the Sith, to see their heart, then that meant facing you, this last time.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «I do not know - yet. I suspect she went in search of you. But just as when she first came here, her path is difficult to see. She has set many things in motion, it is she that ordered the extermination of all Jedi, so long ago. She will answer for her crimes, in time. She is Sith... just as you are, just as all who followed Revan were.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «All the knowledge of the Sith, gathered from across the galaxy. Brought here by my servants, so that I might uncover their secrets - and use them to track them down. But now they have been drawn from the shadows of the Outer Rim - and the only final matter to attend to is finishing you.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «I do not know... yet. But it does not matter. They have come here to face the Republic in battle, and they will be destroyed. I will not deceive you... I knew what could happen there, but it had to be done to make the Sith reveal themselves. But I did not know the extent of their power - and what that meant for the Jedi. I will not underestimate them again.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Yes, the Sith are here at last - you have brought them to this place, as I had foreseen. It has all been part of my plans for you. And when I defeat you and the forces you have brought to Telos, I shall take the battle to the heart of the Sith, and wipe them out – forever.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «These Sith are cowards, striking from the shadows to kill Jedi. I needed a target to draw them out - but I could not risk my own life, all that remained of the Jedi. So I arranged for you to return to the Republic, leaked information of your past, and then waited for the Sith to come. And they did. But you came to Telos, against my predictions. Now they are here, I can finally face this enemy and defeat them.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Surrender? To you? Never. Let us end this.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «I did not expect mercy from you... here, at the end. After all that has happened between us.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «If you will not kill me, then what will you do?» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «You are right. The Jedi were all that seemed left to me, and yet I had become so far removed from them that I betrayed them.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Yes. I had thought she was awaiting me at that place, but I see now that she lied. It was not meant for me... but for you. She has gone there. She is waiting for you to travel to Malachor V, to finish what you started.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Yes, you are an echo in the Force, a hollow space where it has been wounded. It takes a great act of destruction to create such emptiness, but it can be done. It creates places where the Force is difficult to hear, and difficult to find one's way. And you carry it with you, always.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Now she seeks to create another echo, a wound in the Force, greater than the one before - greater than the one you caused. It will deafen all touched by the Force, until no life is left. You were strong enough to withstand it once - but few have your strength in such matters, especially if they are unprepared.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «I do not know. But she needs you there. If you choose not to follow, she will murder herself at the heart of Malachor, and you will die along with her.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «She seeks the death of all Jedi, all Sith... and the death of the Force. It is madness, it is impossible - but she believes you are the key.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Because the Force had been stripped from her... as it had been torn from you. At times, I wonder what we would be if the Force was taken from us - if we would truly be Jedi or Sith... or simply human.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «You are important to her, somehow. But I...» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Because she has gone to Malachor. She is waiting for you there. But you will not survive Telos. Nothing will. It is because the greatest of her apprentices comes to Telos. And he will destroy everything, just as he destroyed Katarr and all the Jedi gathered there.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «And what will you do with me now? Abandon me here on this dead world - or end my life, as I wished to end yours?» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «It does not matter. Not now - it is what I did with such pain that caused the wound. But I thank you.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «No... do not do this. If you leave me to them, in this state... I...» [K2/Telos]
- Kreia: «You have done well. All is as I have foreseen.» [K2/Telos]
- Kreia: «I am not here... not in the flesh, not in your mind. These holocrons hold much of the Sith... and they hold much of my teachings, from long ago.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden: «Kreia... she said the council had ended you. And all along, she was one of those who had sought to kill us.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden: «When I heard her say that you were dead, I... I failed you. I let my emotion run through me, and I acted without thinking. I wanted to punish her... hurt her, see her answer for what she had done to the Jedi, for leading you to the council...» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]**
- Lieutenant Grenn: «You haven't been formally charged, but you will be placed under house arrest pending the results of our investigation.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «You are the only witnesses of the mining facility's destruction. Thus it is necessary for us to keep you under surveillance until we have a better idea of what happened.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Because this vessel and its passengers are connected with the destruction of a fuel mining facility - one that this station depends upon. We would like to find out the extent of your involvement.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Look around you. Even if you managed to overcome all of us, Bay Control could simply open the magcon field and shoot you into space.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Good. My men will relieve you of any arms and armor. Please follow me.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «As I said, this is only temporary while other arrangements are made. I'm sorry, but you'll just have to bear with us until then.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I've many matters to tend to. Your questions will have to wait.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Batu Rem: «Jedi or no, you're the one I am pledged to kill - there is no mistaking that.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Batu Rem: «The security cameras have mysteriously shorted out. There will be no witnesses to your escape attempt, during which I'll have been forced to kill you. By the time the TSF realize I'm not one of them, I will be far from this place.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «That's something we're going to have to look into. I can tell you that it can't have been easy.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «We've arranged for an apartment in Residential Module 082. You'll stay there under house arrest until our investigation of the Peragus matter is complete. You'll be under TSF protection. I'll personally clear any visitors to your quarters, and we'll investigate this incident to the best of our ability.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Officer, get Lieutenant Yima a report of this incident. She'll look into this. The rest of you, come with me. We'll escort you to the apartment in 082 immediately.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «I can't say. We have a ship examining what's left of the Peragus facility now, so your stay might be brief. We'll keep you informed.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- Atton: «What do you think the TSF is going to find at Peragus? That could bring the S- You know what, forget it. As long as we're trapped here, it doesn't matter.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]**
- Darth Sion: «You seek to erode my will. You will not succeed.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Darth Sion: «It is not mercy. What awaits you will weaken you. She will break you, as she did me, and you will no longer know yourself.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «You and her are alike... yet different, in all the ways that matter. And I hate you as I hate her. I hate you because you crawl within my head as she does, but your presence holds no thoughts, no teachings, you are just... there, unspoken. I hate you because you are beautiful to me. And in that weakness lies death. Perhaps in that weakness is the death of my master, as well.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «What it has always been. Her weakness is you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «You are strong... I cannot see as she does, but I know, that in time, you shall surpass her power. But not here, not in this place.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «She awaits on the Trayus Core in the heart of this planet... but you will never reach her.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «I cannot. If you pass, you shall not return as you are now. Return to Malachor - or go through me. There is no middle ground.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «It does not matter. The Lord Revan had the galaxy at his feet, could have made it strong, and he surrendered it to weakness. Like you, Revan touched power and turned away. The chance to heal this fractured galaxy, and he turned away. I was there when the Academy on Korriban fell... and the Sith turned on one another, like beasts. Our chance at an empire... destroyed by Revan.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «She awaits on the Trayus Core - the heart of this planet. But you will never reach her.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «You know nothing of her teachings, nothing of pain. You saw Malachor V and turned away. You turned away from power. She is a fool to see greatness in that.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «Then it shall be proof that you are not the one she seeks... and she shall accept my loyalty again.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «She sought you out because both of you were wounded by the Force - and because she had no choice.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «If you will not leave this place, then I cannot allow you to pass. If you go before her, you will be broken. If killing you will spare you what lies ahead, then kill you I must...» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «[Failure] No. Even if she believes you the stronger, then I shall prove her wrong.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «I... I still have the power to stop you. The Force runs strong through the screaming canyons of Malachor.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «Why... why did she choose you? What makes you able to defeat me, defeat me here?» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «It is not possible to walk away from such things unscarred. To keep living when the universe dies around you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «I have thought of this moment, more than you know. And I wondered, if here, at this ending between us, if you would care enough to try to save me - if a Jedi could find it within themselves to spare one who has fallen so far. I wanted you to say those words - for that I am grateful. But I do not want your mercy. I want you to break.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «You no doubt have many questions. I would be a poor teacher if I did not give you the answers you seek here, now.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «I never destroyed Atris - she had destroyed herself. I merely stripped away the illusion, and brought her truth. Her teachings could not be allowed to continue. And like Malachor, she was part of your past, unresolved. She needed to be something you could confront - and defeat, one last time. It was part of your training. Part of what was needed to make you complete. And there must always be a Darth Traya. The galaxy needs its betrayers, especially in the times to come. She loved you, you know, as one loves a champion. You were all that she could not be.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Perhaps you were expecting some surprise, for me to reveal a secret that had eluded you, something that would change your perspective of events, shatter you to your core. There is no great revelation, no great secret. There is only you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «The apprentice must kill the Master - if you do not, I will kill you. If I do not, then all you have achieved will be as nothing, as empty, and as violent as Malachor itself.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Then you will break. And then, my apprentice, you shall die.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «More talk of machines and threats. If you would end Malachor, then do it. But it will not be a victory for you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Yes, always. From the moment you awoke, I have used you. I have used you so that you might become strong, stronger than I. And I used you to make those who wounded me reveal themselves, so they could be killed by the Republic.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «I used you to keep the Lords of the Sith from condemning the galaxy to death with their power unchecked. I used you to lure them to Telos, where they could be, at last, fought and killed. I used you to reveal Atris' corruption, so that her teachings could be ended before they began. I used you to gather the Jedi so they could be destroyed. And I used you to make those who wounded me reveal themselves, so they could be killed by the Republic.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «If you do not kill me, I shall end you. Strike me down, end this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «You will not show me mercy. I will see you break before you do.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Save me? You already have - it is enough what you have done, from now into the future.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Very well. There is nothing holding you here, not any longer.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Ah... yes. There is... truth in what you say.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «You travel with them for so long, yet you do not know them still. Feel them through the Force, feel what they feel, hear their thoughts and know them, as I fought to know you. They were the Lost Jedi, you know. The true Jedi, upon which the future will be built. They simply needed a leader, and a teacher.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Many battles does that one have left in him... as Revan intended. A general needs an army, as he needs those he trusts. And Canderous is a loyal beast, no matter how much he is broken upon Revan's will. But you know this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «The blinded one shall return to her homeworld, and as she looked upon you, she shall look upon the surface of that world, and perhaps at last see what she was meant to see. After that, I do not know. I do know that you must leave her behind. Where you are destined, you must not take anyone you love. It was Revan's choice as well.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Salvation is a relative thing... but as you understand it, yes.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «She cannot help but love you. It is a curious influence you have over her. If she leaves this place, she will leave battle behind her. She will take Atris' role as historian, and she will teach others of the Jedi who was not a Jedi... and who trained her to feel the Force. After that, I do not know. I do know that you must leave her behind. Where you are destined, you must not take anyone you love. It was Revan's choice as well.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «There is no love left in a heart such as that one. But he would die for you, yes. I do not believe one would die lightly for one one did not care about.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «I would have killed the galaxy to preserve you. I would have let the galaxy die. You are more precious than you know; what you have taught yourself cannot be allowed to die.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Their paths are unknown to me. Even the small one - who waits for you outside this place - I sense it has one last journey for you. You must go where Revan did, into the Unknown Regions, where the Sith, the true Sith, wait in the dark for the great war that comes.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «It is because he remembered what lay buried here - this place, its teachings. It paved the way to Korriban, you know, the remnants here.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Have we? You thought that the corrupted remnants of the Republic, the machines spawned by technology that Revan led into battle were the Sith? You are wrong. The Sith is a belief. And its empire, the true Sith Empire, rules elsewhere.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Because I did not know where he had gone. If he had asked... would I have gone? I do not know. But he will need warriors, Sith and Jedi, any who can be sent after him into the depths of space, any who know the way. Perhaps you shall go there with him, and do battle at the end of all things.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «Dantooine shall survive. The community you saved shall be the foundation upon which Dantooine shall be habitable again. They shall drive back the raiders, the Mandalorians, and all that strike at the Outer Rim. The Republic shall again establish their presence there, and shield it with its forces... And Dantooine shall heal, be safe, and its skies free. The ruins of the academy shall remain.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

**[K2/Underground_Base]**
- Czerka Salvage Crew: «I'm part of the first Czerka salvage team. I hid here when the turrets and droids went on-line. I haven't been able to reach anyone. You... you are here to get us out of here, yes?» [K2/Underground_Base]
- Czerka Salvage Crew: «I've got some decent salvage. You could have that.» [K2/Underground_Base]
- Czerka Salvage Crew: «You're insane! Get away from me!» [K2/Underground_Base]
- Czerka Salvage Crew: «You can't just leave me!» [K2/Underground_Base]
- Damaged HK Protocol Droid: «Patrol mode engaged. Once you end this session the droid will follow you.» [K2/Underground_Base]
- Telos Orbital Shuttle: «[This old orbital shuttle doesn't have a hyperdrive, but it can get you out of the restoration zone.]» [K2/Underground_Base]

**[K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]**
- Kreia: «That crystal is bonded to you. Through you it acquires its character and strength. And through it, your power is enhanced.» [K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]

**[K2/Upgrade_items]**
- Workbench: «All skills except PERSUADE affect what types of items can be created. High skill levels allow access to more powerful upgrades. Which skill would you like to learn more about?» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Workbench: «The item creation screen looks similar to the inventory screen. On the left are the items of the selected category that can be created. To the right, your character's skill level in the relevant skill is shown, along with a description of the item. Also on this screen are the number of components you possess and how many components the selected upgrade requires to create. You can gain more components by BREAKING DOWN useless items in your inventory. Upgrades that you cannot create yet appear on the list in grey. Note that upgrades that require skill levels far above yours will not appear at all. Increase your skills to gain access to more powerful items.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Workbench: «You can BREAKDOWN an item to acquire COMPONENTS, which can then be used to CREATE other, different items.  Use it to recycle items you don't want or need in the field. How many COMPONENTS you get from BREAKING DOWN an item depends upon the item's complexity and value, and your Repair skill.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Lab Station: «Health related items include medpacs and some implants. Creating these items depends upon your Treat Injury skill.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Lab Station: «Stimulants provide temporary boost to attributes and are best used right before a difficult battle. Creating stimulants depends upon your Treat Injury skill.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Lab Station: «Mines detonate when enemies walk near them. They are an excellent way to wear down a superior foe, especially when used in conjunction with Stealth. Your Demolitions skill affects what mines you can create.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Lab Station: «Grenades can have a variety of effects and are most effective at hindering clusters of enemies. The types of grenades you can create depends upon your Demolitions skill.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Lab Station: «The item creation screen looks similar to the inventory screen. On the left are the items of the selected category that can be created. To the right, your character's skill level in the relevant skill is shown, along with a description of the item. Also on this screen are the number of chemicals you possess and how many chemicals the selected upgrade requires to create. You can gain more chemicals by BREAKING DOWN useless items in your inventory. Items that you cannot create yet appear on the list in grey. Note that items that require skill levels far above yours will not appear at all. Increase your skills to gain access to more powerful items.» [K2/Upgrade_items]
- Lab Station: «You can BREAKDOWN an item to acquire CHEMICALS, which can then be used to CREATE other, different items.  Use it to recycle items you don't want or need. How many CHEMICALS you get from BREAKING DOWN an item depends upon the item's complexity and value, and your Treat Injury skill.» [K2/Upgrade_items]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Kreia: «Hmph! Hssiss are enough to humble even an arrogant one such as you. Test yourself if you must, but try not to die.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Kreia: «Ah, the arrogance and ignorance of youth. There will come a day when you'll wish you'd learned from mistakes in the past.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Kreia: «If you were to face an ancient Sith Lord in combat, you would learn that we are as children playing with toys compared to the prowess of the old Masters.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Kreia: «More recently, this tomb was where Revan confronted Uthar Wynn, the leader of the Sith Academy. When Revan left Korriban, the Sith Academy was thrown into turmoil - with their leader gone, many fought for the right to rule. And so the Sith here turned on each other, resulting in the carnage you can see covering the surface of this valley.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K2/Visas]**
- Visas: «My lightsaber... you have destroyed it. I yield... master. It is as I heard through the Force. My life... for yours.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «You *must.* The alternative is only another death... and I would rather die by your hands.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I will do as you ask... but I fear the answers you seek of me... would be useless.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «*Kill* me, I beg you. I... it was not my wish to challenge you... and I *must* pay the price.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I... have nothing to offer you. Your strength is superior... it is as I felt.» [K2/Visas]
- Atton: «Yeah, they're a pretty secretive race. I heard that some of their kind become Jedi, but a Sith? That's... well, that's a new one. I'm not sure how you'd go about killing one. It'd be tricky.» [K2/Visas]
- Atton: «Just mentioning it - she looks like she's suffered enough wounds already... even after the beating you gave her.» [K2/Visas]
- Atton: «They claim to see on a higher plane than we do, you know, the whole Force thing. Makes me nervous.» [K2/Visas]
- Disciple: «I did not believe you would. I am more concerned about what she will do when she recovers.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I am able to serve. If we enter battle, I will fight and die alongside you.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I... I have not heard that question in some time. My flesh is... healed, if that's the answer you seek.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I know. And I fear that others will see the mercy in your actions... and in my survival... and use it as a weapon to do you greater harm.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I... felt you, heard you, through the Force. It was like a sound, at the edge of hearing. And when I heard it, I found I could not ignore it.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I serve my Master. I am an emissary, a scout. My Master was aware of a disturbance in the Force, but was unaware of its nature, of you. The disturbance is not something one feels from a living thing. There is little my Master does not know, and that you eluded his sight for so long... is significant, but I do not know why.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «You cannot. His vessel roams the borders of known space, and even I do not know where he travels.... until he... calls for me. Even if I could lead you to my Master, I cannot permit you to find him... until you are ready.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «If I bring you before my Master, untested, without your potential realized, then you will be lost to me. And I cannot allow that to happen. It would be as if one brought fire to a paradise valley, shattered a cavern of rare crystal... or blinded a painter.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I cannot - I will not. I would die first, and gladly, to preserve you, untouched, unharmed. Now that I have found you, I cannot sacrifice what I have found.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «It is a choice that can be made by neither one of us. Do not be so quick to meet that which you do not understand. Use the time you have now, to grow, to train, and to strengthen yourself.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «You will meet my master. It is inevitable, I have... seen it. And when you stand before him, and realize what you face, you must be prepared. Until then, I must protect you, help you, until you are ready.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «There is a... a greatness in you, a greatness that does not stem from the Force. It stems from who you are. And if my Master does not understand you, cannot see you, then perhaps there is hope for us all. But if you seek to survive, then you must understand why this is so.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «Forgive me, but before you go, I must ask. Why do you do this? Why do you seek to help me, teach me?» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «You must not do this. I cannot allow you to weaken yourself for me.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «So you say, but it is not something I have observed - or seen.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «Very well. Perhaps we shall speak more of this at another time - but know this - I cannot allow you to weaken yourself for me.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I will answer what I can, but my answers may prove useless to you.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I am not familiar with the place you speak of - my Master has not entered Republic space for some time. But there are others, who may move more freely, who may have been responsible for such an act of destruction.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «My Master did not cause the end of the planet you speak of.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I am not certain I did. I was there when the planet died. To see everything around you extinguished... it... was as if I was blinded. It was as if the Force had... been bled from the world.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «It was not a thing done with machines or weapons. The Force is far more terrible, and it touches more lives than any machine can hope to slay. For every one that feels the Force, strongly, deeply, each one feels and perceives it in their own way. You have strengths, whether you know it or not. And my master has his. His power is great, and it comes from hunger. He is a wound in the Force, more presence than flesh, and in his wake, life dies... sacrificing itself to his hunger. And those who feel the Force strongly are beacons to his hunger. My people, my planet, would have been attacked in time, it was inevitable, yet we could do nothing about it.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «Is there something else you wished?» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «My sight has been... damaged. What I have taught you - it is not the full extent of the perceptions of my people.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «[Influence: Failure] My master - he has crippled me. I have seen the galaxy as it truly is, and it has left its mark. It is not something I can explain, for the words are difficult. I only ask that you understand.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «Only when one suffers do certain truths become evident - both of the galaxy, and of the self. And I feel you are an example of this.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «[Influence: Success] If you wish to know, perhaps it is possible to show another what my people see... what I see.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «It will take effort to maintain such sight, but you now have that power. And with it, you can use it to see life around you in a different way... as I used to see it.» [K2/Visas]

**[K2/Western_Square]**
- G0-T0: «Your initiative is commendable. This situation presents an economic advantage to us. Will you allow me to assist?» [K2/Western_Square]
- 1B-8D: «Market conditions all set to 0. All prices now set to base cost value. Do you have any interest in my goods?» [K2/Western_Square]
- 1B-8D: «1B-8D sells droid components and droids. Currently there are 0 droids in stock and a moderate selection of components. Do you have any interest in my goods?» [K2/Western_Square]
- 1B-8D: «Certainly. 1B-8D is programmed to answer many basic questions from prospective buyers. What do you want to know?» [K2/Western_Square]
- 1B-8D: «That information is restricted. Do you have any interest in my goods?» [K2/Western_Square]
- 1B-8D: «Sorry, my memory banks have no information on that subject. Please restate your question or ask another.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Thug: «If you talk to him, tell him I got his twenty credits.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Twi'lek Bounty Hunter: «I'd take you alive, but I doubt I could keep a Jedi from escaping. Dead will have to do.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Twi'lek Bounty Hunter: «The Exchange has quite a bounty on you. Your head's worth many credits... as long as it's recognizable.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Captain Riiken: «What else do you want to know about?» [K2/Western_Square]
- Captain Riiken: «Captain Sullio. She was in charge of the Starport checkpoint. A good soldier. Your friend was one of the last people that saw her alive. So far he's just a suspect. But if he did it... the punishment is death.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Captain Riiken: «I know Dhagon personally. He's one of the worst doctors I've ever heard of. But I can't see him deliberately killing someone. He was one of the last people seen with Captain Sullio in the cantina. Ask around there. I've heard he's got a good motive for killing her. If you can somehow clear that up and prove it wasn't him, command will cut him loose. If I say any more I'll get in trouble.» [K2/Western_Square]
- Anda: «Why would you think that? Does it matter? Her next in command, Gelesi, is just as loyal to the Queen unfortunately.» [K2/Western_Square]

---

## Kreia / Darth Traya
*Corpus coverage: 1014 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 35 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 53 pronoun references resolved from context · 126 lines addressed to them · 78 narration lines · 71 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Kreia was a Jedi teacher. Revan was her Padawan: «At one time, Revan was my Padawan.» [K2/Kreia]. Kavar, in the trial vision: «Revan's teacher intended no harm.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]. She was exiled from the Order for her teachings: her students all «went to fight the Mandalorians, who fell to the dark side» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
She went to the Trayus Academy on Malachor as Darth Traya, Lord of Betrayal, alongside Sion and Nihilus. They cast her out: «I was cast down. Stripped of my power. Exiled.» [K2/Kreia]. On the Harbinger, Sion cuts off her hand. She hides the Exile on Peragus and rebuilds the Exile's connection to the Force.
### Personality and motivations
A manipulator who hates the Force's "will": «Because I hate the Force.» [K2/Trayus_Core]. She teaches self-reliance and the consequences of charity [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]. She wants to use the Exile's wound to kill the Force. Atris: «She seeks the death of all Jedi, all Sith... and the death of the Force.» [K2/Telos]. She claims she used the Exile as a weapon and a test: «I used you to lure them to Telos» [K2/Trayus_Core].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Harbinger:** loses a hand to Sion.
- **Peragus:** telepathic guidance.
- **Telos:** coaching. Threatens Atton on the Polar Plateau.
- **Nar Shaddaa:** frees Hanharr to hunt Mira [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels].
- **Dxun:** lecture to Mandalore.
- **Korriban:** telepathic commentary. The Secret Tomb argument with Atton.
- **Rebuilt Enclave:** pushes back and drains the Council; leaves the Exile.
- **Telos:** declares herself Traya [K2/Telos].
- **Malachor:** commands Sion to respect the Exile [K2/Trayus_Academy]. On entering the core, assassins die as she passes (narration). Final duel with three floating lightsabers. Gives prophecies for each companion, then dies.
### Relationships
- **Revan:** student.
- **Exile:** student and bond.
- **Sion and Nihilus:** apprentices and betrayers.
- **Atris:** tool.
- **Atton:** distrust.
- **Handmaiden:** manipulated.
- **Hanharr:** controlled.
### Abilities (lore)
- Telepathy and the bond.
- Force drain at range (the Council).
- Concealment from Nihilus and Sion.
- Lore knowledge.
- Telekinetic multi-saber combat (final boss).
- Mind control (Hanharr; the threat to Atton).
### Feats
- Trained Revan.
- Survives Sion's attack.
- Drains three Masters [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
- Assassins die as she approaches [K2/Trayus_Core narration].
- Orchestrates the whole plot: «I used you to gather the Jedi so they could be destroyed.» [K2/Trayus_Core].
### Power-scaling statements (key)
- «There are techniques within the Force against which there is no defense.» [K2/Kreia]
- Sion: «She... cannot be defeated.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Atris on Nihilus: «the greatest of her apprentices» (first pass) [K2/Telos]
### Duels and fights
| Fight | Page | Outcome and context |
|---|---|---|
| vs Sion (Harbinger) | Harbinger | Loses a hand. |
| vs the Masters | K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave | Wins. |
| vs the Exile | K2/Trayus_Core | Loses and dies. |
### Verdict implications
- Tier 5 near Nihilus.
- Nihilus > Traya at low confidence: he and Sion cast her down, but that was a two-on-one betrayal.
- Exile > Traya at medium-high confidence.
- **Counterargument:** her duel loss was intended as a test.
### Open questions
- The betrayal scene itself is not on the wiki.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K2/Party] Kreia: Class Jedi Consular, Level 3. Powers: Burst of Speed, Energy Resistance, Force Camouflage. Feats: BBD=3, Class Skill Stealth, Finesse Melee Weapons, Jedi Sense.
- [K2/Secret_Tomb] Kreia: Class Jedi Consular, Alignment 0 (dark), Level 40, Vitality 340, Force 240, Defense 40.
- [K2/Trayus_Core] Darth Traya: Class Jedi Consular, Level 23, Alignment 30 (dark), Vitality 1180, Force 1221, Defense 51. Powers: Death Field, Force Breach, Force Redirection, Force Storm, Force Wave, Insanity, Kill, Master Drain Force, Master Force Scream, Plague, Stasis Field, Throw Lightsaber. Feats: Advanced Jedi Defense, Force Immunity Fear, Force Immunity Paralysis, Force Immunity Stun, Weapon Specialization Lightsaber.

### Quote bank A: Kreia / Darth Traya's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- «Find what you are looking for amongst the dead?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Close to death, yes, closer than I'd like. You have the smell of the kolto tank about you... how do you feel?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Yes, I had hoped as much - I slept here too long, and could not awaken. It may be I reached out unconsciously, and your mind must have been a willing one. Or perhaps you have been trained for such things?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I am Kreia, and I am your rescuer - as you are mine. Tell me - do you recall what happened?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Your ship was attacked. You were the only survivor... a result of your Jedi training, no doubt.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I confess I know little more than you do... I do not know where here is. I do recall rescuing you... the Republic ship you were on was attacked, and you were the only survivor. A result of your Jedi training, no doubt.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Your stance, your walk tells me you are a Jedi. Your walk is heavy, you carry something that weighs you down.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «So it would seem. Keep your past - and let us focus on the now.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I do not know. I was removed from the events of the world as I slept. A survey of the surroundings may provide the answers we seek. The ship we arrived in must still be in this place. We should recover it and leave.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «We were attacked once, and I fear our attackers will not give up the hunt so easily - without transport, weapons, and information, they will find us easy prey indeed.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Even as I slept, I felt much unrest here - I saw strange visions, minds colored with fear - now, everything here feels terribly silent. I would find out as much as you can about this place quickly - I fear we will need to depart as suddenly as we arrived.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «You may wish to extend your search to some clothes... if only for proper first impressions.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I am not offering to help you - I am not so young as to leap from death's door as quickly as you.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Failure] I do not know anything about that.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «[Success] I do not know - why did they spare you?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Very well... if they do not find us first. Be watchful.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Indeed, a Jedi trance could protect one from such poisons... in fact, the sedatives may have been intended to keep you unconscious for some time. It would prove lethal to those untrained in such techniques, however. Most curious.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «And so do you. Perhaps we could discuss it at length later on - now we have other concerns - among them, finding our new enemy.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I have found that answers come in their own time, not ours. Turn your energy to the matter at hand - if we cannot find a way out of here, the answers will prove useless anyway.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I leave you to the explorations of this place... here I will remain and attempt to center myself.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Yes? What have you found?» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Ah yes. I noticed that as well. It may be that our proximity during our long slumber may have had... unforeseen consequences.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «We seem to be able to speak without speaking. Perhaps this effect will pass with time.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Then hope that we have little to say to each other, lest it prove distracting.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I have little choice - and neither do you. It is an advantage to us both, and I suggest we make use of it.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «I am recovering - your concern is noted, but not necessary. Keep your mind on the present - I sense trouble here.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «When I have recovered, I will do what I can to help you - you needed rescuing once, and I am prepared to rescue you again, if the need arises.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «Not anymore, no. But again, that is a discussion for another time.» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: ...this is the exit... but it is sealed... strange... in my visions, it was open... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: ...be careful... there is much energy in the room beyond... yet it stems from nothing that lives... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: ...can you not sense them... reach out... cast aside your sight, cast aside what you see, and instead, reach out with your perceptions... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: ...ah you can feel them... the droids you cannot perceive, but the small oscillations of energy... that you can feel... echoing outwards... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: Ah... you hear it. It is faint... but it is there. ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: It is the Force you feel... it has not been so long as for you to forget... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: Do not turn away from it. Listen... feel it echoing within you. ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: Come, I shall guide you down the familiar paths - you will need it if we are to survive and escape this place. ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: ...ah... beyond this door someone yet lives... be mindful... his thoughts are... difficult to read... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]
- «:: ...but you have nothing to fear from this one... and he might yet prove useful... ::» [K2/Administration_Level]

- «Predictably, he seeks to unbalance you with his erratic attacks. His technique is called [[../Forms#Ferocity (Juyo)» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It is something the Sith, the assassins that stalk us, can do- it is of the dark side, the ability to feed on life, the Force, the closer one comes to it. It makes them stronger - for a time.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Not all techniques in the Force are learned through practice and training - this is something instinctual, born from experience. It is a way that they fill the hollow places where the Force used to be.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Disciple]**
- «:: So close. ::» [K2/Disciple]
- «:: Jedi. ::» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «:: Betrayer. All thoughts of me will slide from your vision, from your mind, like water. You know who I am. But you will be unable to voice it, to remember it. What does a Jedi see? Only what I allow them to see. ::» [K2/Disciple]
- «At last you understand, tiny Jedi.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Enough. What did you see in the web of worlds that have died? What did you see when you saw it through the Force?» [K2/Disciple]
- «You are a wasted pawn of the Republic, young one. You could have been so much more, even with your wide-eyed innocence, your naive love for others. Now you understand the magnitude of what is being done.» [K2/Disciple]
- «I? Do you think I seek the death of all living things? There is no victory in such things. I do not want to win our war like this, little Jedi. When I win, I wish it to be because I was right, my teachings true.» [K2/Disciple]
- «You know the truth. I have always been here, watching and listening to the echo you have found. You know its source and what must be done.» [K2/Disciple]
- «Little Jedi, you cannot stop me. But you will forget this. Your mind is worse than the others, so open, so trusting. Your feelings for her are your weakness. Yet I will gift you with this - you will remember what you have you discovered... when the time is correct. Know that you have seen what formerly only I knew - now we shall see if you have the strength to stop what comes.» [K2/Disciple]
- «So it ends.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Dock_Module]**
- «You will help us find the information we seek.» [K2/Dock_Module]

**[K2/Docks]**
- «That was well executed - you used his own doubt to foster trust and reliance. Perhaps you are learning much in the flow of Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Docks]
- «Do not mistake my tone. I was not faulting your choice. Far from it, I respect it - there are many who never learn such strength.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Very well. I shall keep my thoughts to myself.» [K2/Docks]
- «It would have been better had he found her on his own. By aiding him, you have only weakened him.» [K2/Docks]
- «He was at a moment of crisis - a moment of indecision. It is those internal struggles, when they are fought and won, that yield the strongest rewards. You took that battle from him, cheapened it. If he truly loved her, truly, he would have entered the Refugee Sector on his own, damn the Exchange, damn everything in his path, and taken her. You have made their union easier, not better.» [K2/Docks]
- «If you care for them, then dispense with pity and sacrifice and recognize the value in letting them fight their own battles. And when they triumph, they will be even stronger for the victory.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «If you ever weakened yourself in such a way for me, I would rather suffer and die than have you demean yourself for me. That is not who you are, who you can become.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Then you do not know yourself, and you will die a fool's death before you are ever tested.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Then you have failed, and when you break, it will be because another does, and then the galaxy shall fall.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Then your allies are your weakness, and if they die, you die with them. And where once the Sith had but one target, now there are many, and you frustrate my attempts to protect you.» [K2/Docks]
- «I grow weary of this. Perhaps time will allow my words to take root if your common sense will not permit it.» [K2/Docks] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- «I have felt a disturbance... our enemy is here. We must leave at once.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «The one that fired upon the Ebon Hawk as we attempted to rescue you... and he will not let us go without blood being shed.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «You know much of battle - enough to know that this is a battle you cannot win.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «The story is a long one, and time is short. Come - we must go, and quickly.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «We are together in this matter, you and I... what affects you, affects me. There can be no division in our actions, or everything is lost.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «I realize that you have traveled alone ever since the Mandalorian Wars, but that time of self-reflection is over. ...and if you cannot see beyond yourself and your own needs, then you should know that I have the only means of accessing the Ebon Hawk's navicomputer. Without it, we both are trapped here.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «We need to make our way to the docking area on this level. I fear the airlock has already opened, and if so, we must be on our guard.  If we cannot reach the Ebon Hawk, then we must find a way to escape on the ship that has docked here.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «What is it? This is not the time for questions.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «They are assassins, and they are responsible for attack on the Ebon Hawk - and the Harbinger. I did not expect them to find us so quickly. We must escape this facility. If we remain here, then we will die.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «And your stubborn blindness to our situation is equally frustrating. I would have expected more from a war veteran, but yet you disappoint me.» [K2/Dormitories]
- «I hope your talent for understatement is offset by your skill with a blaster. If not, then I fear our time together will be short indeed.» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- «That is no exaggeration, Jedi holocrons are tools of instruction, of value to any who study the Force. If this... *man* has one, it is worth his price.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- «He's hiding something.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- «This is not wise. Indiscriminate violence will gain you little.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Engine_Deck]**
- «This battle is mine alone. I am not defenseless.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «He cannot kill what he cannot see, and power has blinded him long ago. Run. I shall be along shortly.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Your senses betray you. As you betrayed me.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «For one as limited as you, perhaps. To have fallen so far and learned nothing - that is your failing.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «Perhaps. We shall see.» [K2/Engine_Deck]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- «Do not use the Force as a crutch - you have survived long without it, do not use it to weaken you.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- «[[../Entertainment Promenade#Someone else» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- «This looks to be a control center of some sort.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «Indeed? But of course - what does one more matter to our journey? I have had enough of this - I will be in my chambers.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You are spending time with the servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I knew her mother. She was a Jedi Knight - a master, named Arren Kae. Jedi are forbidden to have children, and when the crime finally came to light almost a decade later, Kae was exiled. She joined the Mandalorian Wars after the shame of her birth was revealed.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Revan welcomed her. And she was... said to be... a skilled warrior. Beautiful. And strong in the Force. The Force flows strongly in the blood of those born from Force Sensitives. I doubt that Arren was any different. If the servant of Atris is of her blood, then the potential lies within her. If you train her, if you teach her the ways of the Jedi, you will be asking her to break her oath to Atris. It would be best not to train her, and let the bloodline die with Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Should she? By whose judgment should such truths be revealed? I do not have such arrogant presumptions. The Jedi separate children from their parents - as they did you. It is because family exerts a powerful influence on one's development. I am merely saying that revealing such things can have profound consequences... nothing more.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Ah, that was an interesting choice of words, indeed. She has sworn not to follow the path of the Jedi, by her oath... but even that oath is limited. One does not need to be Jedi to learn the ways of the Force. I suspect it cares little for our codes and philosophies.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Once there were only Jedi. I wonder what "evil" was in such days. And to think, once there were no Jedi at all. Perhaps the Force defies such rigid classification of its followers.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I propose nothing except what you choose to read into my words. But no, I would not advocate such teachings, either. I am only saying that she has sworn not to follow the teachings of the Jedi... and that is a curious distinction in her oath.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «But we were speaking of the servant of Atris - I would caution you to be careful of your interaction with her. She is not as... tempered as you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «As you will. But there may be a point where you have no choice... you have a curious influence on those around you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «You must train yourself first. The time will reveal itself. Yet if you persist as you have, building her trust, then you will be training her, whether you know it or not, until the choice is hers - not yours.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Before you continue questioning me, I hope your thoughts in the matter concerning this servant of Atris are clear.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Before you go - a word of caution.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Spend time with her if you must - but recognize where your true loyalties lie - to the galaxy and yourself.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Ah, so then perhaps I was mistaken in my judgment.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Ah, so it is loyalty you claim when you squander away your time with her.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Lectures? No, merely an observation, and obviously, I was mistaken.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Never have you wondered what it would mean in the Echani rituals if the two of you sparred and fought - and you won, completely and utterly? If perhaps she would give in, surrender herself to you? Few are the thoughts that can hide in the shadows of your mind, exile... and such passions are not strength, but erosion.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «No? Then perhaps I was simply mistaken. Very well - I shall keep such judgments to myself.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I cannot help but hear you at times - and such curious thoughts they are, not at all like a Jedi. But I shall keep such thoughts to myself, I think. And you should as well.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «And so it ends.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «:: Betrayal. ::» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «Something troubles you, servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I have seen what you have seen - the slow seduction of the Sith. Perhaps we are more allies than you know. I fear the exile has let his feelings for the Miraluka affect his judgment - and it will doom him.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «I do not believe so. And neither do you - you have seen his stance, his movements. They mirror hers, not yours. But do not mistake my intent, we are together in this. And if we stand together, we may yet prevent a greater tragedy.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «What you think is of no concern - what the exile thinks is what should concern us.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- «There is no time - we must leave.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I have no intention of being left behind. Now... let us leave.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «What of the asteroids? They can be destroyed by us as well as them, can they not?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Then we die here. Choose now.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «The Republic warship was the Harbinger. It was seized on its way to Telos by the Sith - they sought you, Jedi.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yes... to aid in the recovery effort there. Many roads lead to Telos - including ours.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It is where we must go... and where the Harbinger was bound before our unfortunate encounter on Peragus.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You were difficult to find, but... coincidence was on our side. When I learned that you were on the vessel, I knew the Sith would not be far behind. When we intercepted the Harbinger, it was crippled, drifting in space. It was a simple matter to board the vessel and rescue you. Unknown to me, however, the Sith were already on board. Just as we made the jump to hyperspace, they fired upon us, nearly destroying the Ebon Hawk.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Whatever occurred on board the Harbinger had rendered you unconscious. Though your thoughts were faint, I was still able to find you... sealed in one of the cargo holds.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «True - but as one trained in the Force, you know that true coincidences are rare.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I do not know how the Ebon Hawk was able to make it to Pe-» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Be silent! We're having a conversation here.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Because you are the last of the Jedi. Once you are dead, then they have won.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Whatever lies you tell yourself are of no consequence. The Sith believe you to be a Jedi Knight, and that is all that matters.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Exile or not - the Sith believe you to be a Jedi Knight, and that is all that matters.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «The Sith will dare to accuse you of such - they believe you to be a Jedi Knight, and that is all that matters.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «The Jedi Civil War destroyed the Jedi. By the war's end, barely a hundred Jedi remained. Many fell in battle... and many more were seduced by Revan's teachings.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «The Jedi Academy on Dantooine is nothing more than a crater that echoes with the ghosts of dead Jedi. And the Jedi Temple on Coruscant lies empty. The waters in the Room of a Thousand Fountains have fallen still, in reverence to the fallen Jedi... and those now lost. Many Jedi blamed the teachings of the Jedi Masters for Revan's fall... and the civil war that followed.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Many Jedi blamed the teachings of the Jedi Masters for Revan's fall... and the civil war that followed. The Jedi Academy on Dantooine is nothing more than a crater that echoes with the ghosts of dead Jedi. And the Jedi Temple on Coruscant lies empty. The waters in the Room of a Thousand Fountains have fallen still, in reverence to the fallen Jedi... and those now lost.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Perhaps, but they are Jedi no longer. If the Sith have not already slain them, then they will not help you, nor can you help them.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «That... is not an easy question to answer. This threat is greater than you know... and I do not believe it is a battle that can be fought.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «We cannot hope to triumph against them alone. To stop them, you will need weapons, allies, and... a teacher. In the end, I fear it may not be enough.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You fought in the Mandalorian Wars, and it cost you everything. Are you willing to sacrifice as much again?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «For a time, yes. Telos may be such a place - all our paths seem to lead us there. Perhaps there, if you are willing, I can train you, help you to survive a while longer.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You are not listening to me. This is not like any field of battle you have ever fought in. Think carefully on your choice. If you choose to fight, if you choose war, it is a path few turn from once the first steps are taken. It carries with it a terrible price. And in the end, you may find you have nothing left to sacrifice.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Pah, like so many Jedi, you hear, but you do not listen. You have much to learn.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Your fool's words echo of a Jedi - you have much to learn.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «My wound pains me.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «But we have spoken long enough - and my wound pains me.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «If you have other questions, find me in the crew quarters... there, we will speak more.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Also, in private, we will be mercifully free from the opinions of imbeciles and fools.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Have you come for more answers? There is little more left to give.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «That does not surprise me - any more than you hearing my thoughts when we were apart. The pain, however, was unexpected; if I could, I would have shielded you from it.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Of course it was. I knew what was necessary - and that I was the only one who stood between you and him.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «This wound... is a physical thing, and will fade with time. It was necessary... some things may only be learned from sacrifice.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Save your pity. I am here to save you, not the other way around.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I do not need your condescension, nor your lectures. If anyone needs training and guidance, it is you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I do not know. I fear that... the consequences... would have been more extreme.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I do not know if it is possible... and I fear that had the pain been more intense, the consequences would have been more extreme.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Then the sensation you would feel upon my death might be less than that... though much quicker.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Possibly, yes, and I fear it works both ways. I would not wish to test it... nor should you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «When battle is upon us, I suspect our minds are prepared enough to shield each other from the pain. I think we shall not have a repeat incident of what happened at Peragus.» [K2/Hangar_Bay] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «If you think a connection to one such as yourself gives me any comfort, then you would be wrong. I desire this no more than you do. It seems that at times of stress - and pain, if it catches us unawares, then the pain is transmitted between us.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I confess its nature eludes me as well. But the bond is strong, and its roots run deep.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It seems the Force flows easily between us - when one of us manipulates the Force to heal or strengthen ourselves, the other is aided as well.» [K2/Hangar_Bay] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «A powerful technique indeed - though, as we have noticed, it has its drawbacks.» [K2/Hangar_Bay] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I would see to that fool in the cockpit - and remind him of our destination. I would not want him attempting to veer from Telos.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «He is a fool and an imbecile, his potential lies downwards, not up.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Watch that one. His thoughts are slippery... I do not trust him, and nor should you. Such a man serves himself first... and his "allies" next.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Ask, and I will answer.» [K2/Hangar_Bay] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Much has happened in the galaxy in your absence. and since the defeat of the Mandalorians at Malachor V.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «[[../Hangar Bay#Know» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It is a tale you already know well. Almost a decade ago, the Mandalorians began preying on the Republic, bringing the fires of war to many planets along the Outer Rim. Their predations continued, on planet after planet, until the Republic finally begged the Jedi Council for aid.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Indeed. The Jedi Council counseled caution and patience to... assess the Mandalorian threat as the Outer Rim burned.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Two Jedi Knights, Revan and Malak, defied the Jedi Council. They challenged the Mandalorian fierceness and brutality on the battlefield with a viciousness of their own. Revan's entrance into the conflict marked the true beginning... and end... of the war. It was Revan who drove the Mandalorians back into the Unknown Regions.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yes, I have heard tales of Malachor V... and Revan's part in it. I know you served there, in that final battle. It must have been a terrible thing.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yet Revan triumphed... but you know this, for you were there at Malachor V when the Mandalorians were crushed beneath Revan's might.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You speak the truth. The wars' end was merely another beginning, and what seemed a victory for the Republic was far from it.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You asked what had happened, and I am telling you - the past sent echoes into the future, and what seemed a victory for the Republic was far from it.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Many believed the Mandalorians defeated at Malachor V. But the Mandalorians taught the Jedi much through battle. And so it was that Malak, Revan, and the Jedi that followed them discovered their true natures in the Mandalorian crusade. But you know this.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Consumed them? No - taught them, defined them, yes.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Did Revan fail... or was it the failure of the Jedi teachings? A question for another time perhaps.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «If you had followed them, then you would have learned the lesson of a follower. Perhaps it was fortunate you did not.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «As Revan and Malak fought the Mandalorians in battle after battle, they grew to despise weakness, just as the Mandalorians did. In the end, the Mandalorians had taught them through conflict. Shaped the Jedi. And turned them into a weapon - against the Republic.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Revan and Malak and all the Jedi that served them turned against the Republic and the Jedi Order. Jedi fought Jedi. Revan was ambushed by the Jedi and captured. Malak continued to wage war in his Master's place, inflicting terrible wounds on the Republic... wounds that bleed still.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «As all Sith do, without a strong enemy, the Sith turned on each other. Revan escaped the Jedi and returned to finish Malak... and that was the end of the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «No one knows... certainly not I. Korriban lies in ruins, Revan is gone, and the blade of war she promised to stab into the heart of the galaxy has withdrawn.» [K2/Hangar_Bay] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «No one knows... certainly not I. After defeating Malak, Revan left the Republic, and there are none who know where she has gone.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «After defeating Malak, Revan left the Republic, and there are none who know where he has gone.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Where Revan wanders now, I do not know.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It is said that the Sith remnants turned on themselves after Revan defeated Malak, reducing Korriban to ruin, as the Republic still bleeds and struggles for life.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It would seem that way... from a certain point of view, perhaps. The Jedi Civil War left wounds that have yet to heal... we shall see if the Republic has the strength to survive.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Perhaps. It is sometimes... difficult to find truth in the Jedi Civil War. Was it the Republic that defeated Malak, or was it Revan? If it was Revan, then the Republic was never truly tested.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «A culture's teachings, and most importantly, the nature of its people, achieve definition in conflict. They find themselves... or find themselves lacking. Too long did the Republic remain unchallenged. It is a stagnant beast that labors for breath... and has for centuries. The Jedi Order was the heart that sustained its sickness - now the Jedi are lost, we shall see how long the Republic can survive.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «We shall see. The Jedi Civil War cost the Republic much - the resources of the Sith seemed limitless. The Republic's was not. Fleets of warships, soldiers and people were lost. Entire planets were decimated - their inhabitants dead... or refugees. It is a great burden for any civilization to bear. And this new threat... it is a quiet thing, unlike the Jedi Civil War. It drives at something deeper than the strength of the Republic. It is aimed at you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «You see, the war, the true war, has never been one waged by droids, or warships, or soldiers. They are but crude matter, obstacles against which we test ourselves. The true war is waged in the hearts of all living things, against our own natures, light or dark. That is what shapes and binds this galaxy, not these creations of man. You are the battleground. And if you fall, the death of the Republic will be such a quiet thing, a whisper, that shall herald the darkness to come.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I do not know. The Sith struck more swiftly than I thought. And they will not stop until they have you in their grasp. If you fall, all the galaxy will echo it.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It does not matter where we go - it is not the destination that matters, it is the journey. All paths will take us to the end, whatever it may be, and no matter how strongly we fight against it. For now, we are bound for Telos, and that is enough.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «The planet Telos, decimated by the Sith during the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Before the war, Jedi who failed their training were sent to the fields of Telos, to serve the galaxy... not as Jedi Knights, but as farmers and laborers. The destruction of Telos was complete - I doubt any Jedi remain. Yet there may be... echoes of their passing. We shall see.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Then I am left with nothing more than we had already - my faith in you, and your ability to meet what comes.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «These Sith... they seek the death of all Jedi. As have all the Sith since the Jedi Order was first split.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Yes... the Jedi Civil War is not the first one of its kind - thousands of years ago, the Jedi had another civil war that split the Order. It was a... terrible thing. A faction among the Jedi abandoned the teachings of the order, following their own path. They waged war on their fellow Jedi, a war that raged across the galaxy. But these fallen Jedi were cast out, defeated, and they retreated to worlds in the Outer Rim. Over time, they took on the mantle of the Lords of the Sith. But in their hearts, they never forgot the Jedi. The hatred for the Jedi Order burns in their veins like fire, and echoes in their teachings. Revan tasted it... as Malak did.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «In a manner of speaking... they are different from Malak in that they are concerned only with the destruction of the Jedi. For them, it is all that matters... all that ever mattered. It is a different war these Sith wage, a thing of silence and shadow. They strike from the darkness, hiding from the face of the galaxy, until all Jedi are exterminated. After all the Jedi are gone, then the galaxy is theirs, no matter whether the Sith or the Republic rules... it is the dark side that shall reign, unchecked.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I believe them to be the result of... special teachings. Their apparent weakness against you is evidence of this. Those Sith assassins can sense their prey through the Force - it is like a hunger. They feed... and grow stronger... when they are near Force Sensitives. The stronger their prey is in the Force, the deadlier they become. As long as you were cut off from it, you were able to evade their sight. But after Peragus, I fear that you will be no longer shielded from their eyes or the eyes of their masters. The stronger you grow, the more will come.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Indeed? And was it the same as before?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «If my suspicions are correct, perhaps the damage the Jedi Council did was not as permanent as they thought. It is not an easy thing, to cut one off from the Force.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «What did you believe? That you suddenly lost your connection with the Force without reason?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Indeed it is. It is much like losing one's ability to listen, or being put into a deep sleep, unable to awaken to the galaxy around you. Such a thing has been done before, when Jedi have pronounced sentence on their own... and exiled them, as they did you.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «If not the Jedi, then what did you think was the cause of such a loss?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «War leaves many scars, but rarely does it blind one to the Force. If anything, conflict and challenge may make the connection stronger, more intense. No matter what horrors you experienced in the war, no matter who you served, it is unlikely that the Force would be lost to you unless another factor was involved.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «It is possible that such a thing can be undone. Still... even so, the chances of the Jedi undoing such a thing for a traitor... is a slim thing at best, assuming they yet live.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Our link... may have had other consequences. Perhaps you can hear the Force again... distantly, through me. If so, then there is hope. I may be able to teach you, train you to feel the Force again. And if you will not allow me to help you, then other Jedi must train you... or undo the damage they have done.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Then I am your only hope - as you are mine. We are a sad pair, you and I, to defend the galaxy against such a thing.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Do not honor me, fallen Jedi - honor it by listening and learning. Do that, and perhaps we shall survive this thing, you and I.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I fear there may be no other choice. The threat we face is grave... if you cannot defend yourself, then we have already lost.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Life never ceases to teach, fallen Jedi... it is only when one ceases to listen that we grow still... and die.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «I offer to train you to become strong again, to know the ways of the Force, and to hear the Force sing within you as it once did.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Then our training shall begin. Whenever I travel with you, I shall impart what I can to you, through my words and presence.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «That is... acceptable. Whenever I travel with you, I shall impart what I can to you, through my words and presence.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- «Very well. The choice is yours... when we travel, simply leave me behind, and you shall be saved from my counsel.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Influence]**
- «Well executed - you have created an echo, something that will travel for some time. You are learning much.» [K2/Influence]
- «And what is it you think you have accomplished?» [K2/Influence]
- «If you seek to aid everyone that suffers in the galaxy, you will only weaken yourself... and weaken them. It is the internal struggles, when fought and won on their own, that yield the strongest rewards. You stole that struggle from them, cheapened it.» [K2/Influence]
- «Indeed? The currents of the galaxy, of nations, of peoples, may all stem from such small "kindnesses." Every small weakness, small fracture that you create weakens the whole.» [K2/Influence]
- «If you care for others, then dispense with pity and sacrifice and recognize the value in letting them fight their own battles. And when they triumph, they will be even stronger for the victory.» [K2/Influence]
- «Then your allies are your weakness, and if they die, you die with them. And because of that, where once the Sith had but one target, now there are many, and you frustrate my attempts to protect you.» [K2/Influence]
- «If you do not wish to hear it, then you will remain deaf to everything around you, and that shall be punishment enough. I grow weary of this. Perhaps time will allow my words to take root if your common sense will not permit it.» [K2/Influence]
- «That is all I ask.» [K2/Influence]
- «To show such compassion weakens the giver - and the recipient.» [K2/Influence]
- «And so you waste effort upon another.» [K2/Influence]
- «To bestow mercy on another is but another way of proclaiming your superiority.» [K2/Influence]
- «Are psychotic urges all that drives you?» [K2/Influence]
- «I am not speaking to you, fool. Be silent!» [K2/Influence] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I am not speaking to you, alien. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- «I am not speaking to you, slave of Atris. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- «I am not speaking to you, slave. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- «I am not speaking to you, girl. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]
- «Be silent, beast! I am not talking to you.» [K2/Influence]
- «Silence, droid! I am not speaking to you.» [K2/Influence]
- «Did you have any reason behind what you did? Or were you blinded by bloodlust?» [K2/Influence]
- «When one of us is committed to battle, then all of us are committed. We must stand together.» [K2/Influence]
- «I would examine the situation as a whole, all of its ties to the Force, and then decided which string to pull, not sever them all at once. Such crude methods grant you nothing.» [K2/Influence]
- «Perhaps... but not from those who matter.» [K2/Influence]
- «Perhaps...  and perhaps in the future, you will remember such a lesson. There are many paths in life, and some are short - and empty.» [K2/Influence]
- «Your brutality grows tiresome.» [K2/Influence]
- «Is your bloodlust finally satisfied?» [K2/Influence]
- «Such displays are not the signs of true strength.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- «Qixoni crystals are from an earlier age. Some of the first Jedi found them on a planet whose sun was dying. At the time they were matchless in power. The star went nova millennia ago. They are as rare, and as valuable, as she suggests.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- «For the sake of subtlety, I suggest we avoid using grenades, and any other weapons that may injure civilians in this battle.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- «Beasts can be... sensitive to the currents within cities and people. When such things are disturbed, the beasts may echo it.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- «Minds are often weakened when forced to follow familiar patterns... but sometimes those patterns work against you when backed by loyalty or duty. Use your power carefully.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- «A debatable point, but what is done is done.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- «I see.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
