- «The Force is powerful, but overestimating its utility is dangerous.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- «:: Listen to me - clear your thoughts. ::» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- «:: Still your breathing, let the trace amounts of air in your lungs hold you. ::» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- «:: The Force can sustain you... listen to it. Let it keep you alive until you can reach safety. ::» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- «:: It is an old technique, similar to the healing trance - some Jedi can hold their breath for hours, even days... ::» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- «Awaken, beast.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «I have saved your life, beast. That makes it mine. Kneel.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «Because there is something to be learned of strength, beast, even within your empty shell. And it will be needed in the times ahead.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «Because I need you to hunt, beast. This prey is something you have chased all your life... you are born and bred to it, like no predator before you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «No, that you shall not do. You will not bring harm to the exile - and if you do, beast, I shall break you. Even your madness will not save you if you bring harm to the exile. Know this.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «Ah, the life-debts of your people... the life-debt you have twisted with your hate. I felt it within you. I shall promise you this, beast. Unlike the red-maned huntress, as long as you are loyal, I shall never show you mercy. No pity.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «All it requires is that you immerse yourself in another lie, beast. The exile - you shall be her servant, until I call upon you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «All it requires is that you immerse yourself in another lie. The exile - you shall be his servant, until I call upon you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «But most of all, I promise you an end to your debt. Hunt her, pursue her - kill her. And ending her life will end your debt to me. The pain will pass... I was able to heal some of the wounds, but the rest must remain. You will need that pain when you travel, and it will give you strength for the hunt to come.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «Do this thing, and I shall grant your desire.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- «I will tell you where you must go. If you survive that place, then she will come to you. But first, I must prepare you for what is to come.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- «Stop. That is not the skeletal remnant of a war long past. That is a recent kill. It appears that Dxun isn't as abandoned as we would be led to believe.» [K2/Jungle]
- «I cannot say for certain. What I do know is our path came here for a reason.» [K2/Jungle]
- «The past has a curious attraction to us all. Perhaps he came in a small shuttle to revisit old battlegrounds. Perhaps not.» [K2/Jungle]
- «A few days - a standard week, at most. The jungle has not left its mark on his armor yet. You probably have more questions.» [K2/Jungle]
- «But let us press forward. You may find the answers you seek.» [K2/Jungle]
- «This may prove of use to us. Let us hear his words and see if they hold any value.» [K2/Jungle]
- «A moment - that beast there. Do you see it?» [K2/Jungle]
- «Wait a moment... Do you see that boma over there?» [K2/Jungle]
- «The Force flows even through these simple creatures. If you empty your mind you may be able to feel its thoughts.» [K2/Jungle]
- «They aren't fully formed. Basic instincts, primal urges, every breath dominated by the needs of the moment.» [K2/Jungle]
- «They aren't fully formed. Basic instincts, urges, dominated by every breath and every moment. Primal and unsophisticated.» [K2/Jungle]
- «The Force is strong in such creatures... but their minds are weak, prey for those able to hear their thoughts - and influence them.» [K2/Jungle]
- «The point is to learn something about the Force. Even if you cannot appreciate that, you should value the power my teachings will provide you.» [K2/Jungle]
- «Good. Beasts can be easier to affect than other sentients. But you must bridge the gap between what distinguishes us and them.» [K2/Jungle]
- «You feel its thoughts, yes? Like a low rumble before the storm. Use the Force to create a barrier around it - carefully and slowly.» [K2/Jungle]
- «You feel its consciousness, yes? Like a low rumble before the storm. Use the Force to create a barrier around it. Make it with care and subtlety.» [K2/Jungle]
- «[Failure] You came close, and you have the potential. With practice you can make any animal passive and pliable.» [K2/Jungle]
- «You came close, and you have potential. With practice, you can make any animal passive and pliable.» [K2/Jungle]
- «But the cage around their perceptions is a fragile thing. Many things can break its hold. Violence, especially.» [K2/Jungle]
- «With the Force, anything is possible. But that is the end of the lesson for now.» [K2/Jungle]
- «All things in time.» [K2/Jungle] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You may thank me by using what you have learned. Let us go.» [K2/Jungle]
- «The Force is subtle and more powerful than people imagine. Keep an open mind.» [K2/Jungle]
- «[You have gained the power {{s» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- «Those artifacts have tremendous value and could be dangerous in the wrong hands. This is something we should investigate.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «The Jedi Enclave on Dantooine was one of the hidden academies for Padawans and apprentices. Many Jedi came here during their training.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «You should keep your identity secret for the time being. Try to convince this fool you are not a Jedi.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «You should have listened to me. Now we may have to confront villagers with torches and pitchforks.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- «Very good. You can always have your way with the weak-minded by catering to their ego.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Korriban]**
- «There is much that would draw a Jedi to this place. The resting grounds of the ancient... and more recently departed Sith... contain many teachings believed lost. The most likely place to find our lost Jedi is the ruins of the old Academy.» [K2/Korriban]
- «After Malak and his army were defeated, instead of restoring order to Korriban, Revan suddenly departed, leaving both his destination and reasons for leaving a mystery.» [K2/Korriban] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It was said that Revan intended to return to Korriban to subdue any potential Sith insurgents. But Revan disappeared.» [K2/Korriban]
- «It took a year or two for the Republic to send a force here to deal with any Sith that may have remained. They found Korriban much as we have - barren and lifeless. It was assumed that the remnants of the Sith turned on each other, vying for what little power remained. The Republic found evidence that several Sith Lords escaped Korriban, fleeing to remote sections of the galaxy.» [K2/Korriban]
- «As lifeless as it seems the dark side is very strong here. The Sith Lords would not ignore such a powerful place. There is much that can be learned, even here.» [K2/Korriban]
- «If you walk Korriban's surface, you shall walk it without me.» [K2/Korriban]
- «I cannot. This place is strong with the dark side... it is difficult to center myself here. Korriban holds few secrets from me... but much that you should learn.» [K2/Korriban]
- «Perhaps not. But I would caution you to guard your feelings carefully here - Korriban attacks the spirit and the body, and there have been few who can fight its power.» [K2/Korriban]
- «I will remain here and meditate. Our link remains, I shall contact you and provide guidance when needed. The Academy is on the other side of this valley. Be careful. Dark energy fills these ruins... and even the fallen Sith live still.» [K2/Korriban]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- «You have grown strong in the Force - I can feel its mark upon you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Be warned - the highest of mysteries cannot be found by falling to the dark side. It is a quick path - and often, a short one. But I sense a disquiet in you, an unrest. Such things will erode your strength.» [K2/Kreia]
- «They sense the trappings of power and decision upon you. Their lives are static, trapped, and they see one who carries no such chains.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You have grown strong in the Force - I can feel its touch upon you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Do not let the higher mysteries blind you to others. Turning away from that which tempts you or causes you fear is not strength. Facing it is. I have seen you display many acts of mercy - charity, in our journey. Why?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Even if it weakens those you help? Even if it robs them of the strength needed to grow? You have been chased, hounded, since our first meeting. And it has only made you stronger, more capable.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah, then you have learned nothing. From such small things, from such critical points, the universe and its masses may be moved... that is why you must be careful in all that you do, and in every choice you make. Aiding them gives you strength by taking on their challenges but weakens them. If that is your choice, then use their dependency, feed upon it, until you have exhausted them, then leave them. And I would view the ones you travel with much the same way.» [K2/Kreia]
- «So you may think, but it will be best to strike first - your allies are allies by circumstance, and they will not hesitate to do the same to you, if you continue down your path.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah, now you are learning.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Do you know why those we meet display such weakness? As I said, their lives are static, untested. It is only through interaction, through decision and choice, through confrontation, physical or mental, that the Force can grow within you. You have seen it, you have felt it within you as you have traveled with me. The growing anger, the rage, and the power it brings. Yet the power does not build without such struggle. Through small cruelties, greater ones are born.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Yes? Have you come with questions?» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Very well. What is it that drives you?» [K2/Kreia]
- «I confess its nature eludes me as well. But the bond is strong, and its roots run deep. It seems that at times of stress - and pain, if they catch us unawares, then the pain is transmitted between us.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Indeed? A Jedi tool - and a Sith weapon. And why do you need such a thing?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then listen to me. There is much weight, much craving attached to such a tiny thing of light.» [K2/Kreia]
- «For the male, it seems to have inordinate importance. But we shall leave such male preoccupations for philosophers and cultural historians.» [K2/Kreia]
- «A lightsaber - any weapon - only achieves worth in how it is wielded - in the effort, the struggle of one who holds it. Such a weapon does not make a Jedi or a Sith. And at times it makes much them much, much less than they are.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The knowledge has already been imparted to you. Upon our journey, the pieces shall fall into place.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The Jedi practice many forms, many styles of lightsaber combat. It is good to know them, but not to rely on them. You may have already felt the Shii-Cho, the simplest of the forms return to you as your skill and perceptions have returned.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Others may come with time, with experience.» [K2/Kreia]
- «It is simple, and its simplicity is strength. It allows focus, a slightly improved chance of connecting with one's opponent. It has no other advantages or disadvantages - it is an effective form to fall back on when no other form will do.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah - a technique that helps one resist the Force attacks of an enemy - and also is excellent in lightsaber combat. It does more damage, but it leaves you vulnerable to other attacks. Use it against others wielding the Force or lightsabers... but not against anyone else.» [K2/Kreia]
- «A... defensive technique. But effective. Use it if you do not wish to be hit, or if you are facing many opponents with blasters. With a lightsaber blade and enough skill in deflection, it is an excellent offense against blasters, but in other situations, it merely delays the inevitable.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah, one of the techniques you have learned from the masters. This technique is good against a lone opponent, and you will find your critical strikes are more effective. But you will be vulnerable to almost everyone else you are fighting.» [K2/Kreia]
- «An aggressive technique - but it is powerful, effective. It can counter blaster weapons, and add to the damage of your critical.» [K2/Kreia]
- «A blending of younger forms, and all of them in moderation. In the blending, much of the individuality is lost, but the strengths are spread evenly, and there is little weakness in it. Its great strength is the way it allows the Force to flow through you, revitalizing one even in the middle of combat.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Perhaps one of the greatest styles, learned only by the most skilled of Force wielders. Impressive. In many ways, some have described it as a surrender to the Force and to chaos, to strike at an opponent with such speed and angles of attack that they have difficulty countering it. Your number of attacks will increase, and you will notice many more of them shall slip beneath an opponent's guard - critically wounding them. But in surrendering yourself to the Force in such a way reduces your ability to resist its effects should the enemy turn the Force against you. Be on your guard.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well. Of which did you wish instruction?» [K2/Kreia]
- «It allows you to recover your strength with the Force more quickly, and it lends strength to your Force powers. It has no other drawbacks - such a form is a gift, preferred of the Jedi Consulars, and effective in combats where you must fight only through the Force.» [K2/Kreia]
- «It is a great technique that you have learned. Gifted only to the highest of Jedi masters - and the Sith - this technique makes your Force powers more difficult to resist... and they will last longer as well. But it can tire you quickly. Use it only in short combats, where expediency is necessary.» [K2/Kreia]
- «With this form, your use of the Force will have more damaging effects, but it will drain you as well. Effective, but only if conventional means are having no effect.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Helpful, especially in tandem with other force techniques. This one opens you to the Force, allowing you to recover your strength with the Force rapidly in combat.» [K2/Kreia]
- «She did nothing to your eyes that was not already there. She has forced this upon you, but such crude methods are the markings of the Sith.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Close your eyes.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «If that is your belief, so be it.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Feel this ship around you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Listen to my words. Hear the sound of the Handmaiden's training in the cargo hold, her hands cutting the air.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The welding of the droid as it goes about its work.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Shhhhh.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Now stretch out. Hear the rumble of hyperspace, the hum of the hyperdrive.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ignore distractions, and focus on my voice.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The breathing of the blinded one as she meditates in the dark. Now listen deeper. Past her breathing, and listen.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You know what you heard - it is what I hear, constant whispers of wants, desires, lies told to oneself to stave off the darkness around us.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You are strong indeed.... what you heard were surface thoughts only, but it is something that masters have trained for for years and never learned.» [K2/Kreia]
- «That is not the real question you should ask - is such listening enough to perceive the world around you? It is not. Because to listen to the thoughts of another is much like attempting to see the universe only with your eyes. It is equally limiting. Now leave me be. I must rest.» [K2/Kreia]
- «When I spoke of sight before, there is a similar handicap that tends to occur among those strong in the Force. They neglect their skills. Some believe they no longer need them. The greatest wielders of the Force are those that maintain some grounding to the more... physical realities of the universe.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Some wielders of the Force have mastered piloting... others the ability to fix and repair and build, from simple moisture vaporators to more complex machines, such as droids and vehicles. One's ability to understand the human body and its ailments, for example, can make your powers within the Force more complete, more powerful when you attempt to repair the cellular damage of another. And others have mastered the more subtle work of politics, persuasion. Do not doubt that a galaxy may be conquered with words, a Republic overthrown, and an empire made. When such skills are honed, one's abilities with the Force become that much stronger. My warning to you is this - do not rely on your companions to compensate for your weaknesses in skill. There will be times they will not be there to help you when needed.» [K2/Kreia]
- «What skill would you say is your greatest strength?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well - return when you are, and I shall have more to impart.» [K2/Kreia]
- «And what skill would you say is your greatest weakness?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then my task before you is this - take your greatest weakness, devote effort to it, strengthen it, and I will show you how it shall strengthen your power in the Force.» [K2/Kreia]
- «As you learn and train and test yourself against the galaxy, all your skills have a chance to improve and grow as well. When you devote some of that training to your weakest skill, you will know.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Have you done as I asked?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well. Then know this... your ability to perceive the environment around you extends not only to perceiving dangers, but the awareness of the motivations of others. As there are dangers in your environment all around you, there are similar dangers and threats lurking in the mind of others... to see these things sometimes is enough.» [K2/Kreia]
- «That is a skill I know well. To know the knots of muscle, the currents of blood within the veins and flesh... it is a galaxy within the galaxy. The ability to heal is also the ability to harm. And the inability to heal what is damaged can also bring about harm.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well. Then know this... many are the times I meditate in the silence of my chambers. Do you know why I do this? I do this so that I might center myself, listen to the currents of the galaxy. There are others who may achieve this in occupying the mind, in achieving a moving meditation where operating on machinery achieves a similar effect.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well. Then know this... all situations contain within them the potential for violence. To know how to hold that violence in check, to restrain it, and use it to hold the situation to your advantage is the way of any wielder of the Force.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well. Then know this... how some situations play out depend on who is perceiving them... and whether they are being perceived. Your ability to control the perception of others in such situations will allow you to manipulate events, to resolve them peacefully or violently.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The ability to persuade others is a powerful weapon... perhaps more so than any lightsaber. It is a strength to defeat an opponent without combat, to convince them that your will is right, or just... or will simply benefit them in a way that they had not perceived. Examine their actions, and the why of how they do them. And when you achieve such understanding, more truths may be revealed.» [K2/Kreia]
- «There are many secrets that the Force may not unlock, for they are not things of life... to uncover such secrets can allow you to see events more clearly. It can grant you more weapons, more wealth, and allow you access to places and their secrets that can often turn the tide of a battle or change events.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Skill does not always draw from the Force, but it is a measure of power nonetheless. It can grant knowledge, help steady oneself when one's thoughts are in chaos, or grant enlightenment. Much can be achieved without the Force... as you know. Life continues, persists, and may be helped or harmed as a result. Now, I am tired - leave me to my meditations.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well, sit with me.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You have brushed the surface thoughts of another. It is a start. Calm yourself. This time, silence your own thoughts, keep them still. Imagine the waters of the Room of a Thousand Fountains, each stream suddenly falling silent and still. Imagine the ice of Telos, cold and smooth, as it gathers upon the plateau. Now stretch out, feel the ship around you. Strip away the metal, and see the souls and minds of those that fill its corridors, with more thoughts and dreams and worries than can fill the space of this ship.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Not now. Focus on my voice.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Now do you hear me? Truly hear me.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You have taken the first steps on a much longer road, exile.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The droids cannot be read in such a way - nor the beast, he has little thoughts to speak of. As for the alien who served with you in the war, its thoughts are more difficult, requiring many translations in meaning.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The droids cannot be read in such a way - as for the alien who served with you in the war, its thoughts are more difficult, requiring many translations in meaning.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The droid cannot be read in such a way - as for the alien who served with you in the war, its thoughts are more difficult, requiring many translations in meaning.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Often it is better to read their impulses and images than their spoken thoughts. That is why he is deaf to you - I have found his impulses are cold, like a dead weight, his thoughts are black.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Indeed. It is strange that I did not.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Perhaps. I would not put much weight on such things.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Such is the case with primitive minds. It is of no matter.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Of course there was. It is because Atton was not playing pazaak, yet he counts cards in his head. At times, he will list off engine sequencers, memorize the hyperspace routes on the other side of the galaxy, count the ticking in the power couplings even though they are fixed. At other times... he will imagine certain... base lusts, certain... indignities. It may be Atton is far cleverer than he feigns to be. Or perhaps he is simply a fool.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah. I had wondered if... but your powers are strong indeed.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Now is not the time to speak of this. Wait until you have more of the galaxy within you, and we shall speak again.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Every step on our journey shall bring with it discoveries - with persistence, you shall grow in the Force. And for every planet we reach, all that we touch with our presence, you shall grow - for you will have no choice.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then ask, and I will do my best to answer.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Does it matter? Of course it does, such titles allow you to break the galaxy into light and dark, categorize it. Perhaps I am neither, and I hold both as what they are, pieces of a whole. Know that I am your teacher, and that is enough.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Failure] Or what? Shall you kill me? Hurt me? You would only be inflicting harm on yourself. Perhaps you will keep me here on this ship, prisoner, unable to leave.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Is that the way of a Jedi?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Or perhaps you will simply cast me out, exile me - it matters not.» [K2/Kreia]
- «That is enough for today, and I must rest.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Success] What do you wish to hear? That I once believed in the code of the Jedi? That I felt the call of the Sith, that perhaps, once, I held the galaxy by its throat?» [K2/Kreia]
- «That for every good work that I did, I brought equal harm upon the galaxy? That perhaps what the greatest of the Sith Lords knew of evil, they learned from me? What would it matter now? There is only so much comfort in knowing such things, and it is not who I am now.» [K2/Kreia]
- «There are dark places in the galaxy where few tread. Ancient centers of learning, of knowledge... But I did not walk alone. To be united by hatred is a fragile alliance at best. But my will was not law. There were disagreements, ambition. And hunger for power. There are techniques within the Force against which there is no defense. I was cast down. Stripped of my power. Exiled. I suffered... indignities. And fell into darkness.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Take what strength you may steal from me, and that is all I need be to you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Learn from me, my mistakes, and use that knowledge to become greater than I. That is all I ask of you, and that is all I desire.» [K2/Kreia]
- «In you all my hopes rest, for the future, for the Force.» [K2/Kreia]
- «If it means so much to you, then this I swear to you upon my life... upon our lives... that when your training is complete, I will answer everything. There shall be no more shadows between us, only truth that exists between master... and apprentice.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Success] I know of them, yes... and how much like beasts they had become.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Failure] Nothing more than we already know - and anything else I know would be useless. There is danger in such knowledge - even if I were certain of the ones who hunt you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Combined, united against the Jedi, they command legions of Sith. But above these legions, there are three who must be stopped - as long as any one of them lives, then we - and all life are doomed. One bathes in pain, feeds on it for sustenance. The other has ceased being a living being, so consumed by hunger than he has forgotten his own flesh. And the last is a creature of betrayals, for without such things, there is no hope.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The less said of that one, the better - even a stray thought may draw him, and it is possible that he cannot be defeated. He is one who has learned the greatest of the Sith teachings - and it enslaved him. Until you are ready, we must not seek him out.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Yes... of pain, he has learned much. Of knowledge, of teaching, he knows nothing. Like the others, he was spawned by the horrors of the Mandalorian Wars. He exists solely to spread his pain to all Jedi, everywhere.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Even now, she is difficult to see. She must remain hidden for now until the time is right - if not, then all our efforts will be for nothing. In this, you must trust me. If she is exposed too soon, then this war will be over before it has begun.» [K2/Kreia]
- «There are countless reasons, and I have neither the time nor the patience to list them all.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Do you think to turn her from Atris' will? If so, I hope your arrogance will prove true in time. But I will abide her presence. She may have her uses.» [K2/Kreia]
- «No - she may have her uses. I will abide her presence, and so should you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because Atris is a threat. And as much as she would try to use us against you, so may we use her servants against her. Do not see every enemy as an enemy - see them instead as an ally, whether they realize it or not. This situation may yet work to our advantage.» [K2/Kreia]
- «If that is your opinion, it is noted. Is that all you wished to speak to me about?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Good. That is the most to be done until events unfold... as I am sure they will, in time.» [K2/Kreia]
- «There is nothing wrong with my sight, if that is your question. I see all that I need, though the seeing of things flesh and blood has failed me some time ago. They were distractions only.» [K2/Kreia]
- «There is nothing wrong with my eyes - they simply have atrophied from use. They are adequate to distinguish shapes, silhouettes. If need be, I could heal them, restore my sight, but sight can prove a distraction. When one relies on sight to perceive the world, it is like trying to stare at the galaxy through a crack in the door. But that is a lesson for another time. You must learn to see crude matter for what it is before the veil is lifted.» [K2/Kreia]
- «﻿Betrayal. And by a machine, that I will not abide.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Machines - always did Revan use them to inflict her will on the galaxy, whether in war or in assassination. Her passion for such tools, for things dead to the Force, defies me. She would have loved a human droid such as you, dead to the Force, a shell that would carry out her orders.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It is no matter - it is a device, and now it is broken.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Success] Atris herself is not as familiar to me as perhaps she should be. Yet I feel I know her, yes.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Failure] I know her as much as I know any Jedi... if you have other questions, you may ask those, but on Atris, I can provide you no answers that you cannot find on your own.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because Atris' path is one I walked long ago, and it is a chapter of my life that has been read and closed. She has taken the first steps, I think - we shall see. Surely you felt the righteous anger, the spoken judgments, the lack of forgiveness.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I was a historian once, gathering the relics of the Jedi, learning the ancient mysteries. Always, there were more questions. One quickly learns that the Jedi code does not give all the answers. If you are to truly understand, then you will need the contrast, not adherence to a single idea. That is why Atris and the others blamed me, sentenced me. They believed me responsible for Revan's fall.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You have already asked much... I do not wish to speak of this any longer.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Success] Have you never asked yourself how Revan took the Republic and Jedi beneath him, how he made them his?» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Influence: Failure] A discussion, perhaps, for another time.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah, but to make officers turn on their own people, to bomb innocent worlds to make pacts... strong influence, indeed. And where did these Sith teachings come from? And why did Revan embrace them so strongly? So many questions... yet the answers are few.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Very well, then we shall speak no more of this. Leave me - I am tired.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Oh, did they? No, Revan met no Sith empire, yet he learned their teachings.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Many have mistaken the soldiers beneath Revan, the machines that were constructed, to be the "Sith."» [K2/Kreia]
- «They are wrong -  the Sith is a belief. And what Revan formed was not an empire, but something else - yet how he did it is curious.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «And I suspect the answer to that question is tied to another - how was Revan able to corrupt so many, so quickly?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Not a one. But we shall see where our journey takes us, I think. And see how many answers we come across, yes?» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Success] Perhaps... I think it is fair to say that few did.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Failure] I misspoke before - and I do not wish to choose my words unwisely again. Leave this be.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah... and what did you wish to know of Revan?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Revan had a mother and father, parents, ancestors, like all Jedi do. And when he awakened to his potential, I was there to see it. But where he was born, where he came from, I do not know... anymore than I know where he walks now. Some said that Revan was born in the Outer Regions, beyond the Rim, and that's what called to him during the Mandalorian Wars... and after.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It was the call of home.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I know more... but it may not be enough for the answers you seek.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Fall? Ah, already you presume much.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You were there at Malachor. Revan's choices were always his own. It was not teaching, or circumstance, or example. It was him.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Is that what he was? Or was he always true to himself, no matter what personality he wore?» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «And there is something that the Council may never understand. That perhaps Revan never fell. The difference between a fall and a sacrifice is sometimes difficult, but I feel that Revan understood that difference, more than anyone knew.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The galaxy would have fallen if Revan had not gone to war. Perhaps he became the dark lord out of necessity, to prevent a greater evil.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I do not believe the Jedi Council changed Revan, as they claimed. They merely stripped away the surface, and allowed the true self to emerge again - someone who was willing to wage war to save others. But that is my belief, since I knew Revan from long ago... as a master knows their apprentice.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[Influence: Success] He came to me, yes. Both before and after, before Revan knew himself.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Influence: Failure] I do not wish to discuss this - sometimes there is such a thing as knowing too much of one's teacher. It generates... contempt.» [K2/Kreia]
- «And after, in the times when Revan was coming into his own and learning he was more than he had been told. At one time, Revan was my Padawan. In times past, long ago. But Revan, when he had learned all he could, had other masters... that fool Zhar, and other Jedi on other planets. He learned from each. But in the end, he turned back to me. When he realized there was nothing more to be learned from the Jedi - except how one could leave them forever.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Revan was power. It was like staring into the heart of the Force. Even then, you could see the Jedi he would slay etched on his soul.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You are different. When I look at you, it is like staring at the death of the Force.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ignorance... and perhaps they do not remember, or care. It is of no consequence to me, or to them.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Do not let them discover what makes you what you are before you do, or the consequences for the galaxy will be terrible.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because you learned to live without the Force. And that is something that the galaxy is not ready to accept.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Am I? Then perhaps you should know... there are techniques in the Force, where one can cloud the memory of others, make their presence so small as to be unnoticed. And on the worlds where we have encountered these Jedi... there is much life and death, where sensing such things is difficult. As I said, it is no consequence to me - or to them.» [K2/Kreia]
- «No - but if I did, you would never know, so my words only carry as much worth as you believe them to. But perhaps you will understand this - that perhaps it is important to me that you see me and my actions, uncloaked. It is important that your judgments, whether be good or bad, stem from seeing me as I truly am.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I did not ask you to trust me - only that you listen to what I have to tell you. But thank you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then you are learning quickly. Distrust is an effective shield, and should be carried always.» [K2/Kreia]
- «[[../Kreia#Homeworld» [K2/Kreia]
- «He... if he can truly be called a man any longer... is one of the dark lords that pursues you. I do not think he knows what you are, not yet. He spared the Miraluka, and that may have been the last shred of feeling that exists within him. Keep his slave close to you. I suspect there was a reason he spared her... and perhaps a reason that she survived when the rest of her people and the Jedi did not.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Entertain what illusions you will, I am too tired to argue them with you.» [K2/Kreia] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «One cannot have power of that magnitude that her master possesses and still think and perceive the universe as we do, as most of us do. I had hoped that you would not have to confront him... but her presence here has changed all that. You will have to meet him in battle.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Perhaps he is bound to her... as I am bound to you. If so, there may be a death served by hers.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You must be prepared to sacrifice the blinded one. Perhaps her death will buy you the time you need to deal with her master.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then you have a chance. Without her death, I do not know if he can be stopped.» [K2/Kreia]
- «If you have such power, then I would make use of it... there is no need to dirty your hands when you can convince another to drive a blade into their own heart.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Oh, you have promise. But wait until you have more years fall upon you, and you will see what a shell your heart will become.» [K2/Kreia]
- «It is a technique that is almost as old as the Sith themselves... it is a means of severing connections between life, the Force, and feeding upon the death it causes. It cannot be taught... it can only be gained through instinct, through experiencing its effects, first-hand.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Yes. And he fed upon its destruction - it will sustain him, for a time.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because it is not something that can ever truly be controlled... and it leaves nothing to conquer in its wake. And it rules him, not the other way around. It has its own will, its own instincts.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Power? Do you think so? You would be wrong. There is no strength in the hunger he possesses... and the will behind his power is a primal thing. And it devours him as he devours others - his mere presence kills all around him, slowly, feeding him. He is already dead, it is simply a question of how many he kills before he falls.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Nothing is impossible with the Force. It is an energy that flows through all living things. And like energy, it may be harnessed, channeled, and consumed at times. It may even be a substance that can burn and ignite. Do not think of his power as one would a weapon, or one of your warships of the Republic. It is terrible, but it is still a subtle thing. The sect of assassins that chase you feed on the Force... what he does is simply the pinnacle of what they could achieve, in time. And that is why they - and their techniques - must be wiped out. No one again must experience and learn what her master did.» [K2/Kreia]
- «As much as one may use the Force to bolster the wills and strengths of others, the reverse is possible, though not often used. Instead of sending one's will through connections in the Force, instead such connections are drawn upon, fed upon, and drained completely.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then you understand how terrible such a power is. And why it must be ended. It is an empty road to the dark side, and by traveling it, the price is death before one's time. He is a breach in the Force, capable of consuming the lives of those around him. Sometimes the touch is slow, as it is with his crew.» [K2/Kreia]
- «It is not something he can direct or focus, much like hunger itself. He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing. Force Sensitives and worlds rich in the Force draw him. The Miraluka world was one such place. That is why where the Jedi gather, Jedi will die. He will feel it, unless they mask their presence - but Katarr called out as a beacon to him, and he could not resist it. And he cares nothing for the Sith or its teachings... or the Jedi. And when the Jedi are dead, he will feed on the galaxy, the Republic, and eventually, consume the Sith as well.» [K2/Kreia]
- «There is no future in the empty galaxy he sees. And that is why he must be stopped, the breach must sealed, before his power grows beyond what even we can hope to stop.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The beast is a lesson in strength. Learn that lesson, then you will understand.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because he is life at its most primitive. And he represents what happens when civilization comes to primates - the illusion that we are something more than our instincts.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Perhaps not. Then you are correct, there is nothing you may learn from the beast - and clearly, I am wrong.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The beast's strength is prodigious, and you can learn much from it... or draw upon it. His life can be a beacon in the dark places of the galaxy where there is no life.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Speak with him - discern his nature. Perhaps then, it will become clear.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Indeed? And what is that?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Yes. Now the question is why.» [K2/Kreia]
- «No.... I feel there may have been a time when his people felt the Force. But that is only a suspicion. Whether one can feel the Force or not has little to do with their strength. Think more upon this - the answer will come to you, or it will not.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Think more upon this - the answer will come to you, or it will not.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Perhaps you should think upon the matter more - and I think you will discover where real strength lies.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah... you are a perceptive one indeed. In this instance, you are correct. All that strength... that anger. Yet it is held in check, restrained by his beliefs, his doubt. Everyone is made up of events in their past, and it forms walls around one's spirit, or breaks such walls down. The mind makes some powerless - and gives strength to others.» [K2/Kreia]
- «In a manner of speaking, yes. But it is more complicated than that.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Everyone is made up of events in their past. It makes some powerless - and gives strength to others. It is because he is not ready to give up his ties yet - it is much like Jedi who will not give up their code. It is to surrender yourself, to make yourself a slave to a teaching or belief, that makes it so that belief will always rule you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I ask nothing. But since you beg a question, I will ask it. Are you prepared to give up all that is a Jedi, entirely? Until then, you will always be weak.» [K2/Kreia]
- «No, the Jedi lie with you still. You have no choice in the matter - but your willingness to break those ties is a lesson in strength.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I have felt as much from you. But listen to me.» [K2/Kreia]
- «To believe in an ideal, is to be willing to betray it. It is something no Sith or Jedi has ever truly learned. That is the lesson of strength. Yet that is not the only lesson that the beast can teach us. Not only does one's will control one's strength, but one's will can control and draw upon the strength of others. Surely you have felt the beast's presence on board this ship - stalking, restless.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ignore the surface sensations - I am attempting to teach you something greater here.» [K2/Kreia]
- «It is a more primal connection you feel. And the hunger you feel does not stem from Hanharr - it is something you may draw upon, his very life, if need be. He does not realize how deeply his life debt runs - but he will. When you suffer, call upon that hunger, and the beast shall be that upon which your will may draw strength. Reach out... feel his presence within the ship, clawing at you, pacing. Feel the rumbling of the hull, the metal around you like a cage... and the building anger, the blood that rises behind the eyes, a bloodlust that cannot be sated. If you accept it, you will find your strength increase, and your vitality return to you faster with every breath.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Are you sure? It will grant you strength, vitality for the times ahead. Forsake it, deny it, and you will deny power.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah... and that is the choice of Malachor V, at last. You have made a strange choice - a unique choice. Very well. I accept it.» [K2/Kreia]
- «And know that that is the true lesson of strength - to turn away from strength that is not your own.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I am always testing you, never forget that. Always be on your guard - otherwise, you may learn something.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Good - you know it now, the predator-prey relationship, the strong feeding on the weak. It will serve you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because his life is not his own. He walks, but he is dead. And there is a use for ones such as he, along with all of his kind.» [K2/Kreia]
- «How many more do we intend to gather to us? This ship is not the galaxy, there is only so much room.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then prepare for an army, I think, for it seems many more will come in time.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Is it? Perhaps you are wrong.» [K2/Kreia]
- «They will follow you because you are a leader. Their kind always need such, even when the figure deserves no such obedience.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Do not cloak one word within another. Friends. Do friends not follow? Do friends not form a hierarchy of their own, no matter how small the circle?» [K2/Kreia]
- «I am too old for friends, and when the years settle upon you, you will dispense with such words as well.» [K2/Kreia]
- «No? Perhaps not - or perhaps you are different, something more.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You deserve nothing - but you have earned such obedience, yes. They are tools, you are right. More than you know.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because I am not blind, that is why. I see what they see, hear their voices when they speak to you, and notice the change when they speak to others.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I am not blind. I see what they see, hear their voices when they speak to you, and notice the change when they speak to others.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I know many things, and I know what I am not - I am no leader. I speak with a voice that will never move others, I speak with a passion that goes unheard. They obey you because you are a leader, and perhaps something more. Have you noticed what has been happening? Have you felt it in them?» [K2/Kreia]
- «They echo you, either fighting or surrendering to their feelings, their loyalty, their duty. Your mere presence serves as an example to them - of something to uphold, or something to fight against.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The fool dances in your shadow for your favor. The disciple... he worships you quietly. The alien obeys you. Even within the machines, there are echoes.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The fool dances in your shadow for your favor. The woman... she worships you. The alien obeys you. Even within the machines, there are echoes.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Watch them carefully, see their patterns, and recognize the strength in it. Influence can be a weapon, one that you may need before your journey is done.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I? I am but a mirror whose only purpose is to show you what your own eyes cannot yet see.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I care not which of the words you use, as long as you make use of that which you forge.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Good - and then act upon it. It is a powerful tool to motivate others.» [K2/Kreia]
- «That was Revan's way, I believe. It was a strength.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You have befriended the seer.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Her species does not see as we do. They perceive the galaxy through the Force... and it is how she found you. It is a rare gift, squandered on her people.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Despite your... intentions, it would be better to deal with her now. Whether you intend salvation or slavery, she is a threat to us.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Despite your... urges, it would be better to deal with her now. Whether you intend salvation or slavery, she is a threat to us.» [K2/Kreia]
- «She serves one of the greatest of the Sith, she is the most trusted... and only apprentice. Yet you spare her. Why?» [K2/Kreia]
- «We shall see. There may be value in such a choice to keep her alive - or perhaps not.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Whatever your intentions toward her, keep them... restrained. We have no time for mercy.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Whatever your intentions toward her, keep them restrained. Whether mercy or lust, we have time for neither.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Lectures? I am merely making an observation.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Is that so? Well, perhaps there will be a second chance for her to inflict harm so you may test such a statement.» [K2/Kreia]
- «It is good that you have never wondered what lay beneath her robes, if her alabaster skin was as white and unblemished as her face. Or if perhaps she bore the scars of slavery... and if that would stir you more. If perhaps her deferent tone would change once you held her by the throat, and showed her how far a Jedi can fall. Few are the thoughts that can hide in the shadows of your mind, exile... and such passions are not strength, but erosion.» [K2/Kreia]
- «No? Then perhaps I was simply mistaken. See to it you do not ever think such thoughts. Mating with her will bring more harm than you know.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I cannot help but hear you at times - and such curious thoughts they are. Not at all like a Jedi. See to it you do not ever act upon your impulses. Mating with her will bring more harm than you know.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The Sith carry the battle to you, and you spare them. And as we travel, the empty places of this ship are filled.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I hope your thoughts in this matter are clear. If you take her on as a servant, know that the Sith meet their end at the hands of their apprentices. It is not something I would wish to happen to you.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I hope your thoughts in this matter are clear. In saving her, you may destroy yourself. And do not mate with her. Whatever you may feel, whatever... urges consume you, do not let them control you. Such a union would breed... difficulties.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Oh, I think it is. Our lives are twined - I cannot have it twined with another.» [K2/Kreia]
- «We shall see. I trust your exile has taught you restraint and discipline in the ways of the flesh.» [K2/Kreia]
- «This one you have saved... has other masters. Though blind, she has ties to darkness.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Like the servant of Atris, this one... has other masters. Though blind, she has ties to darkness.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Her presence here is a threat to us. To you. Do not underestimate her... or her loyalty.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Then you are learning.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I doubt such acquisitions will prove easy - and they would not help you anyway.» [K2/Kreia]
- «To show mercy to a Sith is to invite a blade to your throat. You walk a fool's path, and I will not aid you in helping her.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Perhaps. I am not convinced.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Ah, without a home. Another exile.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Because it was its time? Perhaps you should ask her - it begs many questions.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Did he? And what do you make of that?» [K2/Kreia]
- «Her people are not prone to violence, war, or hatred - yet their planet is obliterated, scoured from the face of the galaxy, and all that remains is a Sith.» [K2/Kreia]
- «The Mandalorians were right to respect you on the field of battle. The Jedi are gone, vanished, now an entire planet of Force Sensitives wiped clean of life. And now this slice of the galaxy is blind. It is no coincidence, the two events are tied.» [K2/Kreia]
- «I fear you are right. And I fear it may prove more than that. War... is a hunger. And there are spirits in the galaxy whose hunger is never satisfied. But there is little to be done about it now. Watch the seer carefully - she may reveal more.» [K2/Kreia]
- «You are right to trust your instincts. Something is wrong... it is only a matter of discovering what. And why. If your instincts lead you to an answer, seek me out. Perhaps we will discuss more.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- «It may be a trap - but traps work both ways. This Visquis - his kind are spread through the lower reaches of Nar Shaddaa, and he may have information. But the choice is yours. If you go, you will have to go alone.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «Of course. It makes detecting a Jedi difficult.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «A simple question. To which I ask another - why should a Jedi want to hide?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- «She was told to meet alone. We cannot disrupt their meeting until the alien reveals the information he has.» [K2/Landing_Rights] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- «What is the machine saying?» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «We seem to have found it.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «It is no coincidence. There is some larger plan at work here. And we are walking into it. This is too convenient to be anything but a trap.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]

**[K2/Malachor_V]**
- «It has been some time.» [K2/Malachor_V] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Because now I understand why the exile did what she did.» [K2/Malachor_V] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «There is much to be done.» [K2/Malachor_V]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- «Signals? What signals?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Is all in readiness?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «My only concerns are for the one you escort to Onderon, Mandalorian. Would you do any less for one of your clan?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «If by 'apart' you mean scattered, broken, and lost, then yes - you are correct.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Ah, yes. The great crusade - after the first one was ended by Revan and the Jedi. Such a defeat was merciful, an echo of the end, when your ships were in flames, crushed in the grip of Malachor V. But I do not need to remind you of such things.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Indeed? The future is always in motion, it is a difficult thing to see. Perhaps there will be no new age, Mandalore, no great Mandalorian crusade. Perhaps your people fought their last battle at Malachor V, and you have been dying ever since, a quiet death that will last centuries. And perhaps all that remains will be what I see before me: a man, wounded by a Jedi, encased in a Mandalorian shell, haunted by the thought of being the last of the Mandalorians.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «No, Mandalore, you are wrong. I hope that it is you who will keep the one I travel with safe. You are loyal, and you have served many Masters... even following them into darkness.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «No, Mandalore, you are wrong. I hope that it is you who will keep the one I travel with safe. You are loyal, and you have served many Masters... even when they abandoned you.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Do you wonder where she wanders now, Mandalore? Why she cast you down, left you broken, at the edge of the galaxy?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Do you wonder where he wanders now, Mandalore? Why he betrayed you at the end, cast you down, left you broken, at the edge of the galaxy?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Do you wonder where she wanders now, Mandalore? Why she gave you your orders, then abandoned you at the edge of the galaxy?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I know many things, and I can answer the question that burns within your shell, Mandalore. But there is a price - you must keep the one I travel with safe.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «She is important to me - more important than anything.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Show the same loyalty you have shown in the past, Mandalore. If there is a Mandalorian crusade, let it be for something that will carry your people's memory into the future, so when there are no more Mandalorians, at least their honor will remain. The one I travel with has walked your same path - and I ask that when the end comes, that you remember that kinship, even if it seems there is nothing else left.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- «Wait a moment... I sense - there is a disturbance in the camp. Our enemy has tracked us here... I did not expect them so soon - how did they get here, I wonder? Regardless, we must eliminate them all. None of them can escape. Our whereabouts must remain a secret. Let us join the battle. Our "allies" will need our help.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- «You underestimate the Force, Mandalorian. I sense that we may still get to Master Kavar in time. I sense there is something... stirring on the moon itself. Tell me, have your sensors picked up anything from Dxun?» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «The Sith forces must be stopped. Otherwise the Mandalorian is right - Master Kavar and Queen Talia won't survive this day.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Those transmissions are the enemy. They are linked to the fate of Onderon. They must be stopped. Otherwise the Mandalorian is right - Master Kavar and Queen Talia won't survive this day.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «And this is why a common soldier will never triumph against a Jedi. Your military 'tactics' are nothing compared to the Force. It is essential, and inevitable, that we face both enemies at the same time.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «You... you are correct.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «You must choose who will lead the expedition through the jungle to find our enemies and defeat them.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «You should send two others to go with the leader. Who else will accompany the expedition?» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «And who else?» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Yes... good choice.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «If you say so.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Hmmm... Okay.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «So be it.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «As you say.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Fine.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Not the most subtle choice...» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «It's your decision.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Excellent choice.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «Now, are you certain of your choices?» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- «[[../Master of the Palace#Party» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- «We should conserve our strength for more important matters.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Whatever course you wish to take, aiding this woman may be useful. We may gain Vaklu's favor or learn more of his plans. Either way, helping Anda should be carefully considered.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Holding vendettas is a weakness. An open mind can unlock many doors.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «General Vaklu may be at odds with us now, but it need not always be that way.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- «Loyalty can prove an effective wall to your talents. But there may be a way to undermine it... if one looks for it.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Mira]**
- «::Feel the currents of Nar Shaddaa, the ebb and flow of life. Where one creature feels pain, it lashes out...::» [K2/Mira]
- «:: Feel the currents here on Nar Shaddaa, the ebb of life. A simple kindness can be given to another. ::» [K2/Mira]
- «:: This is the Force. And all our choices, from the greatest to the smallest, affect each other. And the echoes travel. ::» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- «You speak of this place as if it is familiar to you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «This moon... it teems with life. It is difficult to center oneself.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «It does not matter where we go... if what we seek is here, we shall come upon it in due time.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «The bounty is a waste of our efforts. All that matters is the Jedi - the intentions of the thugs of this moon are of no consequence.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «If you are so certain of your path then do what you will; I care not.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «If you are so certain of your path then do what you will, servant of Atris.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Finding a Jedi or anyone else touched by the Force here will be difficult... the mass of people, the rush of their emotions... it makes detection difficult.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «But this moon does not get any smaller while we wait. This sector is as good as any place to begin our search, so let us begin.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- «Enough. This moon does not get any smaller while we debate. This sector is as good as any place to begin our search, so let us begin.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- «Something feels wrong here... a great disturbance here in orbit... and again on the planets below.» [K2/Onderon]
- «There were no settlements here. Those clearings were most likely once craters... or crash sites.» [K2/Onderon]
- «This is Dxun, where the Mandalorians began their crusade against the Republic... the remains of whatever outposts you detected here are military ones. We should be careful.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Much is buried here... and there is much that should remain buried.» [K2/Onderon]
- «There may be a means to get to Onderon by another route. The Force has guided us here for a reason. We should explore our surroundings. There is... something here.» [K2/Onderon]
- «Nevertheless, we should explore our surroundings... and that nearby outpost would be as good a place as any to begin.» [K2/Onderon]
- «If we hope to get to Onderon and leave this moon, then we need to explore this place. We landed here for a reason, I am certain of it.» [K2/Onderon]
- «I have a feeling the ship will not be repaired until our business here is concluded. Do I make myself clear?» [K2/Onderon]
- «This is where the Mandalorian Wars began. She fought here once, and there are things here she must see.» [K2/Onderon] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Do you speak of all your battles? Or are there some you wish to forget?» [K2/Onderon]

**[K2/Peragus_II]**
- «:: Awaken. ::» [K2/Peragus_II]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- «Do as they say... I sense we will come to no harm.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The loss of your weapons shall be a temporary thing only - and it is necessary. There is much to be gained here... without violence.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It is a training ground. For Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Yes, it bears the semblance of an academy... but where are all the students? Curious.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It is a place hidden from the galaxy, like the academy on Dantooine. But this place... oh, Atris... you have been clever.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It's none of your concern.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «And what is it about this place that causes you such fear?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «No, I do not... not in the way you seem to.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Stop struggling... let me follow the current, deep... deeper... to its source.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Ah, with the fear... is mingled guilt... it squirms in you like a worm. And the why... ah, and there is its heart. You surprise me - I could not feel it before... your feelings are a powerful shield, indeed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Do not worry, "Atton." If she is Jedi, she will forgive. And if she is not, she will not care.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Do not worry, 'Atton.' If he is a Jedi, he will forgive. And if he is not, he will not care.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «...think less of you? I hardly think that's possible. Still, there is no shame in what you ask. We all wage war with the past. And it leaves its scars. I will not speak of yours, Atton, but there is a price for such things.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «There are those who wage war, and those who follow them. You are a crude thing, murderer, but you have your uses.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You know how important this woman we travel with is - even one such as you can feel it. You will serve her... until I release you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You will not. If you do, then my silence will be broken. And then, Atton, you will be broken. You fear the Jedi and rightly so. If Atris learns of your... choices, you will never leave this place. But whatever fear you hold of the Jedi, know that if you disobey me, that my punishment will make you beg for the death that has long hounded you. Wipe the fear from your mind. You will not find blind obedience a difficult Master... you chose it once. You will learn to embrace it again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «No game of dejarik can be won without pawns... and this may prove to be a very long game. You are a slippery one, your thoughts difficult for even one such as I to read. I suspect the self-loathing that squirms within you gives you a curious strength. Your spirit, as diseased as it is, refuses to allow you to give up, no matter what threats you face... and whatever wreckage you leave behind you. I feel you have crossed our path for a reason... perhaps even you, at the right moment, may be able to turn aside disaster. If so, your potential is not yet spent.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Perhaps. But someone has to fly the ship. And the Force is a hard thing to predict. You have crossed our path for a reason. Our path brought us here for a reason... and now I know why. The past is here, and it must be met before the future can be set in motion.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «No - I have wasted enough time with you. Sleep, murderer - and be silent. I need no distractions. A critical moment approaches.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Prestige_classes]**
- «Ah, you are here. You felt it, did you not?» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- «The time to hide your presence is coming to a close, and you will need to increase your training. You are ready. Your training must increase, and there are higher mysteries you must learn. But only you must know the path you will take, I cannot choose it for you.» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- «Very well, I shall wait. Return when you are ready.» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- «It is not some great test you require to be what you strive to be - it is only your decision to find that path that matters. From here on, you guide your destiny. But in order to take the next steps, you must face your past and put it to rest.» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- «Is it battle that stirs you, to meet an enemy blade upon blade? Such is the way of the greatest of Jedi warriors, the weapon masters. Or perhaps it is investigating the mysteries of the galaxy, seeking out injustice and harm, and bringing it into the light? Such is the way of the Jedi Watchmen in the time of Ulic Qel-Droma… and Exar Kun. Or perhaps your way lies upon the ancient mysteries… and to teach others the ways of the Force, as I have, the way of the Jedi Masters.» [K2/Prestige_classes]
- «Is it battle that stirs you, to meet an enemy blade upon blade? Such is the way of the greatest of the Sith Marauders, who seek strength in war… and their rage. Or is it the ways of darkness and shadow that you seek, the power that comes from striking unseen and sensing weaknesses in others… the ways of the assassins of the Sith. Or perhaps your way lies with the Sith… and the heart of their darkness. To recognize what strength is, and bring that strength to those that follow you… and those that defy you. The way of a Sith Lord.» [K2/Prestige_classes]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- «We must go to Dantooine, to the Enclave. There is something there that you must hear, if you are to understand. I have no time for questions - and any answers will have to wait until we stand within the Enclave together.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «What is left of the Jedi has felt what has happened - the death of Master Kavar.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «What is left of the Jedi has felt what has happened - the death of Master Vrook.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «What is left of the Jedi has felt what has happened - the death of Zez-Kai Ell.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «No... what is left of the Jedi.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «You will find what remains in the ruins of the Enclave on Dantooine.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «This matter has come full circle - and there is something there that you must hear, if you are to understand.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It... it is different, it has been some time.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Forgive me, but I need to rest. Go on... the Council awaits. I will remain here.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Yes, afraid for you. As I always have been.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I will be fine here... whatever answers the Council have are for you alone.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I am... tired. The journey has been a long one, and I need to center myself.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Know that much may happen here, but above all, do not forget this - you may trust in me. We cradle each other's lives, and what threatens one of us, threatens us both. And if you find you cannot trust me, trust in your training. Trust in yourself. Never doubt what you have done. All your decisions have brought you to this point.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «And now, perhaps, they shall see what you have become.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «The years have not been kind to us both, it seems.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «But perhaps now, they will see the truth at last.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «At last, you could hear.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «You were whole.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «And at last, you saw.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Enough!» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Step away from her.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Step away!» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «She has brought truth, and you condemn it? The arrogance! You will not harm her. You will not harm her ever again.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Die? No - became stronger, yes.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «As you would pass judgment on her, I have come to pass judgment on you all.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Do you wish to feel the teachings born of the Mandalorian Wars? Of all wars, of all tragedies that scream across the galaxy? Let me show you - you, who have forever seen the galaxy through the Force. See it through the eyes of the exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «How could you ever hope to know the threat you face, when you have never walked in the dark places of the galaxy - faced war and death on such a scale. If you had traveled far enough, rather than waiting for the echo to reach you, perhaps you would have seen it for what it was. There is a place in the galaxy where the dark side of the Force runs strong. It is something of the Sith, but it was fueled by war. It corrupts all that walks on its surface, drowns them in the power of the dark side - it corrupts all life. And it feeds on death. Revan knew the power of such places... and the power in making them. They can be used to break the will of others... of Jedi, promising them power, and turning them to the dark side. Did you never wonder how Revan corrupted so many of the Jedi, so much of the Republic, so quickly? The Mandalorian Wars were a series of massacres that masked another war, a war of conversion. Culminating a final atrocity that no Jedi could walk away from... save one. And that is what I sought to understand. How one could turn away from such power, give up the Force... and still live. But I see what happened now.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It is because you had no choice.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It is because you were afraid.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «And so here lies all that remain of the Jedi. It is a powerful thing you have achieved here... you have brought death again to Dantooine.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Is it as you expected?» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Yes, and that is why this place is empty.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Yes, and that is I, though I am not of them any longer.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I had thought you were the last of the Jedi, as did the Sith. But there are more of them than you know.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «You are correct - time is something that neither of us has. But for now, we must speak.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Your actions have crippled the Order, perhaps destroyed them.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «No... perhaps... it is difficult to say. For every Jedi slain, for every Sith slain, another rises. But the Order is wounded, yes.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Oh, yes. Your hate has destroyed them - you should be proud of the blood you have spilled, the lives you have ended. But before enjoying this victory - there is something we must discuss first.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Because there is something we must discuss.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Oh no, there is a purpose in such things. That is what we are here to discuss.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «The destruction of the Order, the Masters, it was not an end in itself. I did not expect them to still live, their presence was... knowledge I did not possess. But now this has been corrected, and now the sides of this conflict are as I had thought them to be. There are no more unknowns. And what you have done - it is not enough.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Although I did not expect them to still live, I had hoped you would learn something from the Jedi Masters as they fell before you. Not just of battle, but of yourself... and the Force.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I must know if killing them, if revenge brought you any measure of satisfaction. If seeing them dead has settled the disquiet within you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Did I wish to see them dead? No, defeated perhaps.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Because it matters to me, in a way that never mattered to the Jedi, to the Council when they cast you out. You must understand - I did not wish the Jedi dead. Defeated... perhaps.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «That is your wish - not mine. There is strength in defeating an enemy, not slaying them.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I merely wished them to see that they and their teachings were wrong. That one could not truly understand the Force simply by adhering to the Jedi Code. All I have ever trained have been failures to them, students who went to fight the Mandalorians, who fell to the dark side, who abandoned their training. To see one that had the strength to best them, that is a moment I will not forget. Yet, it has not been as satisfying as I had hoped. To best one in battle is one thing. To defeat them without striking a blow - that was my hope. Regardless... it had to be done. To have such powerful Jedi still live, still be felt in the Force even on such worlds as they had chosen, was a threat that had to be ended.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «To the Sith? No, not to the Sith - perhaps not in the way you would think.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «That is not important. First - let us return to my question. If by killing these Jedi, if you achieved any measure of peace.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Let us return to my question. If by killing these Jedi, if you achieved any measure of peace.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It is important to me - and I wish an answer, not more questions from you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It was as I thought. You have failed me. Completely, and utterly.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «I have taught you to hear the Force again, shown you the contrast, and yet still you do not understand.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «This is what you have wrought. Countless murderers, slayers, assassins, born of war that has as always, has taught the wrong lesson. You showed them life without the Force - and instead of showing them truth, power, all you showed them was how the galaxy may die. You are responsible for all of this. Even now, events spiral towards destruction, and there is nothing that can be done because you refuse to listen, to understand. You have seen the effects you have on those close to you, heard the echoes scream across dead planets, and watched as your strength has grown. Yet it is for nothing. To have the Jedi Masters brought low by such a failure, there is no victory in that. You have not heard a thing I have taught, and for all I have said, you have never learned to listen.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «Vrook was right to come here, though he did not recognize the connection until too late. This place will hide you from the Sith for a time - enough to do what must be done. You were my last hope, the only one who could change what is to come. And now you have left me nothing. I shall teach you no longer. Our bond remains, but that is all. Stay here and die, apprentice, among the wreckage of all that remains of the Jedi. It is a fitting grave until the Sith come to end you... to end everything. And as you lie here, I pray you will listen... and finally awaken.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: Vrook was right to come here, though he did not recognize the connection until too late. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: All life is connected by the Force. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: You have felt it in your companions, your choices affecting theirs, their actions mirroring yours. Imagine such incidents, spreading outwards. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: From the smallest of actions, the smallest of cruelties and kindnesses, great tragedies are made. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: The wounds inside people, the wounds suffered by planets... both cause echoes, heard and felt through the Force. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: And these echoes build, and all that can hear them shall become deafened... or die. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: Choose the right moment, create the right echo, and then all shall be destroyed. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: And there are those that feed on such deaths. And when no life is left, they shall consume themselves. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: You have taught them to bond with others, and then feed on others through that bond. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: What you have brought is the death of all who can feel the Force. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: It is your gift to the galaxy, exile. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «:: And unless you hear it and silence the echo you have caused, then every living thing, everywhere, that is touched by the Force... will die. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- «It is done - she is no more.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Take me to Atris. She will have the strength to do what the Council cannot.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- «Why did you do such a thing? Giving in to your feelings over such a small matter. They would be better served elsewhere.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «And what would you rather do?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «The Force binds all things. The smallest push, the smallest touch, sends echoes throughout life. These acts of cruelty may have more severe repercussions than you know... or can see.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Indeed? Listen - feel the echo.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Cruelty leads to suffering. And when one suffers, it is the way of life to spread suffering. The suffering within builds, until its sound is all one hears. And when a kindness is offered, it is punished. And a greater darkness is served. From one act can come tremendous power, when the echo has traveled as far as it can. Send a great echo, and power will come to you. The day shall come when you can test your strength, I promise you.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Why did you do such a thing? Such kindnesses will mean nothing, his path is set. Giving him what he has not earned is like pouring sand into his hands.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «And would that be a kindness? What if by surviving another day, he brings a greater darkness upon another?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «The Force binds all things. The slightest push, the smallest touch, sends echoes throughout life. Even an act of kindness may have more severe repercussions than you know or can see. By giving him something he has not earned, perhaps all you have helped him become is a target. Seeing another elevated often brings the eyes of others who suffer. And perhaps in the end, all you have wrought is more pain. And that is my lesson to you. Be careful of charity and kindness, lest you do more harm with open hands than with a clenched fist.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «Good. Mind what I have said. Use your power, but in its proper place.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
