- «Very well. But mind what I have said. Use your power, but in its proper place.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: Your thoughts are disturbed... I can feel them from a great distance, like a shiver running through you. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: It is Nar Shaddaa, the true Nar Shaddaa, that you feel around you. It is this moon, with the metal and machines stripped away and the currents of the Force laid bare. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: I am surprised you can feel it. I feared the damage to you had deadened you to such perceptions... What you feel is the echo of the minds of these creatures within the Force. Their anger... their greed... their desperation. It is life. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: One might as well heal the universe... but such manipulation is possible, yes. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: Strange? Perhaps, perhaps not. It is but is the vibration of minds driven by life's struggles - the struggle to feed, to take, to mate... to fight. It is the way of things. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: One might as well move the universe... but such manipulation is possible, yes. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: It requires that one be able to feel the critical point within the fractured mass... and know how to strike it in such a way that the echoes travel to your intended destination. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: Have you learned nothing? Healing is manipulation, and if you do not realize it yet, then you will discover that an act of healing depends largely on your perspective. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: Not in the sense you understand it - the ability to fool the minds of others, to dominate them on a massive scale that you speak of is not achieved best through raw power. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: For as long as it lasts. Like life, such waking moments within the Force are rare, waiting for the right moment when the critical point is struck, and the sound rises.... ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: Manipulation is done through propelling events... or selected ones... into motion. It is done through teaching, through example, and through conviction. And the greatest of victories are not manipulations at all, but simply awakening others... to the truth of what you believe. Of hearing it echoed around you, in life. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- «:: But let us be silent... words and thoughts are distractions. Feel this moment, for as long as it will last. Feel life, as it is, with the crude matter stripped away. ::» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- «This may be an opportunity for us. If we can make life miserable enough for the refugees, they will capitulate. The Exchange will be a valuable ally to us.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «And you haven't already? You weaken yourself by pretending that morality is important to you. You must learn to recognize your true self.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «You allow yourself to be enslaved by morality. You must learn to free yourself of these arbitrary limitations.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Then you will come to realize its wisdom.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «Ah, you are learning quickly.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- «It is a waste of time to reason with you.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- «Perhaps Chodo Habat should turn his eyes to his own people, if they truly suffer so.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Now, perhaps we will be able to rest uninterrupted.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «I do not have the years required - nor the desire to indulge you.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Yes... and what are they without the Force? Take the greatest Jedi Knight, strip away the Force, and what remains? They rely on it, depend on it, more than they know. Watch as one tries to hold a blaster, as they try to hold a lightsaber, and you will see nothing more than a woman - or a man. A child.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Do not be surprised. In many ways, even you are more capable than a Jedi. You could survive where they could not simply because you do not hear the Force as they do. It is irony of a sort - and it is why I tolerate your presence now.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «She has been gone from war some time. It is conflict that strengthens us... and isolation that weakens us, erodes us. Add to that that she turned away from war, did all that she could to forget it, and the last piece clicks into place. But we have spoken enough of this - and we do her a disservice by not speaking of this while she is present.» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It is difficult to say. I feel we came to Telos for a reason, but we may have spent too much time here already. Even if the Harbinger was destroyed at Peragus, more Sith could already be on their way. Still, there is a chance we might learn of other Jedi here, on the planet's surface. Jedi who might help us restore your abilities or sever the link between us.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «It is unlikely, but we shall see.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «He may be right. But whatever we do, we should move quickly.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «That was ill done. What possessed you of the need to have him killed?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Indeed, but all the same, he was willing to surrender them at the threat of injury.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «I see. From where I stood, it seemed to me that you were the one doing the pushing.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «I can see that. However, had you left him alive, we might have had use of him in the future. It is far better to have such a person, even as pathetic a creature as Opo Chano, to take advantage of in the future. Only kill when there is no further advantage to be gained, and no disadvantage to yourself.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «True, he will not be able to run to Lieutenant Grenn, but I feel that he was sufficiently frightened that he would keep silent.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Good, now let us be gone from here, before Grenn finds our involvement in this.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «It won't make any difference unless we get away from here before the TSF comes.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «I do not approve of this alliance we have formed with Chodo Habat and his Ithorians.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Nonetheless, you should heed my advice.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Habat has an agenda, and he hopes to tie you into it, to use you to his own ends.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «Be that as it may, it would be best if you avoided such needless entanglements. You are too valuable to be caught up in the struggles of this planet.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «True, he may be able to help us locate the Ebon Hawk, but still, you must be vigilant.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- «I cannot force you to listen to reason, only hope that you will grow past these infantile delusions of right and wrong.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- «Your speech is filled with "maybes" and "perhaps." You are bold to make promises of healing, while the world under your care burns and dies. You may be able to help, but there is always a price, is there not?» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- «Your speech is filled with "maybes" and "perhaps." You are bold to make promises of healing, while the world under your care burns and dies. Consider carefully. You have already begun to heal, to feel the Force again. If so, then the offer is worthless.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- «Healing a dead planet is one thing, Chodo Habat, and healing a Jedi severed from the Force is quite another.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- «You speak well enough, Chodo Habat, but perhaps you see all this as your chance to exert your own hold over others.» [K2/Residential_082_West]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- «That was not the most pleasant landing I've endured. Next time, we should perhaps seek out a more reputable pilot.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «Yes, our current situation is a vast improvement.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «The Force is strong here. Whether Chodo and his herd has anything to do with that, is another matter. Can you feel anything?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «That is good. As a breeze may swiftly turn to a gale, you are slowly beginning to be reopened to the Force.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- «I see. Even that may be a good sign, perhaps what you feel is the emptiness of the planet, its pain. We should press on.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- «He is too dangerous to leave alive. As distasteful as it is, it might be best to silence him forever. Until he's dead, all of Onderon is in peril.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Ah, the wound is not a mortal one... Though it has been some time since I exercised my healing powers, there is a shred of life within you still. A gift from your Master, no doubt. Awaken, Colonel Tobin. Your part in this is not over.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Colonel Tobin, I am with Vaklu. The war has gone against him... he sent me to rescue you, to tell you must make haste off-planet. The Jedi have struck. They had a secret Academy buried on Telos, and they are showing themselves at last. We are all in danger.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «So we all thought. But they have hidden themselves on Telos.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «Indeed... this war is far from over.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- «We should return to the Ebon Hawk now and plot our next course of action.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- «They are Echani. It is not unusual for their children to share similar features from the same parents.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Indeed? A most curious reason for a war.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «But were the choices you had dictated by your training? A curious question, difficult to answer.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Yes... to teach, one needs students. I have seen few of those since our arrival.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «So... Atris resides within the frozen heart of this world.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «If these relics predate Ossus, then she has saved much knowledge... and much of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Ah... a curious thing. What lies behind it?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Did she? Perhaps it is a... meditation chamber of some sort.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Hmmm. Perhaps it is a.... meditation chamber of some sort.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «To claim to know anything of Revan's choices or what lay in his heart when Malak fell is conceit, servant of Atris. And whether Revan had any choice in the matter at all is something else you should consider. The Force is a powerful thing to wield... or deny.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Ah, but at what point does the power the Force exerts submerge any attempt at choice, or free will? You have taken a complicated question, servant of Atris, and you have trivialized it with your answer... and lack of experience.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Jedi holocrons. Yet warm to the touch, you said?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Ah... of course they did. A simple misunderstanding, no doubt.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «It is like a cloud, a mist that drifts from living creature to creature, set in motion by currents and eddies. It is the eye of the storm, the passions of all living things turned into energy, into a chorus. It is the rising swell at the end of life, the promise of new territories and new blood, the call of new mysteries in the dark.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «It is standing atop the summit of a great mountain, the winds tearing about you, then finding yourself buried alive - trapped, helpless, and alone. It is like having the energy of youth... then feeling the cloak of years fall upon you, and knowing you are weak, fragile, and a thing easily discarded. It is knowing what you want to say and never finding the words. It is a chorus, replaced with silence. Hearing teachings without meaning. It is like a beloved pupil to whom you have shared everything, sacrificed everything, and then having them turn from you... and forget all you were.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Did you find what you came for?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I... felt as much. Quite strongly, in fact. I suspected there was something from your past here - something unresolved.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Are you certain you are ready to depart? I sense some... unrest... in you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I had thought that you would bring you some peace. Yet I still sense some unrest in you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «The ship. That is not what I meant. My question was directed more to you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «There was something from your past here - something unresolved.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I feel we did not come to this place by chance - you were led here. This woman who resides here - she did something to you once... something that hangs upon you still?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Whatever her charm, or lack thereof, you must deal with it. Unresolved events from our past can create wounds in the present, and the future.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Ah, I see it now. The act has left its marks.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Very well - though I can not be blamed for the intensity of your thoughts on this matter... or the depths to which they reach.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Is it? Perhaps so... and perhaps not.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Be warned... unresolved events from our past can create wounds in the present, and the future.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «And more importantly, they can distract you... weaken you. It could prove fatal against the enemies we face.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «There is a Jedi here, perhaps. In that, you are correct. Yet there are no students, and this woman... this Atris... surrounds herself with those who cannot feel the Force. Curious.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Plans are fragile things and life often dashes expectations to the ground. Perhaps students will come to her in time. For now, she is surrounded by those who cannot feel the Force.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «No... her servants are not Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Yes... her servants are not Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Their minds are walls, trained to resist tricks of the mind. This discipline blinds them to the Force as well, even if they were Force Sensitive.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Invade the mind of another? It is not something done carelessly... or when there is nothing to be gained.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Ah... killing such a one could prove difficult. And unwise. Yet she... distracts you, that is clear. And that may be reason enough. Such distractions could prove fatal against the enemies we face.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Direct action is not always the best way. It is a far greater victory to make another see through your eyes than to close theirs forever. Yet all that lives feels. And where something feels, there is weakness.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Why you, of course.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «You are the gravity around which all her actions rotate. You exert a stronger influence than you know. Be her foil, her challenge... and eventually she will see things your way.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Oh, yes. Natural leaders do such things to followers... whether they be simple criminals or old women such as myself.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Very well... let us depart.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «If that is all you wish to hear, then I have nothing more to say. Let us depart.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «He is only sleeping - it seems the journey here has fatigued him.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Let us keep moving. There is nothing more to be gained here.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Very well - but we should not remain here too long.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Echani training?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Don't be a fool - Atris stole the ship and the droid.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- «You are to be commended for making it this far.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «You've revisited the dark moments of your past, and now you must face the present.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Your confusion is natural. The others and I will help you understand.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Atton, I've had enough of your snide contempt!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «I will protect myself from this foul-mouthed ruffian!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Stay out of this Bao-Dur! This is a personal dispute between Atton and myself.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «The three of you would challenge me? You sorely underestimate the power of the force.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Your "friends" are all arrayed against me. Will you stand for this?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «I will be honest with you. I have fallen to the dark side. Does this change your decision?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «You of all people would judge me so? Am I not worthy of redemption?» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «So you will do nothing? Apathy is death. Worse than death, because at least a rotting corpse feeds the beasts and insects.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Apathy is death.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «You've succeeded in this trial. I am impressed.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «We have been linked together in this manner for a reason. The dangers you faced here should have illuminated your need for fellowship.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «The dangers you faced in this tomb were real, but these images of the past served to prepare you for your future.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «You overestimate the power of the tomb. Any change you feel is coming from within yourself. Instinctually, you know your true path. Trust in your feelings. They will lead you in conquering the many challenges that the future holds for you.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Sometimes, a momentary insight is worth lifetimes of experience.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «You may not yet understand what you learned here. That wisdom will come in the future.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Surely you have felt what awaits... events are shaping themselves about you, seeking to draw you into their center.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Take care not to give in to vanity and arrogance. This breeds complacency and stagnation, as exhibited by the Jedi Council. Their prolonged inaction led inevitably to their downfall. The galaxy will bend itself only to those of strength and conviction.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- «Search the room you are in. You should be able to unlock a passage that leads outside the tomb.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Shyrack_Cave]**
- «The power I felt coming through the cave is just ahead.» [K2/Shyrack_Cave]
- «As I told you before, you do not have the strength to enter this place - yet. You should return here later, when your power has grown.» [K2/Shyrack_Cave]
- «[You lack the strength to enter the tomb ahead. To pass here you must be strongly aligned to either the light or dark side of the Force.]» [K2/Shyrack_Cave]
- «[You lack the strength to enter the tomb ahead. To pass here you must gain more experience.]» [K2/Shyrack_Cave]
- «I believe you are strong enough to explore the tomb ahead. You will have to face the challenges of this tomb alone. Are you ready?» [K2/Shyrack_Cave]
- «Are you ready to enter the tomb?» [K2/Shyrack_Cave]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «Sion. He cannot be defeated... he is not a beast of flesh and blood. This is not a battle that can be won. Flee.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «There will be another time. But it is not now, not here, while Korriban runs through him.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Surface]**
- «Of course, apprentice. The huntress is with the exile. She has a special talent for... finding that which should remain lost. It also makes her difficult for me to sense... but not to a creature bred for such things.» [K2/Surface]
- «No, I have brought a beast to Malachor to deal with that one. If she survives, she will become stronger for it.» [K2/Surface]

**[K2/Telos]**
- «Who I am is not the question.» [K2/Telos]
- «Those are titles, words you cling to as the darkness falls around you.» [K2/Telos]
- «"Sith" is a title, yes, but like you, the title is not who I am. It is not what I believe. For you... it is different. Know that there was once a Darth Traya. And that she cast aside that role, was exiled, and found a new purpose. But there must always be a Darth Traya, one that holds the knowledge of betrayal. Who has been betrayed in their heart, and will betray in turn. You have bathed in the knowledge of the Sith. But there is not enough truth in such teachings... but it will be a step for you.» [K2/Telos]
- «Search your heart. It was never battle that called to you. Never battle that caused you to fall. Malachor V has touched many things, and it casts its echoes still.» [K2/Telos]
- «You betrayed yourself. Do not blame the exile. And unlike you and I, there is still a chance that one may be saved. The one that you cast out.» [K2/Telos]
- «Oh, she will come. But it will be too late to save either of us.» [K2/Telos] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «It is such a quiet thing, to fall. But far more terrible is to admit it.» [K2/Telos]
- «Your mistress awaits. She has much to share with you.» [K2/Telos]
- «You have done well. All is as I have foreseen.» [K2/Telos]
- «There must always be a Darth Traya. And if it will not be her, then I must assume that role, and as always, bring about the betrayal of the Jedi and the Sith.» [K2/Telos]
- «I am not here... not in the flesh, not in your mind. These holocrons hold much of the Sith... and they hold much of my teachings, from long ago.» [K2/Telos]
- «Save your questions. I am not here... not in the flesh, not in your mind. These holocrons hold much of the Sith... and they hold much of my teachings, from long ago.» [K2/Telos]
- «But you have much yet to learn. And great tests await you. The death of the past, the death of this false Jedi, was only part of it. One of the Sith Lords has come to Telos. You know why he has come here. And if he is not stopped here, now, then he shall lay waste to this planet. You must confront him. He is part of the past, and like this false Jedi, must be laid to rest. Only then shall you be ready for your final test. If not, then you shall die.» [K2/Telos]
- «Such arrogance.» [K2/Telos]
- «Indeed? Did you think I failed to recognize such? Save your threats for someone not willing to die.» [K2/Telos]
- «You have no choice... apprentice.» [K2/Telos]
- «You know where the final test lies. It is not here, not in the battle that will wage across the surface and skies of this dead planet. And know that if you do not come to me, if you run from this, then I shall sacrifice myself - and end both our lives through the bond we share. You know where you must travel. And there, I shall be waiting for you.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]**
- «Someone is coming.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]
- «We cannot stay in any one place too long. But our path has brought us here for a reason. I must meditate on this. In the meantime, we should rest.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy]**
- «:: When the exile enters the Trayus Academy, she will be faced with a choice. ::» [K2/Trayus_Academy]
- «:: When the exile enters Trayus Academy, he will be faced with a choice. ::» [K2/Trayus_Academy]
- «:: Show her every respect when she arrives in these halls, Lord Sion. This I command you. ::» [K2/Trayus_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- «At last you have arrived. Is Malachor as you remember?» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I have thought of this moment, more than you know. And I wondered, if here, at this ending between us, if you would care enough to try to save me - if a Jedi could find it within themselves to spare one who has fallen so far. I wanted you to say those words - for that I am grateful. But I do not want your mercy. I want you to break.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I know. But there is more than death in this galaxy, and you shall not find it easy. It was difficult to draw you here, but it had to be done. This place is your last test. It is the graveyard of the past, where you lost everything. It is the dark place in your mind that still echoes of failure. Now we shall see if you can overcome the weight of Malachor... and silence the echoes that beat from its heart.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «You no doubt have many questions. I would be a poor teacher if I did not give you the answers you seek here, now.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Indeed. Perhaps it is merely your perception of me that have changed. It is strange that you believe Malachor has not. But it has always been timeless to you, this place. And words have always been inadequate for the horrors that took place here.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I never destroyed Atris - she had destroyed herself. I merely stripped away the illusion, and brought her truth. Her teachings could not be allowed to continue. And like Malachor, she was part of your past, unresolved. She needed to be something you could confront - and defeat, one last time. It was part of your training. Part of what was needed to make you complete. And there must always be a Darth Traya. The galaxy needs its betrayers, especially in the times to come. She loved you, you know, as one loves a champion. You were all that she could not be.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Yes... it is all that is left unsaid upon which tragedies are built. More echoes, traveling through the Force.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Of course. Let us speak no more of such things... we shall let the dead lie, as the dead lie upon Malachor.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «It is said that the Force has a will, it has a destiny for us all. I wield it, but it uses us all, and that is abhorrent to me. Because I hate the Force. I hate that it seems to have a will, that it would control us to achieve some measure of balance, when countless lives are lost. But in you... I see the potential to see the Force die, to turn away from its will. And that is what pleases me. You are beautiful to me, exile. A dead spot in the Force, an emptiness in which its will might be denied.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I use it as I would use a poison, and in the hopes of understanding it, I will learn the way to kill it. But perhaps these are the excuses of an old woman who has grown to rely on a thing she despises.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Perhaps you were expecting some surprise, for me to reveal a secret that had eluded you, something that would change your perspective of events, shatter you to your core. There is no great revelation, no great secret. There is only you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «No, there were not. In times past and in times future, there are Jedi who will stop listening to the Force, those that will try to forget it, but maintain unconscious ties. And those, as in the past just as I, who have had the force stripped from them. But no Jedi ever made the choice you did.  To sever ties so completely, so utterly, that it leaves a wound in the Force. It was a mistake to try to make you feel it again, I see that now. There is no truth in the Force. But there is truth in you, exile. And that is why I chose you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «The apprentice must kill the Master - if you do not, I will kill you. If I do not, then all you have achieved will be as nothing, as empty, and as violent as Malachor itself.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Then you will break. And then, my apprentice, you shall die.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «More talk of machines and threats. If you would end Malachor, then do it. But it will not be a victory for you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «And of course, you must be willing to die, to kill us all. And your friends.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «You may hold Malachor in your grasp, but I hold the answers to your past and future in mine. Would you destroy us both before learning them? If so, then do it - for you have already failed me.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Yes, always. From the moment you awoke, I have used you. I have used you so that you might become strong, stronger than I. And I used you to make those who wounded me reveal themselves, so they could be killed by the Republic.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I used you to keep the Lords of the Sith from condemning the galaxy to death with their power unchecked. I used you to lure them to Telos, where they could be, at last, fought and killed. I used you to reveal Atris' corruption, so that her teachings could be ended before they began. I used you to gather the Jedi so they could be destroyed. And I used you to make those who wounded me reveal themselves, so they could be killed by the Republic.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «If you do not kill me, I shall end you. Strike me down, end this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Then finish this - kill me. Strike me down, and at last, end this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I died long ago - and now the circle is complete. Strike me down, and at last, end this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «You will not show me mercy. I will see you break before you do.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Good... you have strength. But you have yet to learn the full extent of power.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «It is done. At last, it is done. You are greater than any I have ever trained. By killing me here - you have rewarded me more than you can possibly know.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Save me? You already have - it is enough what you have done, from now into the future.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «No... no, many choices were there, but... you made the right ones.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «It is your choice. I had hoped you would follow Revan's path, but you and Revan are... different, and your path is your own. You may take one of the ships that orbit Malachor and depart this place. Or you may remain here on Malachor, and wait for the others, those touched by the Force, who will come in time. Or you may return to your exile, where your presence will no longer affect the actions of others. There is no dishonor in any of these choices... I only ask that you make the choice without regret.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Very well. There is nothing holding you here, not any longer.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Then you need no longer be. You have defeated Malachor... this place no longer holds meaning.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Many things do I see as I gaze here from the heart of Malachor. This place... channels such energies. If it matters to you, at this last moment, I shall look into the future, and tell you of what I see. It is my last gift to you, from one exile... to another.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Ah... yes. There is... truth in what you say.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «You travel with them for so long, yet you do not know them still. Feel them through the Force, feel what they feel, hear their thoughts and know them, as I fought to know you. They were the Lost Jedi, you know. The true Jedi, upon which the future will be built. They simply needed a leader, and a teacher.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «She will continue to hunt life, but it shall have a new edge to it - she shall become a predator. Her life shall be a long one, filled with battles and conflict, but in the end, bounty hunters will come for her... as she came for them. She will die on Ord Mantell, her death a badge of honor for her killers... before they, too, meet their end.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «She will stop hunting life, and instead, live it. She was not born to be a predator, despite her true father and the life she led within the shadow of Nar Shaddaa. She will miss you and think of you often. You, who awakened her, to what life is. She will live... but only for a time. Her death will occur in many years time on a forgotten planet, saving the lives of others. But it will be her choice, and she will have no regrets.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Many battles does that one have left in him... as Revan intended. A general needs an army, as he needs those he trusts. And Canderous is a loyal beast, no matter how much he is broken upon Revan's will. But you know this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «They will die a death that will last millennia, until all that remains is their code, their history, and in the end, the shell of their armor upon the shell of a man, too easily slain by Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «The blinded one shall return to her homeworld, and as she looked upon you, she shall look upon the surface of that world, and perhaps at last see what she was meant to see. After that, I do not know. I do know that you must leave her behind. Where you are destined, you must not take anyone you love. It was Revan's choice as well.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «The blinded one's heart has now been put to rest - vengeance no longer clouds her sight, and she shall be stronger for it. She will leave her memories of Katarr in the wreckage of the past, and instead, turn her eyes to the future that you have put before her.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «The blinded one shall return to her homeworld, and she shall look upon the surface of that world, and perhaps at last see what she was meant to see. Her life has been changed by your meeting, in ways that may not be felt for decades to come.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Salvation is a relative thing... but as you understand it, yes.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «If he leaves this place, he will leave all that is Jedi behind him at last. A heart can only handle so many betrayals before turning away entirely. He will become a Senator on one of the Mid-Rim worlds, and devote himself to the people there. He will be a wise, steady ruler - and he will not call upon the Force again. But his rule will grow to be a cold one, and he will find that the code of the galaxy pales in comparison to the failures of the code of the Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «He cannot help but love you, in his way. It is a pure, ideal love he holds, strengthened by your presence and your actions. If he leaves this place, he will leave the galaxy behind him. He will sit upon the new Council, reluctantly, as all good men do, and he will not forget the Jedi who had lost the Force, yet showed him the way to reclaim it. After that, I do not know. I do know that you must leave him behind. The same choice that Revan made: where you are destined, you must not take anyone you love. And of the ones who traveled with you... that is all I see.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «She cannot help but love you. It is a curious influence you have over her. If she leaves this place, she will leave battle behind her. She will take Atris' role as historian, and she will teach others of the Jedi who was not a Jedi... and who trained her to feel the Force. After that, I do not know. I do know that you must leave her behind. Where you are destined, you must not take anyone you love. It was Revan's choice as well.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «She will seek to forget you and this place, and instead give herself fully to battle. No longer will she be the last of the Handmaidens. She will put her dreams of the Jedi behind her, and return to judging, and punishing, the Jedi for being human.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «If she leaves this place, she will leave battle behind her... in no small part due to your influence. She will take Atris' role as historian, and teach others of the Jedi exile who gave up the Force... and became stronger for it.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Atton shall keep his murderer's heart. Many deaths shall he cause in the dark corners of the galaxy - always hunting, always finding prey. He shall grow hungry in a galaxy where there are few Jedi, and it will eventually consume him... but I shall say no more.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Atton is, as always, the fool. And the Force watches out for ones such as him, I feel. As it does for the old such as I.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «There is no love left in a heart such as that one. But he would die for you, yes. I do not believe one would die lightly for one one did not care about.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «He is a fool, and that should answer your questions. He has nothing to offer to one such as you - and even a fool such as Atton is not so ignorant of that fact.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «I would have killed the galaxy to preserve you. I would have let the galaxy die. You are more precious than you know; what you have taught yourself cannot be allowed to die.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «You are not Sith. Not truly. And it is for that that I love you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «You are not a Jedi. Not truly. And it is for that that I love you.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Their paths are unknown to me. Even the small one - who waits for you outside this place - I sense it has one last journey for you. You must go where Revan did, into the Unknown Regions, where the Sith, the true Sith, wait in the dark for the great war that comes.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «It is because he remembered what lay buried here - this place, its teachings. It paved the way to Korriban, you know, the remnants here.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «She came because she remembered what lay buried here - this place, its teachings. The remnants here paved the way to Korriban.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «And he came because Malachor, like Korriban, lies on the fringes of the ancient Sith Empire, where the true Sith wait for us, in the dark.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Have we? You thought that the corrupted remnants of the Republic, the machines spawned by technology that Revan led into battle were the Sith? You are wrong. The Sith is a belief. And its empire, the true Sith Empire, rules elsewhere.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «And Revan knew that the true war is not against the Republic. It waits for us beyond the Outer Rim. And he has gone to fight it, in his own way. He left the Ebon Hawk and its machines behind, for he knew he would not need them. And, like you, he knew he must leave all loves behind as well, no matter how deeply one cares for them.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «And Revan knew the true war is not against the Republic. It waits for us, beyond the Outer Rim. And she has gone to fight it, in her way. And she left the Ebon Hawk and all its machines behind, for she knew she would not need them. And, like you, she knew she must leave all loves behind as well, no matter how deeply one cares for them.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Because such attachments are not the way of the Jedi, and they would only bring doom to them both in the dark places where he now walks. It would have helped had he made her understand. But she was always strong-willed, that one, and did not understand war as Revan did.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Because such attachments would only bring doom to them both in the dark places where she now walks. It would have helped had she made him understand, but a hero of the Republic, no matter how brave, cannot understand war as Revan did.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Because I did not know where he had gone. If he had asked... would I have gone? I do not know. But he will need warriors, Sith and Jedi, any who can be sent after him into the depths of space, any who know the way. Perhaps you shall go there with him, and do battle at the end of all things.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Because I did not know where she had gone. If she had asked... would I have gone? I do not know. But she will need warriors, Sith and Jedi, any who can be sent after her into the depths of space, for any who know the way. Perhaps you shall go there with her, and do battle at the end of all things.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Instead, I remained here... and now show others the way.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «The Republic will fall, as it always has, a fall that will take millennia.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Telos shall recover, and Czerka shall make it a place for machines and sciences, it will run smooth and cold like a machine. But it shall not forget the time that Saul Karath orbited it and brought fire to it. It shall learn to defend itself against war, and it shall never again be caught defenseless.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Under the care of the herds of Ithor, the surface of Telos will bloom again, and its golden fields shall again harbor scientists and thinkers. And complacent and peaceful, it shall forget the time that Saul Karath orbited it and brought fire to its skies. But it shall be a homeworld again to others, who will stretch out across the galaxy and bring life.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Dantooine shall survive. The community you saved shall be the foundation upon which Dantooine shall be habitable again. They shall drive back the raiders, the Mandalorians, and all that strike at the Outer Rim. The Republic shall again establish their presence there, and shield it with its forces... And Dantooine shall heal, be safe, and its skies free. The ruins of the academy shall remain.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Dantooine shall lie in ruins, as was intended when Malak's fleet brought death to the planet. Its surface will become home to nomads and primitives, who will walk upon the ruins of the Jedi Enclave and not know upon the histories they tread.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Nar Shaddaa shall persist as it always has, but its evil and corruption shall grow. The Exchange shall rise in power, but only in the end to be consumed by the hunger of the true Lords of Nar Shaddaa who dwell upon the planet below. The Hutts will not long tolerate the Exchange, and without Goto among them, they shall feed upon themselves until there is nothing left.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Nar Shaddaa shall persist as it always has, but there will be a heart to the world where there was nothing before. Where once the lost and disposed were trapped there, now they will struggle and grow. From despair shall come hope.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Vaklu shall have a short reign, but Onderon's independence shall persist. As he fought the Mandalorians, his triumph over the Republic shall serve to preserve Onderon against the rest of the galaxy, and Onderon shall maintain its customs, its lore, its history. Its identity. And most of all, its victory shall give Onderon strength, so that the horrors of the Sith War and the Mandalorian Wars will not soon come to its surface again.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Queen Talia shall have a long reign, much good will come of it. She will, as she has, rule wisely and well. Onderon shall remain in the Republic, and the world shall prosper, though its people shall, over time, lose their customs in the ocean of the Republic and become the people of Onderon no longer.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- «Korriban shall be as it always was. A graveyard for the darkest of the Sith Lords, still whispering within their tombs. It shall always be a source of evil, spawning threats throughout the millennia. It, like Malachor, brushes the edges of the empire that waits in the dark. And like Malachor, the Sith have forgotten it... for a time. They will remember. Revan knew this.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

**[K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]**
- «That crystal is bonded to you. Through you it acquires its character and strength. And through it, your power is enhanced.» [K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]
- «Most interesting... your crystal does not perfectly reflect your current self. Remove it from your lightsaber, then ask me about it again.» [K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]
- «Let me focus on the crystal for a moment... There... now it is fully in tune with you again.» [K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]
- «Should your power increase or your resolve change, speak to me again about this matter and I will help you refocus your crystal.» [K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]
- «Is there something else you wished?» [K2/Upgrade_items,_lightsaber]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «The structures you see around you are the plundered tombs of the ancient Sith Lords. Each tomb was once infused with the history and heritage of the old Sith Empire, containing great mysteries and powerful relics of the Force. However, even the many traps could not long hold back the curious, the fools, and the weak. And so these tombs fell, spilling their secrets into the hands of those unable to comprehend or preserve them.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «The broken corpses before you are all that remain of the Sith on Korriban. I doubt there is much to be gained from looting these bodies. It would be best to leave them be.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Fool! You've disturbed the spirits of this place, and they have sent their guardians!» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Hssiss are semi-intelligent beasts, corrupted and strengthened by prolonged exposure to the dark side. As creatures of the Force, they have a limited ability to mask their presence. Hssiss are drawn to suffering and carnage. They must have fed on all the corpses left over from the war here on Korriban. The angry phantoms of the Sith, too weak to influence the sentient, have taken these Hssiss as thralls to their will.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Hmph! Hssiss are enough to humble even an arrogant one such as you. Test yourself if you must, but try not to die.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Ah, the arrogance and ignorance of youth. There will come a day when you'll wish you'd learned from mistakes in the past.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «This was the tomb of Tulak Hord, known as the greatest lightsaber duelist of the Sith Lords.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «His skill was considered remarkable even in his time, when many true lightsaber masters lived.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «If you were to face an ancient Sith Lord in combat, you would learn that we are as children playing with toys compared to the prowess of the old Masters.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «That is unknown. But supposedly he created a holocron to teach his technique to other Sith. The holocron would have been laid to rest in his tomb. Unfortunately, Tulak Hord's tomb was among the first penetrated by the graverobbers of the new Sith order. If the holocron has survived, I doubt anyone living would know its location.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Ahead lies the Tomb of Naga Sadow, successor to Marka Ragnos, and the Sith Lord responsible for nearly conquering the Republic in the Great Hyperspace War a millennia ago.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «More recently, this tomb was where Revan confronted Uthar Wynn, the leader of the Sith Academy. When Revan left Korriban, the Sith Academy was thrown into turmoil - with their leader gone, many fought for the right to rule. And so the Sith here turned on each other, resulting in the carnage you can see covering the surface of this valley.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Before you is the tomb of the great Sith Lord Marka Ragnos, a half-breed who possessed tremendous strength, both physically and in the Force.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Ragnos held power for over a century, using his cunning to turn his enemies against each other. His death left a great vacuum of power. We're standing close to the spot where Naga Sadow first confronted Ludo Kressh to vie for domination of the Sith. Their struggle nearly resulted in a civil war that would have torn the Sith apart before they ever threatened the Republic.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Yes. It is the way of the Sith. They must continually test their strength against each other... even if it means destroying themselves.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «As fate would have it, a pair of hyperspace explorers from Cinnagar landed on Korriban. Naga Sadow manipulated the Sith into believing they were a sign of impending Republic invasion. This fear resonated with many Sith who were discontent with the lack of expansion of the Sith Empire during the reign of Marka Ragnos. Thus Naga Sadow became Ragnos' successor.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «This way leads to the Tomb of Ajunta Pall, a fierce Sith Lord. According to legend, the blade proved more fearsome than the Master, leading to his demise.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Ajunta's dark specter lived on through the centuries, until Revan entered the Sith Lord's tomb in search of the blade.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Revan sent Ajunta Pall's spirit into oblivion and claimed the blade for herself.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Revan calmed the angry ghost of Ajunta Pall and showed him the path back to the light.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «One who has fallen so far, and done so much evil does not deserve redemption. In a way, such a turning from one's nature is cowardly, a betrayal of the self.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Perhaps. But redemption is a form of spiritual collapse, a fall few recover from.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Yes, redemption is merely a form of spiritual collapse, a fall few recover from.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Unknown. Some have suggested that the blade led Revan to her demise, as it did Ajunta Pall. That is only speculation, however. Where Revan wanders now... is knowledge that only Revan holds.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Unknown. Some have suggested that the blade led to Revan's demise, as it did Ajunta Pall. That is only speculation, however. Where Revan wanders now... is knowledge that only Revan holds.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Here you can just barely see the Sith archaeologists' efforts to uncover relics of the ancients.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «The new Sith order sought to progress quickly by finding objects of power. I can only imagine what was lost forever due to the carelessness of these excavators. Now the excavation has been almost completely undone by five years of wind and sand. So does Korriban protect its secrets.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «There is great power and dark energy within this cave.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «You are not yet strong enough to face what lies within this cave. We should return here when you are more powerful.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «I would advise you to finish your explorations within the Academy before venturing into the cave.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «You can expect more than these beasts within the Academy. Be prepared.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Kreia / Darth Traya (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- Peragus Medical Officer: «"Aside from the lone survivor, we recovered an old woman, no life signs. There was also a protocol droid and a utility droid on board - sent both down to maintenance while security sorts through the other items on the ship."» [K2/Administration_Level]

**[K2/Atton]**
- Atton: «I can't help but feel it - all of us can. Whatever that witch is doing to you, she's dragging you down into whatever hole she crawled out of.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Well... you're starting to get creases - almost thought you were that old witch for a second.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Well... you're starting to get creases - you could pass for the old witch's son.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I don't know what that witch did to you, but she's dragging you down into whatever hole she came out of. I'm only saying this because there's still a chance for you to stop this before whatever darkness she brought on you eats you alive.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «Well, it's good to see. You're a nice counterbalance to that old witch back there.» [K2/Atton]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- HK-47: «You see, I do not believe my Master knew who she was most of the time, which led to some confusing situations that could have otherwise been avoided.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Clarification: Suffice to say that my Master had her ups and downs, and in my opinion, traveled from a high point in her life to a low point. She developed qualities such as mercy, compassion, and even love for others. It was really rather revolting. Still, my Master had some remarkably efficient droid-like qualities which I admired. She, too, suffered memory problems as I did.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Clarification: Suffice to say that my Master had her ups and downs, and in my opinion, traveled from a high point in her life to a low point, and then back again to her true potential. And even as a meatbag, my Master still had some remarkably efficient droid-like qualities which I admired. She, too, suffered memory problems as I did.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Conjecture: I believe there was something about my Master's destination that she did not wish me to know.» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- Atton: «Hey, I just got out of prison. If we had a decent navicomputer, trust me, we'd be dropping out of hyperspace into the Nar Shaddaa Red Sector right now. After spacing that old witch of course.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Hanharr]**
- Hanharr: «The old woman knows the lesson of strength. Her strength is to fasten shackles from air, from the mind. She is a dangerous predator - because she hunts other predators.» [K2/Hanharr]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- Colonel Tobin: «You think to lie to me still? I will not be deceived again. I told him, you know... what the old woman told me. I told him of the Academy here... of the Jedi here. I... could not have kept it from him, even if I had wanted to.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Mandalore: «He means that Jedi witch you travel with – Kreia.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «Yes... the old woman on Onderon. She spoke of the Jedi Academy, here, on Telos... and my Master was forced to come here.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «He will create nothing... he will end this place. Your old woman has killed this planet, and with it, countless other worlds that depend on its survival.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Atton: «The Handmaiden took her. She thinks Kreia killed you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «Because that's the lie Kreia told her, that's why.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «The only thing that matters is the Handmaiden believes it. And she's going to react exactly how that old witch hoped she would. That's why she wanted the Handmaiden on board, you know. So she could use her to reach the Telos Academy whenever she wished - without needing the access codes.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Rescue]**
- Peragus Dock Officer: «"Regardless, still no luck accessing the navicomputer - it's been voice-locked. maybe by one of the corpses we found on the ship... like the old woman."» [K2/Rescue]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- Atton: «You're welcome, Kreia. You know, if I weren't such a crack pilot, we could have hit the shield wall or one of those rock faces.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Colonel Tobin: «Out of my way, old woman. This war can still be won and Onderon can be freed.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Yes, mistress: the Iridonian, the Echani-trained pilot, the exile, and the old woman.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Atton: «Think again, Kreia! Your dark influence will end!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- Darth Sion: «I have studied you, immersed myself in you. I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher. I know the fires that raged upon the Dxun moon while the Republic died around you. You know war. You know battle. And I know of Malachor. You know what it means to be broken. The one who travels with you will destroy you, as she did me. I can end it before it begins.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- Darth Sion: «I have studied you. I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher. I know the fires that raged upon the Dxun moon while the Republic died around you. You know war. You know battle. And I know of Malachor. You saw the heart of war, what Malachor wrought, yet you turned away from it.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Atris: «Kreia? Oh... that is not her name. She is gone, and is now beyond your reach, exile. Where she has gone, only I shall follow... after I have dealt with you.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «The old woman you traveled with, finally made me... listen to myself, to the galaxy. She said that you would come here, and that you would face me in battle.» [K2/Telos]
- Atris: «Because she has gone to Malachor. She is waiting for you there. But you will not survive Telos. Nothing will. It is because the greatest of her apprentices comes to Telos. And he will destroy everything, just as he destroyed Katarr and all the Jedi gathered there.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden: «Kreia... she said the council had ended you. And all along, she was one of those who had sought to kill us.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]**
- Batu Rem: «You have goaded me once, and you shall not do so twice. But I shall dispose of all of you eventually. And an old woman, a fool, and a broken Jedi are no match for my skills.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]**
- Darth Sion: «[Success] You... are wrong. I have come farther than any of her apprentices since Revan... even you... and I am stronger for it.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Darth Sion: «[Failure] You are wrong. She respects power... I have come farther than any of her apprentices since Revan, and I am stronger for it.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Darth Sion: «It has been here for thousands of years. It is a place where the Sith teachings run strong... it is the threshold of the borders of an ancient empire. Kreia says it was a place of reflection for the ancient Sith... a gateway to their lands. It drew Lord Revan... and it calls to her as well. She said that the teachings here will lead one to the Sith... the true Sith... and all their shadowed worlds. This place led Revan to the graveyards of Korriban... and beyond.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Darth Sion: «Kreia, she will try to break you, to teach you how far someone can fall.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

- [context-resolved] Darth Sion: «Do not harm her. I command it. She... has earned this. She and I will meet again.» [K2/Sith_Academy] (anchor: Darth Sion: I have studied you. I know the paths you walked ...)

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Kreia / Darth Traya (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- Atton: «Nice outfit - what, you miners change regulation uniforms while I've been in here?» [K2/Administration_Level]

**[K2/Command_Deck]**
- Atton: «"You sense no one on board?" Sense any assassin droids creeping up behind us like last time?» [K2/Command_Deck]
- Atton: «Then what are we doing on this ship? We were better off in the facility! You two are supposed to be Jedi? You two are the worst Jedi I've ever met!» [K2/Command_Deck]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Azkul: «Return to me when you when you are ready for us to begin our assault. I warn you not to keep me waiting.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Mandalore: «Watch out for Azkul. I've known his kind before. He will attack you without hesitation if he believes you aren't working with him.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Handmaiden: «I applaud your conviction! A lesser person might have deceived these brutes to avoid battle.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «No more words. Defend yourself or perish!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Are you arrogant enough to think you can divide your attention while fighting me?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Khoonda is safe in no small part due to you. I feel certain that the Administrator wouldn't have made it without your assistance. I may have misjudged you.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «You - who are you? What are you doing on this sh-?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I know you - not even the markings of the dark side can hide it. Why have you done this?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «How long have you been here, among us?» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I will not let you hurt her.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «I want you to teach me the ways of the Force. To become a Jedi Knight, what I meant to be.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Dock_Module]**
- Duros: «But I see no harm in helping you. There will be a small expense to find this for you.» [K2/Dock_Module]

**[K2/Docks]**
- Lootra: «Maybe you're right - thanks again, stranger.» [K2/Docks]

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- Atton: «Uh... all right. I'm guessing that Republic ship that just docked isn't carrying friends of yours.» [K2/Dormitories]
- Atton: «Yeah, and I'm also good at running and drinking, your majesty. And even if you two aren't big friends of the Republic, that warship's the only way off this station.» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Engine_Deck]**
- Darth Sion: «I sense you, my Master. Faint... weak.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Darth Sion: «After all that has happened, still you live. You are difficult to kill.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Darth Sion: «The failure is yours. No longer do your whispers crawl within my skull. No longer do I suffer beneath teachings that weaken us. And now you run in search of the Jedi. They are all dead, save one - and one broken Jedi cannot stop the darkness that is to come.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- Atton: «Wh- what's wrong? Are you all right? Dammit, hold on! It's only a little further. Don't give up on me now!» [K2/Engine_Deck]

**[K2/Entertainment_Promenade]**
- Door Guard: «[Success] You can go in.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]
- Twi'lek Domo: «I don't mean any offense to you, but Vogga tends to favor younger dancers.» [K2/Entertainment_Promenade]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Perhaps exile has been good to you indeed - it has certainly not dulled your instincts, nor the speed at which you learn. I shall go to Dantooine, to the ruins of the Enclave. If you gather the others, I will meet you there. And... thank you, exile. Your returning... it is good that you are back among us.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Unh. If you think to learn anything from my death... mm...then you are wrong.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «As I followed Atris, perhaps what I wished to follow was the call of my bloodline, of my mother. I have felt incomplete, hollow, since her loss at Malachor V... and with your help, perhaps this wound might be healed.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I want you to teach me to wield the Force as my mother did, to fight those that would threaten us - and end them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Mira: «Call it what you want. Me, I love my targets.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I do not believe you. And I do not trust you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Why are you here?» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- Atton: «Well, now that we just killed a planet, maybe one of you can tell me what's going on. Because between assassin droids, a Sith Lord that looks like he sleeps with vibroblades, and being target practice for a Republic warship, I was better off in my cell.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Atton: «That's the first smart thing I've heard since you two started talking.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Atton: «Hey, don't stop your long, boring rants on my account. I was just getting sleepy-eyed.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Atton: «Look, uh, not like I care or anything, but you might want to go check on our passenger - especially with that hand of hers.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Visas: «Your acts are subtle indeed - much can be born from such manipulations.» [K2/Influence]
- Visas: «I fear that will be your undoing.» [K2/Influence]
- Hanharr: «To show such to another is to make yourself prey.» [K2/Influence]
- Visas: «It is strange... as you acted, suddenly I acted. It is like we were fighting in tandem, as a Force Bond, though I do not feel the same connection. Your impulse to kill, to attack that one - I felt it mirrored in me. It was a strange sensation. It was as if I had no choice in the matter.» [K2/Influence]
- Hanharr: «Establish you are the stronger in all things, and others shall fear you.» [K2/Influence]
- Visas: «As you command, I follow.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Colonel Tobin: «Wh-What have you done to my men? Blast! Men, take care of him. I won't let Kavar escape!» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Iziz_Spaceport]**
- Checkpoint Guard: «[Success] I don't think your business is any concern of mine.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]
- HK-47: «Observation: Master, your powers of persuasion without the use of pain or violence is a mystery to me.» [K2/Iziz_Spaceport]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Your friend has been captured by Goto.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «Who are you... how did you bring me here, from the tunnel? I was attacked... by another Jeedai! And you, what did y-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «What... what have you done?» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «Why... why do you do this?! Why... did you not let me die?!» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «You... want me to hunt the human Jeedai?» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «You want me to hunt the human Jeedai, the exile - and kill h-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «Kill me... I cannot bear the weight of another life-debt... kill me, or I swear I shall kill you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Mandalorian Sentry: «We have orders to escort you to our camp - our leader wants to speak to you.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Dillan: «But since you're a salvager, I expect you already know all that.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Dillan: «Really? You seem pretty well-armed for a farmer. But a lot of us joined the military during the Jedi Civil War. If this is your first time back, a lot has changed. You may want to go into Khoonda and ask around about your family. There was a lot of... movement when the Sith invaded. Hopefully everybody you knew is safe.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Battered Protocol Droid: «May I be of further service to you, Jedi?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Battered Protocol Droid: «Greetings, Jedi. May I be of any further service to you?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Tarn: «[Success] You're right... Well, between the droids, the security doors and the gun turrets, I have my hands full!» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Atton: «You're as smooth as the barrel of a blaster. I like that.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Korriban]**
- Visas: «It seems quiet - just the wind... but deep beneath the surface, you can feel the pain of what took place here. There is great power in this place - for those who can hear its call.» [K2/Korriban]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Atton: «Could you please lighten up for one second?» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Atton: «The truce between the bounty hunters in Nar Shaddaa is off. There's going to be war. A trap in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is bad enough, but having a hundred bounty hunters on your back is something else.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- Master Vrook: «Do you know why we have called you here?» [K2/Lost_Jedi]

**[K2/Malachor_V]**
- Darth Sion: «You were a fool to return. I spared you once, I will not do so again. Why have you returned?» [K2/Malachor_V]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- Mandalore: «What do you want?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «Hnh. It is, like I promised. Why, you want to back out now?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «I was at Malachor V. And I remember how many Jedi died to stop us there. And no matter how many dead orbit that planet, the Mandalorians still live. Clan Ordo still lives. See Kex there - he was serving on Nar Shaddaa as muscle for the Hutts. Kelborn was a scout for the Duros on frontier worlds. I brought them here, gave them a purpose. This galaxy will be ours again, I promise you. That is the future.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «You've got some guts, talking to me like that. You think your age or your Jedi whelp are going to keep you safe from me?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «How do you know that?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «Forget the Jedi. Keep your eyes on her.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Kelborn: «You didn't think you were the only ones with stealth generators, did you?» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Master_of_the_Palace]**
- Kelborn: «You received my message, then? Colonel Tobin contacted us. He arranged safe passage to Onderon for you. But I don't know if his offer still stands.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Captain Bostuco: «[Failure] I... I will let you... no. No, you're not permitted past this point.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Visas: «His loyalty runs deep. But perhaps there is another route to the control you seek.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Atton: «We're going to touch down in the Refugee Sector. There's a lot more traffic there, and it's harder for people to spot you coming in... or find you once you arrive.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «Uh... yeah. If you want to stay on the ship and meditate some more, don't let us stop you.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «Well, that means finding either a bounty hunter, a ranking member of the Exchange, or someone willing to talk - none of which are too appealing. Bounty hunters and the Exchange are going to want to shoot you, and someone who is willing to talk is willing to talk to anyone, which means trouble.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Handmaiden: «I do not wish your support, Dark Jedi - your words only undermine my arguments.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Atton: «It's up to you. There's bound to be someone in the Sector willing to spill their guts for a credit or two.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Disciple: «If you come within striking distance of the Jedi, then the search may be over before it's begun.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]
- Handmaiden: «We do not need your assistance, Dark Jedi.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Onderon]**
- Atton: «Until the ship is repaired, we're not going anywhere. Unless you can find another route to Onderon, we should sit tight.» [K2/Onderon]
- Atton: «Well, if you go, be careful - no telling what other ships were forced down in the battle.» [K2/Onderon]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Handmaiden: «I will not warn you again. Drop your weapons, or we shall take them from you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atton: «You've got to be joking. What is a Jedi Academy doing out here in the middle of nowhere?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atton: «What do you mean? We're in the middle of a bunch of Jedi. You know how they are.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atton: «What... what are you doing? Get out of my head!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atton: «You can't tell her. Please - I'm asking you. I don't want her to...» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atton: «I don't know how you became such a manipulative witch, but why a vicious old scow like yourself would even bother with me is a bigger mystery.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atton: «Fine. I'll be your pawn. But I still think you've got the wrong man.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Master Vrook: «You were deafened.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Kavar: «You were broken.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «You were blinded.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «When you returned to us, we saw what had happened. You carry all those deaths at Malachor within you, and it has left a hole, a hunger that cannot be filled.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «Do not be afraid - you shall feel no pain, but this must be done. As long as you feel the Force, you are a danger to those around you.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Kavar: «I thought you had died in the Mandalorian Wars...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «Is this your new Master, exile? If so, then you follow Revan's path. Her teachings will cause you to fall as surely as she did.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Refugee: «Forgive me, stranger... please, I beg you, do not kill me!» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Thank you, stranger - I won't forget your kindness.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- Hussef: «You might be right. But I can't imagine why else they'd be coming down on us.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- Moza: «I am unclear as to what Chodo means by this. He says the echo he felt upon your arrival suggests that you yourself are damaged. He can feel the pain through the Force.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Moza: «Forgive me. I am unclear as to Chodo's message, and I may have related it incorrectly. If this offer of mutual aid interests you, please go to the Ithorian compound here in Residential 082 when you are able. Chodo Habat would be most pleased to see you.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Moza: «I do not wish to press you. But please, speak with Chodo Habat before you make your final decision. Farewell.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Well? What do you think?» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Atton: «Makes sense. I'll follow your lead.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- Chodo Habat: «It is a most unusual wound you carry. If you would permit me to examine you, perhaps I could help restore some of what was lost.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «I felt an echo within the Force upon your arrival. It was not a natural thing. It spoke of a great pain, not of scars, but of the spirit. It is as if you once felt the Force flow through you, then it was torn away, brutally and quickly. The echo comes from the hollows where it once flowed through you. It is a most unusual wound you carry. If you would permit me to examine you, perhaps I could help restore some of what was lost.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «When you stood before me, you opened my eyes to a hurt almost as great as the planet's. Your wound... I can feel the immensity your loss. Yet I can feel that you are slowly regaining what you have lost, and that in time, you may fully heal. Perhaps your time here has helped. I believe that it has. I think that I can help in your recovery, at least partially. I must admit, however, that even as the healer of my herd, I have never faced an injury such as this.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «With all due respect, I believe you are wrong.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «In healing a planet, it is a matter of connections. As plants feed animals, and animal populations thrive and grow, life connects and expands, the living web of the Force. I have walked in the growing restoration zones and felt the fullness of life, and perhaps that connection will help make you whole again. Perhaps you will find more than you think on the surface. You, me, my herd, helped to build that, the life of the planet. Though we describe the restoration as a process, we are, in truth, opening Telos to the Force. Thus, I believe I can help you. That perhaps through your work with the planet and my guidance, I might restore some part of the Force to you.» [K2/Residential_082_West]

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- Atton: «Amazing at wasting the Republic's credits, maybe. But now that you mention it, I think I feel it too. Like a weight's been lifted off my shoulders.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- General Vaklu: «You've won... this battle, Talia... But your reign won't be an easy one. The Republic is a sinking ship. And you're too attached to it.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Queen Talia: «What is done, is done. I don't think the service you have given us can ever be repaid. I must go with Captain Kadron, the fighting must be stopped. I will be back shortly to try and repay you. The crisis is over thanks to you... and Master Kavar. I believe he'd like to speak to you. If you'll excuse me.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Captain Riiken: «The Queen has requested you stay here until she gets back. I imagine you'll get some sort of reward. Talk with Master Kavar. She shouldn't take too long.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Your... power... is undeniable. But it won't be enough.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Xarga: «Queen Talia has hidden depths as a leader. There is more to warfare than mastery of battle tactics. Equally important is the ability to motivate the troops that carry your banner. Vaklu was formidable, but through all of the Queen's hardships she kept her army together. Tactics can be learned, but some elements of leadership are innate. This bodes well for her future.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «If you have a reason for approaching me, speak it.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris has said that if Revan and Malak had been properly instructed in the ways of the Jedi, they never would have fallen... and nor would you, exile.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «Regardless, I would have liked to have been there for that final exchange and seen the truth of their conflict with each other... as perhaps you would have.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I see. Thank you, exile. I appreciate you sharing your knowledge with me.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Huh? What are you talking about?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Well, if it isn't the one who stole the Ebon Hawk. Not so smug now, are you, you little thief?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atton: «Says you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Bao-Dur: «You're threatening Atton with a lightsaber, and I'm supposed to just stay out of it? No!» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Atris: «You are that which has attacked the Jedi... you are Sith.» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]**
- Atton: «Yeah, you go ahead and meditate. As for me, I could use some sleep.» [K2/Telos__Citadel_Station]

**[K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- G0-T0: «I just realized there was a business transaction I needed to make, back at the Ebon Hawk. Surely, you can do without me here.» [K2/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

## Darth Nihilus
*Corpus coverage: 0 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 39 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 36 pronoun references resolved from context · 0 lines addressed to them · 9 narration lines · 3 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Nihilus is a Sith Lord born of Malachor's wound: «He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing.» [K2/Kreia]. He destroyed Katarr and the Jedi conclave there. Kavar: «all the Jedi on the planet were killed, along with the Miralukas and all other life on the planet» [K2/Royal_Palace]. His ship is the Ravager, «tore... from the mass shadows of Malachor» (Tobin) [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck].
### Personality and motivations
Hunger; he barely speaks, and Visas speaks for him: «You... you are the darkness in which all life dies, milord.» [K2/Visas]. Kreia: his hunger is «not something he can direct or focus» [K2/Kreia].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Visas, his emissary, scouts the Exile.
- **Telos:** the Ravager arrives to devour Telos («he will cleanse it of life», Visas) [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck].
- **Bridge:** incapacitates the party. His drain backfires on the Exile. Visas disrupts the link or sacrifices herself. He dies.
### Relationships
- **Traya:** master, betrayed.
- **Sion:** fellow Lord.
- **Visas:** servant.
### Abilities (lore)
- Planet-scale Force consumption.
- Wound-in-the-Force sense.
- Fleet from Malachor.
### Feats
- Katarr [K2/Royal_Palace].
- With Sion, cast down Traya [K2/Kreia].
- Incapacitates the party [K2/Bridge].
### Power-scaling statements (key)
- «perhaps he will grow strong enough to eradicate all life, merely with his presence» (Visas) [K2/Visas]
- «he will destroy everything, just as he destroyed Katarr» (Atris) [K2/Telos]
- Mandalore: «If he's so powerful, why hasn't he stopped us, then?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
### Duels and fights
| Fight | Page | Outcome and context |
|---|---|---|
| vs Exile and party | K2/Bridge | Loses and dies. |
### Verdict implications
- Tier 5, with the hard counter noted.
- The neutral lean vs the Exile moves to low-medium, the Exile, because the counter is innate to the Exile.
### Open questions
- He has no transcribed lines.
