**[K2/HK-47]**
- HK-47: «Hypothetical: Master, it is entirely possible that one day you may no longer be my Master. It is also possible that my new Master might become jealous of our prior relationship. It has happened before. As a result, I have a responsibility to know your weaknesses should I be required to assassinate you.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «You see, I do not believe my Master knew who he was most of the time, which led to some confusing situations that could have otherwise been avoided.» [K2/HK-47] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- HK-47: «Clarification: Suffice to say that my Master had his ups and downs, and in my opinion, traveled from a high point in his life to a low point, and then back again to his true potential.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «He developed qualities such as mercy, compassion, and even love for others. It was really rather revolting. Still, my Master had some remarkably efficient droid-like qualities which I admired. He, too, suffered memory problems as I did.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «He could be a merciless, efficient killer when the need arose. It really made my behavior core glow. And even as a meatbag, my Master still had some remarkably efficient droid-like qualities which I admired. He, too, suffered memory problems as I did.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Clarification: Suffice to say that my Master had her ups and downs, and in my opinion, traveled from a high point in her life to a low point. - HK-47: «Clarification: Suffice to say that my Master had her ups and downs, and in my opinion, traveled from a high point in her life to a low point, and then back again to her true potential. And even as a meatbag, my Master still had some remarkably efficient droid-like qualities which I admired. She, too, suffered memory problems as I did.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Answer: Even with my memory impairment, I recall that my Master was about to embark on a dangerous journey.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Conjecture: I believe there was something about my Master's destination that he did not wish me to know.» [K2/HK-47] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- HK-47: «Answer: I am somewhat disappointed that my Master did not choose to take me along, of course. Wherever my Master traveled, destruction and death were assured.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Recollection: I believe my Master speculated that many Jedi did not fully form such connections because of their discipline, because they never opened their lives to the passions around them. I believe Revan termed it that "one would need to be a human being to develop such connections." It is something that the Jedi code could not teach. One simply knew it instinctively, or not.» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Visas: «You are still unready to face my Master. Any flaw, he will sense. As he is a seam in the fabric of the Force, he senses fractures and weaknesses around him. By extending such kindnesses to others, you leave yourself vulnerable.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Kreia: «Power? Do you think so? You would be wrong. There is no strength in the hunger he possesses... and the will behind his power is a primal thing. And it devours him as he devours others - his mere presence kills all around him, slowly, feeding him. He is already dead, it is simply a question of how many he kills before he falls.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «It is not something he can direct or focus, much like hunger itself. He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing. Force Sensitives and worlds rich in the Force draw him. The Miraluka world was one such place. That is why where the Jedi gather, Jedi will die. He will feel it, unless they mask their presence - but Katarr called out as a beacon to him, and he could not resist it. And he cares nothing for the Sith or its teachings... or the Jedi. And when the Jedi are dead, he will feed on the galaxy, the Republic, and eventually, consume the Sith as well.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «It is a more primal connection you feel. And the hunger you feel does not stem from Hanharr - it is something you may draw upon, his very life, if need be. He does not realize how deeply his life debt runs - but he will. When you suffer, call upon that hunger, and the beast shall be that upon which your will may draw strength. Reach out... feel his presence within the ship, clawing at you, pacing. Feel the rumbling of the hull, the metal around you like a cage... and the building anger, the blood that rises behind the eyes, a bloodlust that cannot be sated. If you accept it, you will find your strength increase, and your vitality return to you faster with every breath.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- HK-47: «Exclamation: My behavioral core glows when my Master acts like this.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]**
- Colonel Tobin: «He happened - the hunger that fills this vessel... it is power... but it consumes without end. It is that power we felt on Onderon, that Vaklu felt... it was an echo, too strong to ignore.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «The Master suffers... if he cannot feed, then the hunger begins to consume him. The planet... Telos... He may feed on something upon its surface to sustain him awhile longer.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Colonel Tobin: «Yes... the old woman on Onderon. She spoke of the Jedi Academy, here, on Telos... and my Master was forced to come here.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]
- Visas: «If there are no Jedi here, then my Lord cannot feed his hunger. He will destroy the planet, the station... he will cleanse it of life. Even if the people below are not Force Sensitive, the small amount he can feed on from the mass destruction of the station... and the life on the planet... will sustain him a while longer. And if there are no Jedi below, he will have no other choice.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Visas: «I want to look upon you, where no one else can see, where it is just us. I want to see your face, the color of your skin. I wish to see what the Handmaiden sees when she looks at you that causes her heart to race, and her tone to change. What causes her to forsake her heritage, her oaths... as you made me forsake mine. I... need to know if perhaps I am wrong, if the universe is not as my Master once showed me.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**

**[K2/Sith_Tomb]**
- Visas: «The source of the power of this place is behind that door... it is strong, indeed. It must be what has drawn the Sith here... and drawn the attention of my Master.» [K2/Sith_Tomb]

**[K2/T3-M4]**
- Bastila: «T3, I have one last order for you. Our master does not know about this, and I order you to keep it a secret. Every day as we build upon Korriban, more of his memories return - and the bond he and I share cannot hide it from me. He believes a threat lurks on the edge of the galaxy. Something that could threaten his power. I... fear he will go there... and I fear he will not return. If he leaves, he will take you with him - but not I. He knows the bond we share may prove a weakness... but he will take you. If something happens, T3, then I need you to return to the Republic and find me. And if you cannot, then I need you to seek whatever help you can elsewhere, whether Jedi or the Sith... or someone in-between. This is my last order to you, T3. Obey it - and do not let me lose my Master... even if he believes he is protecting me.» [K2/T3-M4]

**[K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]**

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «Nar Shaddaa shall persist as it always has, but its evil and corruption shall grow. **[K2/Visas]**
- Visas: «You... you are the darkness in which all life dies, milord. All life... exists to feed your power, and my life... my life is yours.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I serve my Master. I am an emissary, a scout. My Master was aware of a disturbance in the Force, but was unaware of its nature, of you. The disturbance is not something one feels from a living thing. There is little my Master does not know, and that you eluded his sight for so long... is significant, but I do not know why.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «You cannot. His vessel roams the borders of known space, and even I do not know where he travels.... until he... calls for me. Even if I could lead you to my Master, I cannot permit you to find him... until you are ready.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «If I bring you before my Master, untested, without your potential realized, then you will be lost to me. And I cannot allow that to happen. It would be as if one brought fire to a paradise valley, shattered a cavern of rare crystal... or blinded a painter.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «There is a... a greatness in you, a greatness that does not stem from the Force. It stems from who you are. And if my Master does not understand you, cannot see you, then perhaps there is hope for us all. But if you seek to survive, then you must understand why this is so.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «There is much I see my Master cannot. I fear it is because of my nature, the nature of my race. My people spend their lives seeing the galaxy, the energy streaming off stars, the growth of life - all things touched by the Force.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I remember little of my homeworld before I entered my Master's service. It is not as it was, there is little left of such memories - or the planet itself.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I am not familiar with the place you speak of - my Master has not entered Republic space for some time. But there are others, who may move more freely, who may have been responsible for such an act of destruction.» [K2/Visas]
- Visas: «I still wonder what would have happened if I had died with the others... if perhaps there would have been some way to hide my presence from the galaxy. If only I had not... felt that pain, that loss, as strongly as I did. But it could not be done. When the life was bled from the planet, and yet somehow, I remained, my Master came for me. He walked upon the surface of my dead world, and there, lying in the bodies of my race, he took me for his own. And he made me *see.* And for the first time, I saw the galaxy. And I wished to die.» [K2/Visas]

- «If Visas disrupts the link then Nihilus is incapacitated for one combat round. Otherwise, if Visas is wearing clothing (not robes or armor, or even the Miner Uniform) and using a melee weapon or» [K2/Bridge, narration]
- «If Visas didn't sacrifice herself, then she walks over to the body of Nihilus:» [K2/Bridge, narration]

- «Nihilus releases her, and she collapses:» [K2/Visas, narration]

## Darth Sion
*Corpus coverage: 82 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 2 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 3 pronoun references resolved from context · 3 lines addressed to them · 9 narration lines · 8 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Sion is the Lord of Pain. Kreia: «Like the others, he was spawned by the horrors of the Mandalorian Wars.» [K2/Kreia]. He trained under Traya, then betrayed her. Held together by pain: «The power of the dark side is what holds me together.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)].
### Personality and motivations
Obsessed with killing the Jedi; secretly loyal to Traya. «I have waited years to see the last of the Jedi fall before me.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Harbinger:** severs Kreia's hand.
- **Peragus pursuit.**
- **Korriban:** confronts the Exile; restores himself; the Exile flees.
- **Malachor:** guards the academy and is defeated four times. Persuasion breaks his will («flesh and belief, both cast down», first pass) and he dies.
### Relationships
- **Traya:** loyalty and hatred.
- **Exile:** hunted, then admired.
- **Nihilus:** fellow Lord.
### Abilities (lore)
- Pain-fuelled immortality: «As long as the dark places of this world flow through the cracks of my flesh, I cannot be killed.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)].
- Lightsaber mastery.
### Feats
- Severing Kreia's hand.
- Killing Lonna Vash (her body lies at the academy) [K2/Sith_Academy].
- Surviving countless deaths: «Many times have I been near death» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)].
### Power-scaling statements
- «I have come farther than any of her apprentices since Revan» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
### Duels and fights
| Fight | Page | Outcome and context |
|---|---|---|
| vs Kreia | Harbinger | Wins. |
| vs Exile | K2/Sith_Academy | Draw or retreat. |
| vs Exile | K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top) | Loses four times and dies. |
### Verdict implications
- Tier 7.
- Below the Exile (high) and Traya (medium-high).
### Open questions
- None.

### Quote bank A: Darth Sion's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Engine_Deck]**
- «I sense you, my Master. Faint... weak.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «After all that has happened, still you live. You are difficult to kill.» [K2/Engine_Deck]
- «The failure is yours. No longer do your whispers crawl within my skull. No longer do I suffer beneath teachings that weaken us. And now you run in search of the Jedi. They are all dead, save one - and one broken Jedi cannot stop the darkness that is to come.» [K2/Engine_Deck]

**[K2/Malachor_V]**
- «You were a fool to return. I spared you once, I will not do so again. Why have you returned?» [K2/Malachor_V]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
- «She is here. Find her.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «Her apprentice is here. Find him.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «Did you come here for answers? There are none. The call of Korriban is strong, but it is the call of the dead.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «I have studied you, immersed myself in you. I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher. I know the fires that raged upon the Dxun moon while the Republic died around you. You know war. You know battle. And I know of Malachor. You know what it means to be broken. The one who travels with you will destroy you, as she did me. I can end it before it begins.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «It is fitting you came here.I have studied you and found nothing but weakness. Yet still she clutches at you, as if you are all that gives her life.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «I know her as an apprentice knows their Master... and as a Master knows an apprentice.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «I want her to die, and see all that she has built cast down. All that she holds dear, in shards at her feet.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «She clings to hope. That perhaps she can train one as great as her first. She is a fool who escaped death once. She will not do so again.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «But you do not know her as I do. You have not survived her teachings, as I have. And you have not bested her in battle, as I have. You are nothing. Yet still she walks with you, is willing to sacrifice herself for you!» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «I have studied you. I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher. I know the fires that raged upon the Dxun moon while the Republic died around you. You know war. You know battle. And I know of Malachor. You saw the heart of war, what Malachor wrought, yet you turned away from it.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «You are a wretched thing, a thing of weakness and fear. You are her apprentice in name only. I am the Master. And that is why you will die.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «She protects him. Shields him. Find him. Hunt him wherever he travels. He will not escape me again. I will bring his corpse to her, cast it at her feet. It will be as if killing her children. I will kill all she protects, all she shields, until her hands are drenched in blood.» [K2/Sith_Academy]
- «Do not harm her. I command it. She... has earned this. She and I will meet again.» [K2/Sith_Academy]

- «It is not mercy. What awaits you will weaken you. She will break you, as she did me, and you will no longer know yourself.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I fear no one, for while the Force is with me I yet live.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You and her are alike... yet different, in all the ways that matter. And I hate you as I hate her. And in that weakness lies death. Perhaps in that weakness is the death of my master, as well.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «What it has always been. Her weakness is you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «The power of the dark side is what holds me together. Many times have I been near death - and always have I been in pain.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «One cannot abandon the Force, any more than one can abandon one's will.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «[[../Trayus Academy#End» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

- «You are strong... I cannot see as she does, but I know, that in time, you shall surpass her power. But not here, not in this place.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «She awaits on the Trayus Core in the heart of this planet... but you will never reach her.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I cannot. If you pass, you shall not return as you are now. Return to Malachor - or go through me. There is no middle ground.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I am ready for you, exile. I have waited years to see the last of the Jedi fall before me, to join the rest that lie buried within this planet's core. The end of the Jedi is at hand.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «So you have come to die, like the Jedi before you. This planet is a graveyard for your kind. She thinks you should be spared, but only so you might suffer. You will break. And when you do, you will die. Why she would bother with one such as you is something I will never understand.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «It is ancient, a relic that survived the destruction of Malachor. It was always here, far before the Mandalorian Wars. It draws death and hate to it, channels it. Atrocities feed its power, and with its power, it creates hunger. Many Jedi have been consumed by it.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «It has been here for thousands of years. It is a place where the Sith teachings run strong... it is the threshold of the borders of an ancient empire. Kreia says it was a place of reflection for the ancient Sith... a gateway to their lands. It drew Lord Revan... and it calls to her as well. She said that the teachings here will lead one to the Sith... the true Sith... and all their shadowed worlds. This place led Revan to the graveyards of Korriban... and beyond.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «It does not matter. The Lord Revan had the galaxy at his feet, could have made it strong, and he surrendered it to weakness. Like you, Revan touched power and turned away. The chance to heal this fractured galaxy, and he turned away. I was there when the Academy on Korriban fell... and the Sith turned on one another, like beasts. Our chance at an empire... destroyed by Revan.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «She awaits on the Trayus Core - the heart of this planet. But you will never reach her.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You know nothing of her teachings, nothing of pain. You saw Malachor V and turned away. You turned away from power. She is a fool to see greatness in that.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «Then it shall be proof that you are not the one she seeks... and she shall accept my loyalty again.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «Now you realize the true power of the dark side. As long as the dark places of this world flow through the cracks of my flesh, I cannot be killed.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You are strong... as strong as I had believed. But she knows you cannot defeat me.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You are strong... perhaps she was correct about you. But you cannot kill me. She knows this.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «There is no life without the Force... the Force is a blade, without it, one is defenseless.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «She sought you out because both of you were wounded by the Force - and because she had no choice.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «The Force is pain. The Force is focus. The Force is that which drives the strong and kills the weak. I shall teach you this - and in so doing, spare you the pain that awaits.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «Those are the words of Jedi, Jedi who over thousands of years, have never turned from power, from inflicting their will on the galaxy. They know power. The only weakness is not seizing it when it lies before you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «She would not willingly return to being the helpless woman she once was... nor would her apprentice.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «She would not willingly return to being the helpless woman she once was... nor would she wish that of her apprentice.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «If you will not leave this place, then I cannot allow you to pass. If you go before her, you will be broken. If killing you will spare you what lies ahead, then kill you I must...» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You are the one who will fall. Die, Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «[Failure] If I am the weapon to be used against you, then so be it. But you will die, Jedi.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «[Failure] Betrayal is at the core of what she is. She knows this, and she respects it. No more words, exile. Now you die.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «[Success] Should you die... then she will have no choice but to accept me at her side. My training shall be complete, as was intended.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You intend to make me doubt her... doubt myself. This is not a battle of words, exile. It is one of blood.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «If I die here, then you will have sealed your fate.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «You will not pass.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «[Success] There is truth in your words... but there is nothing left for me except my master.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I fight because it is the power that the Force fills me with. To survive, to inflict the pain on others.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Failure] No. It is you that she has marked for death - for suffering.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «[Failure] No. Even if she believes you the stronger, then I shall prove her wrong.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I... I still have the power to stop you. The Force runs strong through the screaming canyons of Malachor.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I can die a hundred times, exile, and still I will rise again, as strong as before.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I will not fall. I cannot die.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «Why... why did she choose you? What makes you able to defeat me, defeat me here?» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

- «Her weakness... is you. As you were mine.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «Her weakness... is you. She has done all this... all of it... for you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «I am glad to leave this place... at last.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
